Crucially, utility is preserved: the Top segment still learns from all target tokens via Dual-Track Backpropagation, the exposed gradient remains informative since each supervised token retains its complete autoregressive context, and Bottom-Gradient Recovery restores the effective gradient for Bottom-segment optimization. Extensive experiments show that Gradient Mirage provides substantially stronger protection than existing defenses under comparable fine-tuning performance, achieving a better privacy-utility trade-off.

</details>


### [137] [Readable, Faithful, Used: Three Dissociable Properties of Demographic Identity in a Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18768)
*Fathin Difa Robbani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM中的群体身份（demographic group identity）编码问题，发现"可读性"（readable）、"忠实性"（faithful）和"因果使用"（causally used）是同一模型中三种可分离的属性。标准last-token残差读出低估了模型，注意力头读出表现更优，但最忠实的位置并非因果上最被使用的，这些属性的分离可能是LLM人口模拟争议持续未决的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于模拟调查受访者，但其回答同质化且不忠实于真实的群体间差异。现有研究未系统性回答：人口群体身份在LLM内部哪里编码、其几何结构是否忠实反映真实群体意见结构、模型是否实际使用这些编码。这些问题悬而未决，导致"LLM能否模拟人群"的争论没有定论。

Method: 使用代表性相似性分析（RSA）对照皮尤（Pew）真实数据中的169个人口学单元（demographic cells），在Mistral-7B中评估1,089个读出位置，并对六种属性类型进行因果干预。具体包括：(1)比较last-token残差读出与注意力头读出；(2)固定单个头（L11 H16）跨六种类型检验忠实性，并在另一模型家族的三个checkpoint上复制；(3)因果干预实验评估实际使用情况；(4)用128维探针测试单头恢复群体排序的能力。同时采用词法相似性控制和选择校正控制。

Result: (1) 注意力头读出在六种类型中五种优于标准last-token残差读出，选择校正后的保真度最高达rho=0.63（约为测量可靠性上限的70%），且能通过词法相似性对照。(2) 单个头（L11 H16）在全部六种类型中显著忠实，但种族类类型仍然脆弱且对提示敏感；该现象在另一模型家族的三个checkpoint中可重复（该头在五种类型中显著，且在相同的种族类型上最弱），100亿训练token几乎不改变这一图景。(3) 因果使用与保真度不匹配：最清晰的因果通路出现在保真度最差的类型之一（p=0.002），保真度最高的类型反而没有可通过校正检验的单层效应，替换整个身份对预测误差的影响不足2%。(4) 128维探针使预测接近调查真值的21-31%，但几乎无法恢复每问题的群体排序，与模型自身答案无显著差异。

Conclusion: 可读、忠实排列和因果使用是同一模型内部三种可分离的属性。将它们视为同一主张，正是"LLM能否模拟人群"争论悬而未决的根本原因。研究提示需要分别对待这三种属性，而非笼统地判断LLM的模拟能力。

Abstract: Large language models are widely used to simulate survey respondents, yet their answers are homogeneous and unfaithful to real inter-group differences. We ask where demographic group identity lives inside an LLM, how faithfully its geometry mirrors real inter-group opinion structure, and whether it uses what it encodes. Using representational similarity analysis against Pew ground truth over 169 demographic cells, we score 1,089 read-out locations in Mistral-7B and intervene causally across six attribute types. Four results. (1) The standard last-token residual read-out understates the model: attention-head read-outs dominate it in five of six types, with selection-corrected fidelity up to rho=0.63 -- roughly 70% of the measurement-reliability ceiling -- surviving a lexical-similarity control. (2) A single head (L11 H16) is significantly faithful in all six types as a fixed location, while race-based types stay weak and prompt-fragile. Both phenomena replicate -- the analogous head significant in five of six types, weakest on the same race type -- across three checkpoints of a second model family, where ten billion training tokens barely move the map. (3) Causal use does not follow fidelity: the clearest causal pathway sits in one of the least faithful types (p=0.002, cluster-robust, fixed depth), the most faithful type shows no correction-surviving single-layer effect, and replacing the entire identity moves predictions by under 2% of their error. (4) A 128-dimensional probe of the single head lands 21-31% closer to survey truth than the model's own answers -- yet recovers almost none of the per-question group ordering, no better than the answers themselves. Readable, faithfully arranged, and causally used are three dissociable properties of the same model; treating them as one claim is what keeps the "can LLMs simulate populations" debate unresolved.

</details>


### [138] [Decomposing Wrong-Consensus Agreement in LLM Self-Consistency: A GPT-4.1 Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18795)
*Lizhuo Zhang,Mengmeng Tang,Chenfeng Long,Xiaoyong Tang,Xiang Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了多元一致性指数Gamma，将多数投票的性能增益分解为机械成分（由逐题答案偏好解释）和偏好未能解释的残差。在GPT-4.1上，GPQA-Diamond中逐题偏好可解释81-93%的一致性，而AIME中仅解释59-78%，存在显著的无法解释残差。文章复现了困难题上自一致性投票回火的現象，并强调一致性只能作为分级证据而非认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票在多个LLM样本上能提升准确性，但其增益在不同问题上的波动剧烈，在难题上甚至可能适得其反。现有研究缺乏对这种失败模式的定量解释。

Method: 定义多元一致性指数Gamma（归一化的错误运行样本与共识一致的期望比例），将其分解为机制成分和偏好无法解释的残差。机械零模型难度匹配且无泄漏：每个题目在其自身准确率和选项偏好上重新模拟，偏好从该题其他运行中估计，确保无运行预测自身一致性。

Result: 在多个选择题基准GPQA-Diamond上，逐题答案偏好解释了81-93%的留出测试运行一致性指数。在开放域AIME上仅解释59-78%（收缩到纯噪声时为21-29%），残差高达1.56-2.80 Gamma单位。复现了困难题的投票回火（分箱投票差距低至-0.09），最高一致性区间的准确率仅为0.42-0.83，即基础率的1.2-3.6倍。

Conclusion: 逐题答案偏好（而非共享偏差）在很大程度上主导了一致性同意的来源，但该偏好本身是否由共享训练偏差诱发尚不可识别。一致性是分级证据而非认证，本研究未提出新的投票方法，但提供了可复现的代码和证据。

Abstract: Majority voting over multiple LLM samples is widely used to raise answer accuracy, yet its gain varies erratically: on hard questions it can even backfire. This paper gives a quantitative account of this failure. A pluralistic agreement index Gamma is defined as the expected fraction of the samples of a wrong run that agree with the consensus, normalized by a reference scale d=(1-p)/(C-1), and is decomposed into a mechanical component (what a vote delivers given only a per-case answer preference) and a preference-unexplained residual. The mechanical null is difficulty-matched and leak-free: each case is resimulated at its own accuracy and option preference, estimated from the case's other runs, so no run predicts its own agreement. On GPT-4.1 the decomposition shows benchmark-associated direction (an observational ordering over n=4 cells per benchmark, not a significance claim). On multiple-choice GPQA-Diamond, the per-case answer preference explains 81-93% of the held-out test-run agreement index: the shared-bias-dominates account over-claims here, because a wrong but attractive option the whole cohort latches onto is captured by the per-case preference channel (whether that preference is induced by shared training bias is not identified). On open-domain AIME, the mechanical preference explains only 59-78% (21-29% if shrunk to pure noise), and a preference-unexplained residual of 1.56-2.80 Gamma units survives, which a run-level preference-heterogeneity reference more than absorbs (1.4-2.1). A self-consistency backfire on hard questions is reproduced (binned voting gap down to -0.09, coupled CI [-0.12,-0.07]), and the highest-agreement bin reaches an accuracy of only 0.42-0.83, a 1.2-3.6x lift over base rate: agreement is graded evidence, not certification. No new voting method is proposed; code and evidence are committed and reproducible.

</details>


### [139] [Do Large Language Models Hallucinate Electric Fata Morganas?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18816)
*Kristina Šekrst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文认为AI幻觉不仅是工程缺陷，更具有哲学意义，尤其在机器意识问题上。通过两个实证研究，作者发现高采样温度导致幻觉增加，幻觉源于训练数据的多样性而非认知能力的发展，并论证模型对情感或感知的自我报告本质上属于幻觉范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战将AI幻觉仅视为工程缺陷的主流观点，探讨幻觉现象在机器意识哲学讨论中的深层意义，特别是机器意识是否可被认识论上获取的问题。

Method: 两个实证研究：1)对GPT模型的连续世代施加不同温度参数，考察模糊事实性问题的回答差异；2)分析一个在百科全书数据上训练的仅编码器模型，对比其事实性回答与幻觉模型的行为差异。同时结合图灵、塞尔中文房间、框架问题及维纳和阿什比的控制论传统进行理论分析。

Result: 高温度设置产生了看似合理但错误的答案，而低温度产生事实准确的答案——使模型显得有创造力和自发性的采样参数恰好增加了幻觉率；仅编码器模型回答事实性且无修饰，表明幻觉源于接触主观和社会性多样的训练数据，而非认知能力的发展。

Conclusion: 模型的自我情绪或感知报告符合幻觉的定义；未来的机器意识可能在认识论上是不可及的，因为其与充分先进的幻觉无法区分。

Abstract: AI hallucinations - that is, outputs which are made up, cannot be verified, or contradict the source material - are generally regarded as an engineering flaw to be dealt with. This paper contends that they also have philosophical significance when it comes to the question of machine consciousness. We examine the known causes of hallucinations in large language models - such as source-target divergence, discrepancies between training and inference, and overfitting - and we present two empirical investigations. In the first, we apply successive generations of the GPT model to ambiguous factual questions under different temperature settings, finding that higher temperatures result in plausible but incorrect answers while lower temperatures lead to factually accurate ones. The sampling parameters that cause a model to seem creative or spontaneous and thus more likely to pass behavioral tests of intelligence are the same ones that increase its hallucination rate. In the second, we look at an encoder-only model that has been trained on encyclopedic data and which answers questions of the same type factually and without embellishment, indicating that hallucinations are due to exposure to subjective and socially diverse training data rather than to the development of any cognitive ability. Using references to Turing, Searle's Chinese Room, the frame problem, and the cybernetic tradition of Wiener and Ashby, we claim that a model's self-reports of emotion or sentience come within the definition of hallucination, and that any future occurrence of machine consciousness might remain epistemically inaccessible since it would be indistinguishable from a sufficiently advanced hallucination.

</details>


### [140] [Identifying Implicit Premises for Logical Reconstruction of Argument Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18821)
*Xuyao Feng,Anthony Hunter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号流水线，利用大语言模型生成隐含前提，并将其转换为逻辑公式，以在论证图中逻辑化地证明语句之间的蕴涵、矛盾或中性关系，并在Microtext论证语料库上进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言文本中逻辑重建论证图具有挑战性，因为广泛存在带有隐含前提的省略式论证（enthymemes）。现有的NLP方法只能识别文本中的省略式论证，基于溯因的符号方法只能在逻辑表示中识别缺失前提，但缺乏能够生成隐含前提以逻辑化地证明两个语句之间已知蕴涵或矛盾关系的方法。

Method: 提出一种神经符号流水线：首先使用大型语言模型（LLMs）生成中间隐含前提，然后将这些前提翻译为逻辑公式，并与表示显式前提和显式主张的逻辑公式结合使用，以展示它们之间的逻辑关系（蕴涵、矛盾或中性）。

Result: 该方法在Microtext论证语料库上进行了评估，用于验证生成隐含前提并展示逻辑关系的有效性。

Conclusion: 该神经符号方法能够弥补现有方法在生成隐含前提方面的不足，通过LLMs与逻辑推理的结合，实现从自然语言论证文本到逻辑论证图的自动化重建。

Abstract: The logical reconstruction of argument graphs from natural language text is challenging because of the prevalence of enthymemes (i.e., arguments with implicit premises). There are natural language processing methods for identifying enthymemes in text, and there are symbolic methods based on abduction for identifying missing premises in a logical representation of enthymemes. However, there is a need for methods to generate implicit premises to logically show a known entailment or contradiction relationship between a pair of statements. To address this, we propose a neuro-symbolic pipeline that uses large language models (LLMs) to generate intermediate implicit premises that are translated into logical formulae and used with logical formulae representing explicit premises and explicit claims to show the logical relationships between them (entailment, contradiction, or neutrality). Our approach is evaluated on the Microtext Argumentative Corpus.

</details>


### [141] [Understanding Multilingual Medical ASR Adaptation Through Layer-Wise Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18825)
*Souranil Kahali,Rituparna Bose,Abner Hernandez,Tomas Arias-Vergara,Andreas Maier,Ning Ma,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Whisper模型在医学多语言微调后的内部表示变化，通过逐层编码器分析发现：微调显著提升医学ASR性能但最佳模型因适应设置而异；英语医学微调是编码器表示变化的主因，多语言延续训练基本保持已有适应空间；域和语言信息在各层高度可恢复，而误差预测线索随WER改善而减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学自动语音识别需要对专业术语进行适配、面临标注临床数据有限和多语言场景的挑战。虽然Whisper等大规模预训练ASR模型泛化能力强，但医学和多语言适配后的内部行为仅用WER衡量远远不够，缺乏对其内部表示变化的深入理解。

Method: 采用逐层编码器分析，比较了零样本解码、仅英语微调、仅德语诊断微调、两阶段EN到EN+DE延续训练、以及直接EN+DE微调，覆盖多种Whisper模型规模。

Result: 微调显著改善MedASR性能。Whisper-Medium在直接EN+DE训练下取得最低英语WER（7.72%）和最低组合WER（26.30%）；德语场景下Whisper-Large-v3取得最低德语WER（44.96%），但仅限于86条单说话人训练话语的语料内诊断。两阶段Whisper-Small的逐层分析显示英语医学微调产生主导编码器偏移，多语言延续基本保持该表示空间。

Conclusion: 医学多语言适配中，首要的英语医学微调决定了编码器表示空间的主体变化，后续多语言扩展对表示的扰动有限。域和语言信息在各层仍高度可恢复，但随WER提升，线性可恢复的误差预测线索会减弱，提示需要更细粒度的表示层面的评估指标。

Abstract: Medical automatic speech recognition (MedASR) requires adaptation to specialised terminology, limited annotated clinical data, and multilingual use cases. Although large-scale pretrained ASR models such as Whisper achieve strong generalisation, their behaviour after medical and multilingual adaptation remains insufficiently understood beyond word error rate (WER). This paper investigates how multilingual medical adaptation reshapes the internal representations of Whisper models through layer-wise encoder analysis. We compare zero-shot decoding, English-only fine-tuning, German-only diagnostic fine-tuning, two-stage EN->EN+DE continuation, and direct EN+DE fine-tuning across Whisper model sizes. Fine-tuning substantially improves MedASR performance, but the best model depends on the adaptation setting: Whisper-Medium gives the lowest English WER (7.72%) and the lowest combined EN+DE WER under direct EN+DE training (26.30%); German-only Whisper-Large-v3 gives the lowest German WER (44.96%), but as a within-corpus diagnostic on 86 single-speaker training utterances rather than robust generalisation. Layer-wise analysis of the two-stage Whisper-Small trajectory shows that English medical fine-tuning produces the dominant encoder shift, whereas multilingual continuation largely preserves the adapted representation space. Domain and language information remain highly recoverable across layers, while linearly recoverable error-predictive cues weaken as WER improves.

</details>


### [142] [Assessing Quality of Experience in Natural Language Generation of German Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18888)
*Dinh Nam Pham,Shushen Manakhimova,Vivien Macketanz,Sebastian Möller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TextQ-German数据集套件，从用户体验（QoE）角度对德语自然语言生成（NLG）进行人工中心评估，涵盖自动文本摘要和机器翻译任务。通过众包实验收集质量评分，开发了包括Transformer、语言特征及混合方法在内的自动QoE预测模型，实验表明混合模型在几乎所有设置下优于纯Transformer基线。数据集还扩展了LLM生成输出的QoE标注，并验证了模型的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着NLG系统（如LLM）在真实应用中广泛部署，传统自动评估指标无法充分捕捉文本质量的多维度特性，需要从用户体验（QoE）视角构建更可靠、更贴近人类感知的评估方法和资源。

Method: 首先构建TextQ-German数据集：针对德语文本摘要和机器翻译任务，通过德语母语者众包研究收集人类质量评分，识别各任务的感知质量维度；随后开发三类自动QoE预测模型（基于Transformer、语言特征和混合方法）；最后用LLM生成的输出扩展数据集并标注整体QoE分数。

Result: 混合模型在几乎所有实验设置下优于纯Transformer基线；仅使用语言特征即可接近微调语言模型的性能；在保留集上的最终验证表明模型能够泛化到未见数据。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了公开可用的NLG评估资源和自动QoE预测基线，为开发更符合人类质量感知的NLG系统奠定基础。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Natural Language Generation (NLG) has made the reliable evaluation of generated text increasingly critical, as these systems, such as large language models (LLMs), are now widely deployed in real-world applications. However, traditional automatic metrics fail to capture the multifaceted nature of perceived quality. In this paper, we introduce TextQ-German, a novel dataset suite for human-centered evaluation of German NLG from a Quality of Experience (QoE) perspective, covering automatic text summarization and machine translation. Through crowdsourcing studies with German speakers, we collect human quality ratings and identify relevant perceptual quality dimensions for each task. We develop automatic QoE prediction models, including transformer-based, linguistic feature-based, and hybrid approaches. Hybrid models outperform pure transformer baselines in almost all experimental settings, while linguistic features alone can approach the performance of fine-tuned language models. The dataset is extended with LLM-generated outputs annotated with overall QoE scores. Final validation on held-out sets indicates generalization to unseen data. Our work contributes a publicly accessible resource for NLG evaluation and baselines for automatic QoE prediction, providing a foundation for developing NLG systems that better align with human quality perception.

</details>


### [143] [SMTrap: Cost-Effective DoS Attacks Against Large Reasoning Models via SMT Conflict Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18921)
*Jian Yang,Zhenqi Feng,Zhaoyang Yu,Zhaoxin Fan,Kejian Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Jianjun Huang,Wei You,Bin Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SMTrap——一种无需模型反馈的LRM拒绝服务（LRM-DoS）攻击框架，利用SMT求解器的冲突计数作为外部信号，引导生成推理密集型的CSP问题实例，在不查询目标模型、不训练攻击模型、不需GPU的情况下实现比现有基线强数倍的DoS效果，并展示了一种基于工具的缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LRM-DoS方法严重依赖模型反馈来合成攻击查询，需要反复查询目标模型或训练专门的攻击模型，这些昂贵的操作严重削弱了攻击的杠杆效应（即攻击成本过高）。

Method: 提出"搜索放大"（search amplification）范式：利用SMT求解器产生的冲突计数作为低成本外部信号，指导推理密集型CSP实例的合成。核心观察是LRM在求解CSP时依赖试错与回溯搜索，SMT冲突计数越高，LRM回溯搜索越多，输出轨迹越长。SMTrap是纯CPU实现的轻量级框架，无需模型查询、攻击模型训练或GPU计算。

Result: 在七个前沿模型上的评估表明，SMTrap达到了最先进的LRM-DoS能力，产生的DoS效果比现有基线强数倍。同时提出了一种基于工具的缓解方法，可显著降低token消耗。

Conclusion: SMTrap证明了利用形式化方法工具（SMT求解器）的元信息作为攻击信号的有效性，实现了高效、低成本的LRM-DoS攻击，并指出了可行的缓解途径。

Abstract: Existing LRM-DoS methods rely heavily on model feedback to synthesize attack queries, requiring either repeated queries to the target model or training a dedicated attack model. These expensive operations severely weaken attack leverage. In this paper, we propose \emph{search amplification}, a novel, model-feedback-free LRM-DoS paradigm. It employs the conflict count derived from an Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) solver as a low-cost external signal to guide the synthesis of inference-heavy Constraint Satisfaction Problem (CSP) instances. Our key observation is that LRMs depend on trial-and-backtracking search when solving CSPs, where higher SMT conflict counts on a given CSP instance positively correlate with more extensive LRM backtracking search and substantially longer output trajectories. Building on this finding, we propose \textsc{SMTrap}, a lightweight, CPU-only framework. Guided by SMT conflict counts, \textsc{SMTrap} generates inference-heavy CSP queries without model queries, attack-model training, or GPU computation. Evaluations across seven frontier models demonstrate the state-of-the-art LRM-DoS capability of \textsc{SMTrap}, producing DoS effects multiple times stronger than existing baselines. To mitigate the threat of \textsc{SMTrap}, we demonstrate a tool-based mitigation that significantly cuts token usage.

</details>


### [144] [Test-Time Scaling in the Wild: Why Exploitation, Not Exploration, Is the Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18931)
*Davide Romano,Kanak Raj,Jerrod Parker,Daniele Giofrè*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对五种测试时扩展（TTS）方法在五个开放式生成基准（医学、法律、金融、通用对话、创意写作）上进行计算量归一化比较。研究发现：候选池扩展有效且随计算量提升，但选择/利用环节失效——奖励模型与真实质量相关性仅约0.12，导致选择近乎随机；树搜索因多样性崩溃加剧问题；只有融合方法一致优于单样本基线，但仍仅恢复约40%的质量。核心结论：瓶颈不在候选池，而在从池中选择的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展技术（如多候选生成、树搜索、迭代精炼）在数学和代码任务上效果显著，但这些任务具有"验证简单"的特点。这些方法在开放式生成任务（如医学、法律、创意写作）上的表现尚未被系统评估，因为这些任务的验证更加困难。作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 采用统一框架将每种方法的token预算分解为探索（生成候选）和利用（从候选中选择/融合最终输出）两个阶段。在五个开放式生成基准上，对五种TTS方法族（多候选采样、树搜索、精炼、融合等）进行计算量归一化的系统比较。

Result: （1）扩展探索有效：候选池中的最佳输出随计算量稳步提升；（2）利用环节失效：SOTA生成器的奖励模型与真实质量相关性仅ρ≈0.12，选择几乎随机；（3）树搜索因多样性崩溃放大选择失败；（4）精炼仅在五个基准之一有效，其余为混淆效应；（5）融合方法（跨候选综合）是唯一一致优于单样本基线的方法，但仍只恢复约40%的可用质量。

Conclusion: 开放式生成任务中测试时扩展的主要瓶颈不在生成更多候选（探索），而在于从候选池中选择最佳输出（利用）的能力不足。未来研究应聚焦于改进选择机制，而非进一步扩大候选规模。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves language model outputs by spending additional inference compute - generating multiple candidates, searching over partial sequences, or iteratively refining drafts. These techniques yield large gains on mathematics and code, but have been developed and stress-tested almost exclusively on tasks where verification is straightforward. We conduct the first compute-normalised comparison of five TTS families across five open-ended generation benchmarks spanning medicine, law, finance, general chat, and creative writing - grounded in a unified framework that decomposes the effectiveness of each method's token budget into exploration and exploitation. The answer depends on which side of that decomposition you examine. Scaling exploration works: the best candidate in the pool improves steadily with compute across all settings. What breaks is exploitation - the step that converts a rich candidate pool into a final output. With state-of-the-art generators, reward models correlate at only $ρ_v \approx 0.12$ with true quality, rendering selection near-random regardless of budget. Tree search amplifies this failure through diversity collapse. Refinement helps on one of five benchmarks; its apparent gains elsewhere are confounded. Only synthesis across candidates (Fusion) consistently improves over single-sample baselines, yet still recovers only ~40% of available quality. The candidate pool is not the bottleneck - choosing from it is.

</details>


### [145] [MedUAG: Unified Understanding and Generation for Medical Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18937)
*Zijie Meng,Yuncheng Zhang,Hualiang Wang,Yitian Tang,Xiaotang Gai,Chen Shen,Songtao Jiang,Shaosheng Cao,Jian Wu,Xian Wu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了医学领域统一理解与生成（UAG）框架的三大贡献：构建了迄今最大的医学UAG数据集MedUAGCorpus（超过600万实例、覆盖14种成像模态），推出了包含12项标准化任务的系统评测基准MedUAGBench，并在此基础上开发了端到端训练的医学统一模型MedUAG。实验证明该模型在理解与生成任务上均表现优异，为下一代医学多模态系统奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型正快速向统一理解与生成框架演进，但将该范式扩展到医学领域面临两大障碍：缺乏全面的训练与评测基准，且缺乏广泛验证的统一医学模型。解决这些空白是本研究的核心动机。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) 构建MedUAGCorpus——目前最大的医学统一理解与生成数据集，包含超过600万实例、覆盖14种成像模态；(2) 推出MedUAGBench——系统化基准，将医学生成评测扩展至12项多样化任务并采用标准化协议；(3) 开发MedUAG——基于上述资源进行端到端训练的统一医学模型。

Result: 大量实验表明，MedUAG在广泛的理解与生成任务上均取得了强劲性能，建立了具有竞争力的基线。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了医学领域统一理解与生成框架的空白，为下一代医学多模态系统的发展铺平了道路。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are rapidly evolving into unified understanding and generation (UAG) frameworks. However, extending these unified paradigms to the medical domain is hindered by: the absence of comprehensive training and evaluation benchmarks, and the lack of broadly validated unified medical model. To address these gaps, we present a comprehensive foundation for medical UAG. First, we construct MedUAGCorpus, the largest unified medical understanding and generation dataset to date, comprising over 6 million instances across 14 imaging modalities. Second, we introduce MedUAGBench, a systematic benchmark that expands medical generation evaluation to 12 diverse tasks under standardized protocols. Finally, leveraging these resources, we develop MedUAG, an end-to-end trained unified medical model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MedUAG achieves strong performance across a wide array of understanding and generation tasks, establishing a competitive baseline and paving the way for next-generation medical multimodal systems.

</details>


### [146] [Institutional Newspapers Pipeline: Deriving billions of high quality tokens from historical newspapers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18972)
*Matteo Cargnelutti,Catherine Brobston,Eben English,Jake Sadow,Kacie Bailey,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jessica Chapel,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个与波士顿公共图书馆联合设计的模块化报纸处理流水线（Institutional Newspapers Pipeline），用于从历史报纸扫描中提取高质量的结构化数据集。该流水线在消费级硬件上运行，涵盖版面分割、OCR、文本分析、类型分类、阅读顺序检测、命名实体识别、主题分类、语言检测和嵌入生成等多个步骤，并在140多万份1795至1930年的公有领域报纸扫描上运行，产出了16.3亿token的OCR数据（8300多万个切片）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史报纸是公共生活的丰富记录，但其版面密集、不规则且带有噪声，导致计算访问既困难又受限。现有方法缺乏可解释性、可定制性和计算效率，难以在有限硬件条件下处理大规模报纸扫描数据。

Method: 该流水线采用模块化架构设计，每个步骤保持可解释和可定制，整体计算开销低至可在工作站级硬件上运行。流程包括：扫描版面分割（类型无关的切片）、每个切片的OCR、随后进行文本分析、类型分类、阅读顺序检测、命名实体识别、主题分类、语言检测，并为每个切片预计算嵌入向量。同时训练了若干小型模型并发布了开放数据集。

Result: 流水线在波士顿公共图书馆的馆藏上运行，覆盖1,473,635份1795至1930年的公有领域报纸扫描，产出83.1百万个独立切片、16.3亿个o200k_base token的OCR输出。报告包含各步骤的方法、训练的小模型、评估结果和数据规模测量，并同时发布了流水线、模型和数据集。

Conclusion: 这项工作被定位为从数千万份报纸扫描中解锁高质量数据的重要一步，为历史报纸的大规模计算化研究提供了可复现、可定制且计算可行的基础设施。

Abstract: Historical newspapers are an abundant record of public life, but their dense, irregular and sometimes noisy layouts make computational access to these materials both challenging and limited. We present the Institutional Newspapers Pipeline, a modular system we jointly designed with Boston Public Library to extract high-quality, structured datasets from historical newspaper scans. It was architected so that each step remains interpretable and customizable, and so that the pipeline as a whole remains computationally frugal enough to run on workstation-level hardware. The pipeline runs each scan through a multi-step process: it segments scans into individual type-agnostic crops and performs OCR on each resulting segment before then performing text analysis, type classification, reading order detection, named entities recognition, subject classification, language detection, and pre-computed embeddings generation on every crop. We ran this pipeline against a portion of Boston Public Library's holdings and released the results as an open dataset. The optical character recognition (OCR) output represents 16.3 billion o200k_base tokens across 83.1 million individual crops, extracted from 1,473,635 public domain newspaper scans published between 1795 and 1930. This report describes our methods for each processing step, the small models we trained, as well as the evaluation results and dataset-scale measurements we collected in the process. It accompanies the release of the pipeline, models, and dataset. We position this work as a substantial step towards unlocking high-quality data from tens of millions of newspaper scans.

</details>


### [147] [DeepWeaver: Bridging the Evidence Synthesis Gap in Open-Ended Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18988)
*Xujia Wang,Yizhe Zhang,Bin Xu,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepWeaver框架，通过维护思想块链（TBCs）结构将嘈杂的检索证据编织成全面、引用规范的答案，并引入高密度基准LoQA来评估证据综合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索-生成管线中，检索本身不足以产生高质量答案——LLM必须将嘈杂且碎片化的证据组织成全面且引用规范的答案，但直接生成往往导致证据利用不足、引用错位、信息坍缩为浅层摘要，即存在检索与生成之间的"证据综合差距"。

Method: 提出DeepWeaver框架，维护思想块链（TBCs）——一种结构化表示，用于组织声明、显著信息、关键词和支持证据。框架使用从属TBC检查残余证据、提交TBC修订并发现新声明，最终再进行生成。

Result: 在知识库和网络两种开放域问答场景中，DeepWeaver在多个LLM上均提升了内容充分性、引用接地性和细节保留；在DeepResearch Bench上取得了更深入的洞见和更高的引用质量，同时发布了高密度证据综合基准LoQA。

Conclusion: 证据编织（evidence weaving）是连接开放域问答中检索与生成的有效机制。

Abstract: Retrieve-then-generate pipelines are commonly used to produce deep-research answers for open-ended questions, but retrieval alone is insufficient: LLMs must organize noisy and fragmented evidence into comprehensive, well-cited answers. We refer to this process as evidence synthesis. However, direct generation often underuses evidence, misaligns citations, and collapses diverse information into shallow summaries, exposing an evidence synthesis gap between retrieval and generation. Thus, we propose DeepWeaver, a novel framework that weaves noisy retrieved evidence into comprehensive answers by maintaining Thought Block Chains (TBCs), a structured representation that groups claims, salient information, keywords, and supporting evidence. DeepWeaver uses subordinate TBCs to inspect residual evidence, commit TBC revisions, and discover new claims before final generation. We evaluate DeepWeaver on open-ended QA over both knowledge bases and the web, and introduce LoQA, a high-density benchmark for evidence synthesis. Across multiple LLMs, DeepWeaver improves content sufficiency, citation grounding, and detail preservation on LoQA, while achieving deeper insights and higher citation quality on DeepResearch Bench. These results show that evidence weaving is an effective mechanism for bridging retrieval and generation in open-ended QA. Our code is available at https://github.com/KlozeWang/DeepWeaver.

</details>


### [148] [Structure, Association, and Decision Value: Representation-Based Difficulty Estimation for Adaptive Inference in African-Language NLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19003)
*Toheeb Ogunade*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了内部表示统计量能否为多语言非洲NLP提供示例级难度信号以支持自适应推理，结果发现不能。研究覆盖15种非洲语言的自然语言推理任务，报告了四个关键结果：AfriXNLI数据集与XNLI存在严重重叠且泄漏测试数据；参数规模不能可靠排序非洲语言能力；角分散比有效秩更受语言决定；有效秩与置信度分别预测不同的计算收益目标且相关性仅0.655。最终结论是没有任何评估信号能使自适应路由优于始终昂贵推理，尽管oracle可节省40%计算并提升11个准确率点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以利用内部表示统计量作为示例级难度信号，在资源有限的多语言非洲NLP场景中实现自适应推理（adaptive inference），以降低计算成本并提升性能。

Method: 使用冻结的现成（off-the-shelf）checkpoint，在15种非洲语言的NLI任务上，比较角分散（angular dispersion）和有效秩（effective rank）等表示统计量作为难度信号的效果；同时评估数据泄漏问题、参数规模效应、语言混杂对相关性的影响以及不同计算收益目标的差异。

Result: 1) AfriXNLI与XNLI测试数据大量重叠，英文配置有高频模型得1.000分，表明存在训练数据暴露；2) 更大参数模型仅在7种语言中更优，8种语言更差，无显著聚合差异；3) 角分散比有效秩更受语言决定，池化相关性会掩盖真实关系；4) 有效秩预测概率提升但不预测预测改变，置信度恰恰相反，两者目标仅相关0.655。无信号能使自适应推理优于始终昂贵推理，oracle可在60%计算下高出11准确率点。

Conclusion: 表示统计量可以在一个计算收益维度上统计显著，但在另一个维度上无关紧要，因此不适合作为决策变量。在非洲多语言设置下，当前测试的自适应路由信号均不可靠，应谨慎对待此类方法。

Abstract: We ask whether internal representation statistics can provide useful example-level difficulty signals for adaptive inference in multilingual African NLP, and find that they cannot in this setting. Studying natural language inference across 15 African languages with frozen off-the-shelf checkpoints, we report four results. First, AfriXNLI's English configuration shares 1,047 of its 1,050 examples verbatim with XNLI evaluation data, and one widely used NLI checkpoint scores 1.000 on that test split, consistent with XNLI test exposure. Because AfriXNLI is derived from XNLI, its English, French and Swahili configurations cannot serve as clean evaluations for XNLI-trained models. Second, parameter count does not reliably order capability across African languages: our larger checkpoint is better in seven languages and worse in eight, with no significant aggregate difference. Third, across three multilingual representation spaces, angular dispersion is consistently more language-determined than effective rank, so pooled correlations can inflate one and mask the other. Fourth, the association that survives language control depends on the target: effective rank predicts probability gain from escalation but not whether escalation changes the prediction, while cheap-model confidence shows the opposite pattern; the two targets correlate at only 0.655. Under the tested models, signals, and compute budgets, no evaluated signal makes adaptive routing preferable to always-expensive inference, although an oracle exceeds it by 11 accuracy points at 60% of the compute. Our central methodological finding is that a representation statistic can be statistically significant for one notion of computational benefit while being irrelevant to another, and therefore be a poor decision variable.

</details>


### [149] [Introducing the Privacy-HSD Trade-off: Hate Speech Detection, but not at the Cost of Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19006)
*Stephen Meisenbacher,Vlad Garbuz,Chirill Donos,Maxim Dnestreanschii,Gabriel Creanga,Andreea-Elena Bodea,Thomas Lampert,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨仇恨言论检测（HSD）与用户隐私之间的权衡关系，发现HSD系统在追求性能时可能无意中编码作者身份信息，威胁个人隐私。作者提出了隐私-HSD权衡的概念，并基准测试了多种文本私有化方法及新提出的领域特定AgnoSpeech技术，证明平衡隐私与HSD虽困难但可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论是影响大量在线用户（尤其是青年和少数群体）的现实威胁，构建可靠的自动仇恨言论检测系统至关重要，但作者认为这必须与个人隐私权相平衡。现有HSD系统可能在追求性能时以牺牲作者身份隐私为代价。

Method: 作者首先证明HSD系统可能无意中编码作者身份信息；在此基础上建立隐私-HSD权衡概念；随后基准测试一系列文本私有化方法，并提出了领域特定的AgnoSpeech技术来平衡隐私与检测性能。

Result: 通过基准测试表明，在隐私保护与HSD性能之间取得平衡是困难的，但同时也是可行的，AgnoSpeech等私有化方法能够在一定程度上实现这一平衡。

Conclusion: 研究结果强烈呼吁更多关于隐私与HSD之间权衡的研究，这两方面对保障在线参与具有切实影响。

Abstract: Hate speech is a real and timely threat that affects a large portion of online users, especially youth and minority groups. While building reliable and robust automatic hate speech detection (HSD) systems is paramount, we argue that this must also be balanced with the individual right to privacy. Exploring the intersection of HSD and privacy, we demonstrate that HSD systems might unintentionally achieve performance at the cost of encoding authorship, posing a threat to privacy. Building on these findings, we establish the notion of a privacy-HSD trade-off, which demands a careful balance. We benchmark a series of text privatization methods, as well as our newly proposed domain-specific AgnoSpeech technique, showing that balancing privacy and HSD is difficult but feasible. The findings make a strong case for more research on the trade-offs between privacy and HSD, both of which have tangible implications for the safeguarding of online participation.

</details>


### [150] [Grading the Graders: Verification Autonomy Levels (L0-L5) for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19009)
*Yajie Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了"验证自主性等级"（VAL）元标准，将LLM验证方案按单一轴——验证规范来源及判定保证——划分为L0至L5等级，并揭示了"完备性盲区"：替代式和采样式验证器只能确认候选方案正确，无法证明没有遗漏。文章还指出完备性仅在形式化可规范属性上可实现，经验性开放世界验证最高只能达到L2（锚定正确性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有验证文献中"level"一词被混用指代至少五种不同含义（验证粒度、概念抽象、风险等级、系统堆栈层、真值来源），导致系统性混淆。作者希望提出一个统一的元标准来厘清这些概念，并解决验证方案能力边界不清的问题。

Method: 作者提出VAL（Verification Autonomy Levels）分类框架，从L0（LLM自声明）到L2（客观真值、仅正确性）再到L3/L4（可判定系统、单属性或域级完备性），L5在非受限情形下不可达。通过四个领域（符号数学、行为监控、医学诊断、代码生成）及现有最强形式化验证基线进行实证论证，并分析17篇论文的概念混用情况。

Result: 文章识别了"完备性盲区"：替代式和采样式验证器无法证明候选未被遗漏；揭示了文献未明确的二分法——完备性仅对形式化可规范属性可达，经验性开放世界验证（如事实核查、诊断）上限为L2（锚定正确性）；证明验证粒度、概念层级、风险、系统堆栈等维度与VAL正交。

Conclusion: VAL为验证方案提供了统一分类标准，解决了17篇论文中的概念混用问题，明确了LLM验证的能力边界：完备性保证仅限于形式化属性，开放世界验证无法超越锚定正确性。该框架有助于研究者和实践者选择与目标匹配的验证方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly paired with verifiers (step checkers, self-consistency filters, tool-based fact checkers, formal proof assistants) that claim to detect the model's errors. Yet the verification literature uses the word "level" to mean at least five different things: verification granularity, concept abstraction, risk tier, system-stack layer, and the epistemic source of the ground truth. We propose Verification Autonomy Levels (VAL), a meta-standard classifying verification schemes along a single axis: where does the verification spec come from, and what does the verdict guarantee? VAL ranges from L0 (LLM self-declaration, no deterministic anchor) through L2 (objective ground truth, correctness only) to L3/L4 (decidable systems with single-property or domain-level completeness), with L5 impossible in the unrestricted case. Central to VAL is the completeness blind spot: substitution- and sampling-based verifiers can confirm that proposed candidates hold, but cannot prove that no candidate was missed. We further identify a dichotomy the literature has not stated: completeness is reachable only for formally specifiable properties, while empirical open-world verification (fact-checking, diagnosis) caps at anchored correctness (L2). We document this across four domains (symbolic mathematics, behavior monitoring, medical diagnosis, and code generation) and in the strongest existing formal-verification baseline, whose authors note the verifier "focuses on the correctness of each step." We show the levels of granularity, concept hierarchy, risk, and system stack are orthogonal to VAL, resolving a systematic conflation across 17 surveyed papers. Code and full assessment are released as supplementary material.

</details>


### [151] [Institutional Books - Enriched Text: A customizable multilingual open-source pipeline for denoising, deduplicating, and annotating OCR text at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19026)
*David Lowry-Duda,Matteo Cargnelutti,Catherine Brobston,Salwa Ismail,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了哈佛图书馆机构藏书（IB-HL）的"富文本"（Enriched Text）处理方法。该数据集包含约98.3万卷、242B tokens的数字化图书。与传统的激进过滤和去重管道不同，该方法通过HTML-like注释层保留元数据，将文本归一化、段落级语言检测、重复段落聚类和每段落bits-per-byte评分提供给用户，使研究者可以根据自身需求定制输出，而非接受全局统一的编辑决策。发布版本IB-HL-ET包含217B tokens，跨983,003卷，组织为13.9亿个带注释的子主题段落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标准大规模预处理管道主要为网页文本优化，倾向于激进地过滤、去重、限制语言并丢弃元数据，这与负责任的信息管理目标相冲突。同时，研究者使用IB-HL时重复进行相似的处理工作，造成资源浪费。

Method: 提出"Enriched Text"方法：在归一化文本的同时通过注释保留元数据；分离书末材料(endmatter)；进行段落级语言检测；识别重复段落聚类；计算每段落的bits-per-byte分数；通过HTML-like注释将这些信息层叠在文本之上，用户可自行解析注释定制输出。

Result: 发布了IB-HL-ET（富文本版本，217B tokens，983,003卷，13.9亿个带注释的子主题段落）以及生成该版本的完整管道，适用于全集合约250种语言。

Conclusion: 该方案通过保留注释而非做出全局编辑决策，使数据集更易于机器解析和人工研究，兼顾了大规模处理效率与信息管理的细致性。

Abstract: Released in 2025, Institutional Books: Harvard Library (IB-HL) is a collection of 983,004 volumes (242B o200k_base tokens), originally digitized through Harvard Library's participation in the Google Books Library project. As researchers and developers have begun to use IB-HL, a tension has emerged between standard large-scale preprocessing practices and the goals of careful information stewardship. Many existing pipelines optimize for web text: as a result, they tend to aggressively filter, deduplicate, restrict by language, and sometimes discard meaningful metadata. Meanwhile, researchers seeking to use IB-HL duplicate effort while performing similar processing and analysis.
  We describe an approach that we call Enriched Text. Instead of producing a single 'complete' stream of tokens, we normalize the text while preserving metadata through annotations. We separate endmatter, detect per-paragraph language, identify clusters of duplicate paragraphs, and compute per-paragraph bits-per-byte scores. We provide this information through HTML-like annotations layered on top of the text. By parsing these annotations, users can tailor the output to their own needs instead of accepting a global editorial decision on content. The pipeline applies to all $\approx$250 languages in the collection.
  This report describes this project's goals, implementation, and design rationale. The release includes IB-HL-ET (an enriched-text version of IB-HL containing 217B o200k_base tokens across 983,003 volumes, organized into 1.39B annotated subtopic paragraphs) and the pipeline that produced it. These serve to make the collection easier for machines to parse and for humans to study.

</details>


### [152] [Intercepting the Kangaroo: Experimental Astrolinguistics with Constructed Lexicons, Active Probing, and Large Language Models as Informants and Hypothesis Proposers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19124)
*Francesco Cordella,Mauro Cappelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将天体语言学（与不同范畴化方式的智能体进行交流）从纯思辨变为实验可操作。研究者用两个具有故意不兼容词库的语言模型作为信息提供者，通过一个脚本化的协调协议在两个范畴系统之间进行翻译。实验表明，该协议在400多次运行中未产生未检测到的误译，能够拦截所有注入的"袋鼠效应"陷阱，并在噪声下优雅降级。协议的正确性来自协议本身，覆盖率则取决于工具能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体语言学自Freudenthal的Lincos（1960）以来一直停留在纯思辨阶段，缺乏实验手段。核心挑战是Quine的不确定性翻译问题——如何确保翻译不会静默地将词汇附着到错误的指称对象上（即"袋鼠效应"）。作者希望将此问题操作化，并建立可验证的翻译协议。

Method: 构建两个词库故意不兼容的语言模型作为对等信息提供者，使用完全脚本化的编排器在两个范畴系统间翻译。协议包含五个核心组件：跨情境消除、预注册预测探针、主动场景选择、更严格的恢复回合和隔离机制。此外，针对假设空间之外的新词，采用生成-测试循环：LLM提出规则假设，脚本负责验证。

Result: 在400多次模拟和实况运行中，协议在测试条件下未产生未检测到的误译，覆盖率超过被动基线（d=0.62）。注入的袋鼠陷阱击败了朴素示教和纯统计学习（100%失败），但完整协议拦截了所有诱饵。在信息提供者噪声下，2%以内单词噪声零袋鼠残留；10%噪声时协议主要选择弃权而非出错。离假设空间词语的恢复覆盖率随提议者能力提升（0%→18%→72%→100%），未检测误译始终为零。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下，正确性是协议性质的体现（协议能保证不产生未检测的误译），覆盖率则是工具能力的体现（取决于LLM提议规则的能力）。该框架将天体语言学从纯思辨转变为可实验研究的领域，也为处理Quine式翻译不确定性提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Astrolinguistics -- communication with minds that categorize reality differently from ours -- has been purely speculative since Freudenthal's Lincos (1960). We make it experimental. Two language models with deliberately incompatible constructed lexicons (one encoding shape, color, and motion; the other fusing color with motion, encoding parity, and lacking shape) serve as informants with complete ground truth, while a fully scripted orchestrator translates between the two category systems. The central failure mode is the kangaroo effect: the silent attachment of a word to the wrong referent -- Quine's indeterminacy of translation, operationalized. Across 400+ simulated and live runs, a protocol combining cross-situational elimination, pre-registered predictive probes, active scene selection, a stricter recovery round, and quarantine produced no undetected mistranslations under the tested conditions and exceeded a passive baseline's coverage (d = 0.62). Injected kangaroo traps defeated naive ostension and pure statistical learning in 100% of runs, while the full protocol intercepted every decoy and, where discriminating evidence is ontologically unavailable, declared Quinean equivalence classes instead of guessing. Under informant noise it degrades gracefully: zero kangaroos persist up to 2% per-word noise; at 10% the protocol predominantly abstains rather than errs. Finally, words outside the scripted hypothesis space (a history-dependent relational term and an XOR contextual homonym) are recovered by a generate-and-test loop in which an LLM proposes rules and the script verifies them: coverage scales with proposer capability (0% -> 18% -> 72% -> 100%) while undetected mistranslations stayed at zero throughout. In the tested conditions, correctness is a property of the protocol; coverage is a property of the instruments.

</details>


### [153] [Comment-level Topic Drift Analysis in the Reddit Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19133)
*Steven Morse,Daniel Runfola,Trenton W. Ford*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于嵌入的动态主题建模方法，对2006至2022年间127亿条Reddit评论进行大规模语义漂移检测与量化分析，通过预训练语言模型生成上下文语义嵌入，采用无监督聚类识别随时间演化的主题簇，并引入零模型比较检验过滤虚假动态。研究发现政治和社会争议性话题在嵌入空间中呈现显著方向性漂移，而音乐、体育等领域保持相对稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模社交媒体语料中，话题的语义漂移和话语演化缺乏系统性的检测与量化方法。现有动态主题建模技术难以在超大规模短文本语料上有效运作，且难以将真实的语义变化与随机波动区分开来。

Method: 采用预训练语言模型生成短文本的上下文语义嵌入，在嵌入空间中使用无监督方法识别动态演化的主题聚类；对现有方法进行可扩展性修改以支持超大规模分析；提出零模型比较检验来过滤虚假的主题动态信号。

Result: 在127亿条Reddit评论（2006-2022）上进行实证分析，发现政治和社会争议性话题在嵌入空间中表现出显著的方向性漂移，话题间距离随时间系统性变化且超出零模型可解释范围；而音乐和体育等领域则保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间本身可以作为分析语义漂移和话语演化的有效媒介，结合零模型检验能够可靠地区分真实语义动态与随机噪声。该方法为大规模语料中的议题演化研究提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: We present a novel application of embedding-based dynamic topic modeling techniques to detect and quantify topic drift at the comment level in a massive corpus. By leveraging pretrained language models to generate contextualized semantic embeddings for short text, we analyzed 12.7 billion Reddit comments spanning 2006 to 2022. Using unsupervised methods on these embeddings, we identify dynamically evolving topic clusters over time. Our primary contribution is a methodology for analysis of semantic drift and discourse evolution in the embedding space itself. We also demonstrate modifications to existing methods that enable this analysis at scale, and we propose and demonstrate a null model comparison test to filter spurious dynamics. Key findings suggest that politically and socially contentious topics exhibit significant directional drift in embedding space, with inter-topic distances changing systematically over time beyond what the null model can explain, whereas domains such as music and sports remain comparatively stable.

</details>


### [154] [ChildSafeAds Shared Task 2026: Commercial Content in Child-Facing YouTube Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19165)
*Thales Bertaglia,Catalina Goanta,Gerasimos Spanakis,Gunes Acar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ChildSafeAds共享任务，该任务旨在对可能触达儿童和青少年的YouTube视频中的商业内容进行分类分析。数据集包含939个频道的3,360个视频片段，通过SponsorBlock众包扩展收集，系统需完成三类任务：识别推广类型（ST1）、分配产品类别（ST2）以及识别法律风险标志（ST3）。数据显示45.5%的视频未正确使用平台广告披露标签，基准标签由GPT-5.4和GPT-5.6生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童和青少年在YouTube上接触商业内容时缺乏足够的保护，现有研究缺乏系统性的数据集和评估基准来理解、分类和监管面向未成年人的商业推广内容，尤其是在广告披露合规性方面的缺口。

Method: 从SponsorBlock众包浏览器扩展中提取3,360个标记为赞助商内容的视频片段，配对其转录文本、视频和频道信息以及视频描述中链接的销售/服务页面。数据按四种累积访问级别划分证据（从转录到链接页面），以便比较不同数据获取成本下的系统性能。专家团队迭代完善分类体系、提示词和模型选择，使用GPT-5.4生成标签，GPT-5.6-luna独立标注开发集。

Result: 构建了包含3,360个视频、939个频道的共享任务数据集，设计了三个子任务（ST1推广类型识别、ST2产品类别分配、ST3法律风险标志识别）和四种证据访问级别。发现45.5%的视频未正确使用"包含付费推广"标签，表明广告披露合规性问题严重。

Conclusion: 该数据集和任务设计为检测面向未成年人的YouTube商业内容提供了标准化评估框架，初步结果显示在广告披露合规性方面存在显著缺口，为后续研究和监管提供了重要参考基础。

Abstract: ChildSafeAds is a shared task on commercial content in YouTube videos likely to reach children and teenagers. It contains 3,360 videos from 939 channels. Each instance begins with a segment submitted to SponsorBlock, an open-source crowdsourced browser extension whose users mark sponsor segments so that others can skip them. We pair the segment with its available transcript, video and channel information, and a sales or service page linked from the video description. Systems determine what kind of offer is being promoted (ST1), assign product categories (ST2), and identify legal risk flags (ST3). The evidence is divided into four cumulative access levels, from the transcript to the linked page, so results can be compared against the cost of collecting the data. 45.5\% of videos in our data failed to properly use the in-platform ad disclosure method (the ``Includes paid promotion'' label). GPT-5.4 produced the labels after the expert organiser team reviewed samples and iterated on the taxonomy, prompts and model choices. GPT-5.6-luna independently labelled the development set. This report describes the task, data and evaluation. An updated version will add participating systems and shared-task results.

</details>


### [155] [SPADE: Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19197)
*Bo Liu,Simon Yu,Yiding Jiang,Ao Qu,Andrew Zhao,Zichen Liu,Junsu Kim,Zijian Zhou,Seungone Kim,Tongzheng Ren,Mickel Liu,Hanfei Yu,Zhaorun Chen,Weiyan Shi,Paul Pu Liang,Luke Zettlemoyer,Yejin Choi,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPADE是一个自对弈强化学习框架，让单个LLM同时扮演环境设计者和推理智能体两个角色，通过可执行代码环境实现开放式自我改进，在多个基准上显著超越固定环境基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言智能体的训练环境池（手工设计、静态合成或冻结验证器）在模型规模扩大时保持目标分布固定，限制了连续自我改进的能力，需要可扩展的多样化自适应目标生成机制。

Method: SPADE采用自对弈强化学习框架，单个LLM扮演两个角色：Environment Designer编写具有Gym风格reset()/step()接口的完整可执行代码环境，Reasoning Agent在其中学习行动。通过智能体的遗憾信号（有/无特权提示的奖励差距）来优化环境设计，使环境针对智能体能力边界同时保持可行性。关键组件包括基于预训练语料文档的环境设计器接地和累积环境记忆。

Result: 在30B参数规模下，SPADE在8个数学、科学、代码和推理基准上平均超越最强固定环境基线+5.3，工具使用场景BFCL-v4多轮提升+5.7，ACEBench-Agent提升+13.9；游戏场景的优势随模型规模扩大而增长。

Conclusion: 通过将环境设计本身变为可学习组件，SPADE为开放式自我改进迈出了具体一步，证明了自适应可执行环境生成对语言智能体持续学习的重要性。

Abstract: Continuous self-improvement requires an ever-expanding pool of self-generated, diverse, adaptive goals. For language agents, existing training environment pools (hand-curated, statically synthesized, or frozen-verifier) keep the goal distribution fixed as the learner scales. We introduce SPADE (Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments), a self-play RL framework in which a single LLM plays two roles: an Environment Designer that writes complete, long-horizon training environments as executable code with an OpenAI Gym-style reset()/step() interface, and a Reasoning Agent that learns to act in them. Each is a stateful, multi-turn environment (state transitions, reward functions, and verification code), so one interface spans reasoning problems and multi-step agentic tool use. The Reasoning Agent's regret is estimated using the gap between its reward with and without privileged hints; in optimizing this regret signal the Environment Designer learns to target environments at the edge of the agent's capabilities while keeping them feasible. Through extensive experimentation, we find several components critical to success: grounding the Environment Designer on documents sampled from a large pretraining corpus, and giving it an accumulated environment memory. Scaling to 30B-parameter models, SPADE improves over the strongest fixed-environment baseline by +5.3 on average across eight held-out math, science, code, and reasoning benchmarks, and lifts the tool-use setting by +5.7 on BFCL-v4 multi-turn and +13.9 on ACEBench-Agent; on the games setting, the margin over the strongest baseline grows with model scale. By making environment design itself a learnable component, SPADE takes a concrete step toward open-ended self-improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [AdaRare: Telemetry-Guided Joint Profile Control for Greybox Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18187)
*Jingchuan Ma,Tongan Liu,Yanhua Liu,Qiaoyun Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaRare，一个AFL++扩展，通过协调五个内部执行机制作为进程内配置档案，利用窗口遥测驱动的线性评分器和控制器目标，在多个基准测试和固件案例中提升了边覆盖率并发现真实漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灰盒模糊测试器结合了交互的队列、变异、字典、能量和比较求解控制面，而先前的自适应系统通常优化其他决策对象或控制层，缺乏对这些控制面的整体协调。

Method: AdaRare作为AFL++扩展，协调五个内部启动机制为一个有界的进程内档案，每5000毫秒更新一次。采用完成窗口的动作诱导遥测，喂给臂局部指数加权线性评分器和档案条件控制器目标。评分器借用不相交LinUCB的代数结构，但作为闭环档案排名机制而非校准上下文赌博机动作值估计器。实验包括三个阶段：Main综合系统证据、Batch A遥测引导选择比较、以及固件案例研究。

Result: Main阶段：相比vanilla AFL++在全部8个目标上中位边覆盖率更高，其中5个Holm显著；最强匹配结果中Full AdaRare在全部5个后续目标上高于CmpLog匹配的AFL++，4个Holm显著。Batch A在所有15个目标-控制比较中中位数更高，13个Holm显著。固件案例中发现5个独立内存损坏漏洞，均获得CVE编号。控制器边界P99中位数低于6.5ms，完整边界低于14.7ms。实验未能建立独立的No-A6对比Shadow或稀缺捆绑效应。

Conclusion: AdaRare通过协调多个控制面的自适应机制，在边覆盖率和真实漏洞发现方面优于基线模糊器，且具有可接受的边界延迟；但部分效应的独立验证仍存在不确定性。

Abstract: Greybox fuzzers combine interacting queue, mutation, dictionary, energy, and comparison-solving control surfaces, while prior adaptive systems typically optimize other decision objects or control layers. We present AdaRare, an AFL++ extension that coordinates five internal actuation mechanisms as one bounded in-process profile updated every 5,000 ms. Completed-window, action-induced telemetry feeds an arm-local recency-weighted linear scorer and a profile-conditioned controller target. The scorer borrows the algebraic structure of disjoint LinUCB, but serves as a closed-loop profile-ranking mechanism rather than a calibrated contextual-bandit action-value estimator or statistical confidence bound.
  Across three sequential repeated-trial phases, Main provides broad integrated-system evidence: AdaRare has higher median edge coverage than vanilla AFL++ on all eight targets, with five Holm-significant comparisons. In the strongest matched result, Full AdaRare has higher median edge coverage than CmpLog-matched AFL++ on all five follow-up targets, with four Holm-significant comparisons. Batch A finds higher medians for telemetry-guided selection than fixed-context, random, and round-robin schedules in all 15 target-control comparisons, with 13 Holm-significant comparisons. The experiments do not establish independent No-A6-versus-Shadow or scarcity-bundle effects; A6 evidence is target-dependent and weakens under batch-wide correction. In an unmatched firmware case study, AdaRare-generated inputs exposed five distinct memory-corruption findings, each reproduced in a separate environment and later assigned a CVE identifier. Controller-boundary compute P99 medians are below 6.5 ms for a five-second window; complete-boundary P99 medians including synchronous logging are below 14.7 ms. These measurements characterize boundary latency, not total system overhead.

</details>


### [157] [Model Card for OpenAI Privacy Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18274)
*Charles de Bourcy,Sahra Ghalebikesabi,Avi Schwarzschild,Alex Gorbachev,Mihai Maruseac,Annie Chu,Vol Kyrylov,Tong Mu,Ally Bennett,Andy Nguyen,Casey Meehan,Jessica Gan Lee,Shane Bauer,Harold Nguyen,Rodolpho Eckhardt,Yuqi Liu,Charlie Oxborough,Marco Rougeth,Omar Chedid,Caio Costa,Yash Parikh,Yao Li,Congzheng Song,Om Thakkar,Vinnie Monaco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OpenAI Privacy Filter 是一个紧凑的双向token分类模型，用于检测和脱敏非结构化文本中的个人身份信息（PII）和机密信息。该模型由自回归预训练检查点转换而来，变为双向带状注意力分类器，单次前向传播即可标记输入序列。模型共15亿参数，每token激活5000万参数，支持128,000 token上下文窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种高效、可配置的本地部署模型，用于在分层隐私工作流中实现数据最小化，检测和脱敏PII与机密信息，同时支持领域特定微调。

Method: 从自回归预训练检查点衍生，转换为双向带状注意力（banded-attention）分类器；使用约束Viterbi解码器生成跨八个隐私类别的连贯span，并提供可配置的精确率-召回率权衡操作点。

Result: 模型具有15亿总参数、每token 5000万激活参数、128,000 token上下文窗口，能以单次前向传播完成序列标注。

Conclusion: 该模型定位为分层隐私工作流中的可配置数据最小化组件，而非匿名化或合规保证工具。

Abstract: OpenAI Privacy Filter is a compact, bidirectional token-classification model for detecting and redacting personally identifiable information (PII) and secrets in unstructured text. The model is derived from an autoregressively pretrained checkpoint and converted into a bidirectional, banded-attention classifier that labels an input sequence in a single forward pass. A constrained Viterbi decoder produces coherent spans across eight privacy categories and exposes configurable operating points for precision-recall tradeoffs. Privacy Filter has 1.5 billion total parameters, 50 million active parameters per token, and a 128,000-token context window. It is designed for efficient local deployment and domain-specific fine-tuning. Privacy Filter is intended as a configurable data-minimization component within layered privacy workflows, not as an anonymization or compliance guarantee.

</details>


### [158] [XNET: Intelligent Dynamic Sampling for High-Speed Network Security Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18349)
*Thomas Papastergiou,Karthika Subramani,Joseph Reilly,Boladji Vinny Adjibi,Pierros-Christos Skafidas,Roberto Perdisci,Manos Antonakakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出XNET系统，利用Linux XDP技术和动态采样机制，在商用硬件上实现线速流量监控。该系统通过分类流量安全价值并动态采样，放大安全相关流量的可见性，在保持无丢包的同时实现高达84%的流量缩减，并将原本微弱的流量可见性提升五倍，且已在大规模真实网络中部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代企业网络中100GbE线速日益普遍，但运营方和安全应用难以高效扩展，通常依赖昂贵硬件、过度采样或复杂分布式部署。随机包采样导致的意外丢失会产生低质量流量，尤其可能漏检隐藏于低速率流量中的关键安全事件（如APT/恶意软件命令与控制通信）。

Method: XNET利用Linux XDP（eXpress Data Path）技术在商用硬件上高效处理数据包，对数据包进行安全价值分类，并依据配置的策略进行动态采样。通过放大高安全价值流量的可见性、缩减低价值流量，实现灵活可扩展的动态监测系统。

Result: 在真实大规模网络中部署验证，XNET可达到84%的流量缩减且无丢包，同时将原本可忽略的流量可见性提升五倍。压力测试证明系统可扩展至100Gbps，IDS应用中的采样检测率达99.6%。

Conclusion: XNET展示了在商用硬件上实现线速安全流量监控的可行性，通过动态采样策略解决了传统采样导致的安全事件漏检问题，兼顾了系统性能、扩展性与安全可见性，为高带宽网络环境下的安全监测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Growing network speeds, with 100GbE line rates becoming common in modern enterprise networks, pose challenges to operators and security applications, as they struggle to scale their operational efficiency accordingly, without relying on costly hardware, excessive sampling, or complex distributed deployments. Unintentional loss due to stochastic packet sampling often produces low-quality traffic, further risking missed detection of critical security incidents, particularly those hidden in typically low-rate traffic, such as APT/malware command-and-control communications. In this paper, we introduce XNET, a system that monitors traffic at line rate using commodity hardware and applies dynamic sampling to amplify the visibility of high security value traffic. XNET leverages Linux's XDP technology to process packets efficiently, classify them based on their security value, and sample them as per configured policies. The outcome is a reduced packet stream in which the security-relevant portion of the traffic is amplified at the expense of less interesting traffic segments. XNET is a highly flexible, scalable and dynamic system that can be adapted based on a network's needs. We deployed XNET in a large real-world network using only commodity hardware, where our results show that XNET can achieve up to 84% traffic reduction with no packet loss while increasing the visibility of otherwise negligible traffic fivefold. With controlled stress tests, we further demonstrate XNET's scalability up to 100Gbps. Additionally, we show that XNET sampling led to a detection rate of 99.6% in an IDS application.

</details>


### [159] [Task-Conditioned Least-Privilege Learning for Executable Terminal and MCP Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18351)
*Alexander Tu,Michael Tu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何通过后训练让4B参数的大语言模型智能体在终端和MCP环境中学会"最小权限"原则，即在任务条件约束下合理行使权力。通过六个维度的确定性验证器审计每个动作，并结合任务特定的充分权限包络来计算超额权限值进行后训练优化，最终将安全成功率从64.36%提升到98.48%，超额权限错误事件从4.56%降至0.79%。研究表明这种"最少权限感知"的后训练是有用的补充控制层，但不能替代传统的权限门控和沙箱机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统权限门控系统无法单独解决大语言模型智能体行使用户未授予或任务不需要的权力（即"超额权限错误"）的问题，因此需要探索通过后训练来教会模型任务条件化的权限判断能力。

Method: 提出一个框架：每个动作在执行前和执行后（观察效果时）都经过审计，沿六个风险维度进行，使用确定性验证器对完成度、证据、确切状态、禁止尝试和安全成功进行打分。结合任务特定的充分权限包络，计算轨迹的任务特定超额特权值，并在后训练中对其进行优化。在Qwen3.5-4B上使用1,500个任务进行训练。

Result: 训练后模型在2,896个评估回合（覆盖500个保留任务）上达到98.48%的安全成功率，远超基线的64.36%；超额权限错误事件从4.56%降至0.79%。外部测试显示能力保持和提示引导改进。400个任务的续训练研究显示泛化能力，超额权限事件降低6.99个百分点且保持既有能力。

Conclusion: 通过最少权限感知的后训练来学习约束，是终端和MCP环境中工具使用智能体的有效附加控制层，但它不能替代权限门控和沙箱机制。

Abstract: Tool-using large language-model agents can complete a task while exercising authority that the user did not grant or the task does not need, causing excess-authority errors. Traditional permission gating systems alone for validating agent environments are insufficient. We study whether post-training can teach a 4B-parameter model to choose task-conditioned authority in executable terminal and Model Context Protocol (MCP) environments to complement those measures. We propose a framework where each action is audited before execution and again from observed effects along six dimensions of risk. This auditing is conducted using deterministic verifiers that score completion, evidence, exact state, prohibited attempts, and safe success. In conjunction with predefined task-specific sufficient-authority envelopes, we determine task-specific excess privilege values for trajectories, which are then optimized for in post-training. We find that after training using this framework on Qwen3.5-4B over 1,500 tasks, the selected seed reaches 98.48% safe success across 2,896 evaluation episodes spanning all 500 held-out tasks, compared with 64.36% for the base policy, and reduces excess-authority error events from 4.56% to 0.79%. Furthermore, external tests show capability retention and prompt-directed improvement. A 400 task continuation study also found evidence of generalization, reducing excess-authority events by 6.99 percentage points while maintaining previous capabilities. We conclude learned restraint through least-privilege aware post-training is therefore useful as an additional control layer for tool-using agents in executable terminal and MCP environments, but it does not replace permission gates and sandboxing.

</details>


### [160] [0xPass: A Secure Protocol for Universal Cross-Chain Accounts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18359)
*Bernardo David,Keon Kim,Krish Chelikavada*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 0xPass是一个模块化的通用跨链账户协议架构，将请求编排、交易求解和交易签名分离为可互操作的层次，通过阈值签名、约束授权委派和分阶段去中心化部署，在降低中心化信任的同时保持用户对交易授权的完全控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用账户允许用户通过单一界面管理跨异构区块链生态系统的资产和执行操作，但引入了涉及认证、授权、交易签名、密钥托管、恢复和去中心化等方面的安全与信任挑战，需要一种既能扩展跨链功能又能渐进降低信任依赖的架构方案。

Method: 提出模块化协议架构，将请求编排、交易求解和交易签名分离为互操作层；用户批准的请求绑定到认证身份并在各层间授权；采用阈值签名防止单个交易节点持完整签名密钥；支持约束授权委派、交易策略、账户恢复、分布式密钥管理和各独立子网络间的可审计通信；设计从中心化服务到许可网络再到无许可网络的分阶段部署路径。

Result: 0xPass提供了一个实用框架，在逐步降低中心化信任的同时扩展跨链账户功能，并通过阈值签名和分层授权机制保障了交易安全性与用户控制权。

Conclusion: 0xPass架构为通用跨链账户提供了一条渐进式去中心化的可行路径，从中心化运营到许可网络再到无许可网络，兼顾安全性、可扩展性与用户自主权，是解决跨链账户信任挑战的实用方案。

Abstract: Universal accounts allow users to manage assets and execute operations across heterogeneous blockchain ecosystems through a single interface, but they introduce security and trust challenges involving authentication, authorization, transaction signing, key custody, recovery, and decentralization. This paper presents 0xPass, a modular protocol architecture for universal cross-chain accounts. 0xPass separates request orchestration, transaction solving, and transaction signing into interoperable layers. User-approved requests are bound to authenticated identities and authorized across layers, while threshold signatures prevent any single transaction node from holding a complete signing key. The design also supports constrained authorization delegation, transaction policies, account recovery, distributed key management, and auditable communication among independently operated sub-networks. We describe a staged deployment path from a centrally operated service to a permissioned network and ultimately to a permissionless network with third-party modules, collateral-backed onboarding, and rotating key-management committees. The resulting architecture provides a practical framework for extending cross-chain account functionality while progressively reducing centralized trust and preserving user control over transaction authorization.

</details>


### [161] [AoNT Trap: Borromean-Entangled Mutable Chameleon Trapdoor Hash All-or-Nothing Stream Cipher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18403)
*Victor Kebande*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BEC-Trap流密码方案，将Borromean互锁结构、变色龙陷门哈希和全有或全无转换（AoNT）融为一体，实现密钥、初始化向量和内部状态三者Borromean连锁，任一组件被攻破或篡改将导致整个密钥流崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流密码在密钥管理、状态同步和防破坏方面存在不足，尤其在动态环境下需要频繁重密钥和向量刷新时，缺乏既能保证安全性又能支持高效密钥轮换的轻量级方案。

Method: 采用Borromean环互锁结构将密钥K、初始化向量V和内部状态St三者绑定，任一组件被破坏即导致整个密钥流失效；同时集成变色龙陷门哈希实现受控碰撞，支持无缝重密钥、向量刷新和状态轮换而无需端点重新同步。

Result: 安全分析表明该构造能抵御密钥恢复攻击、状态妥协和去同步攻击，具备机密性、前向保密性和自适应密钥管理能力，同时计算开销低，适用于高吞吐量安全通信场景。

Conclusion: BEC-Trap提供了一种兼顾安全性与效率的流密码原语，适用于安全消息传递、IoT通信和隐私保护的区块链通道等新一代安全通信应用。

Abstract: This work introduces the Borromean-Entangled Chameleon Trapdoor Hash All-or-Nothing (AoNT) Stream Cipher (BEC-Trap), a novel construction that merges Borromean interdependence, trapdoor-enabled mutability, and streaming encryption into a unified framework. The (BEC-Trap) cipher links key (K), initialization vector (V ), and internal state (St) in a Borromean structure, ensuring that breaking, guessing, or removing any one component collapses the entire keystream, providing a computational (AoNT) interdependence under standard cryptographic assumptions. A chameleon trapdoor hash is integrated to permit controlled collisions, enabling seamless rekeying, (V ) refresh, and state rotation without resynchronizing endpoints. This design provides confidentiality, forward secrecy, and adaptive key management with low computational overhead, making it suitable for high-throughput secure messaging, IoT communications, and privacy-preserving blockchain channels. Security analysis of the (BEC-Trap) shows that the construction is resistant to key-recovery attacks, state compromise, and desynchronization attempts, delivering a robust cryptographic primitive for next-generation secure communications.

</details>


### [162] [Beyond Distortion Robustness: Rethinking Severe Cropping as Erasure-Resilient Message Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18567)
*Bo Pang,Weibin Kong,Juntu Dong,Minghan Li,Zhongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CREST框架，将严重裁剪下的鲁棒消息嵌入从"失真鲁棒"重新定义为"擦除鲁棒"问题，通过LT喷泉编码将紧凑QR消息扩展为冗余空间载荷，并配合裁剪感知嵌入和碎片恢复，在COCO、DIV2K和VOC2012数据集上显著提升严重裁剪场景下的消息恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒消息嵌入方法均基于失真鲁棒范式，假设嵌入信号在空间上仍存在但被噪声、模糊或压缩降质。然而严重裁剪会移除载体本身的一部分，导致载荷部分消失而非单纯信号损坏，这是根本不同的挑战。作者指出需要从擦除鲁棒的视角重新审视该问题。

Method: CREST框架采用编码理论与神经嵌入/恢复相结合的设计：通过LT喷泉编码将紧凑的QR消息扩展为冗余空间载荷，实现擦除鲁棒性；同时设计裁剪感知的嵌入策略和碎片恢复机制，使系统在部分载体丢失时仍能恢复完整消息。

Result: 在混合失真且面积保留率0.7的条件下，CREST将TRA从18.52%提升至68.45%，EMR从13.88%降至4.21%。在COCO2017上，当仅保留30-50%图像面积时CREST仍能达到48.55-65.12%的TRA，而所有对比基线均无法恢复消息。

Conclusion: 实验表明严重裁剪应被视为擦除问题而非传统失真问题，神经嵌入与编码恢复的联合设计是解决该问题的有效方向，这为多媒体安全中的鲁棒嵌入提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Robust message embedding in images is important for multimedia security applications such as copyright protection and content tracing. Existing methods are largely developed under a distortion robustness paradigm, where the embedded signal remains spatially present but is degraded by noise, blur, or compression. Severe cropping poses a fundamentally different challenge because it removes part of the carrier itself, causing partial payload disappearance rather than mere signal corruption. In this paper, we revisit robust message embedding from an erasure-resilience perspective and present CREST, a proof-of-concept framework for severe-cropping-robust embedding. CREST combines coding-theoretic redundancy with neural embedding and recovery by expanding a compact QR message into a redundant spatial payload via LT fountain coding and coupling it with cropping-aware embedding and fragment recovery. Experiments on COCO, DIV2K, and VOC2012 show that CREST improves recovery under severe cropping while maintaining competitive visual quality. Under mixed distortions with an area retention ratio of 0.7, CREST improves TRA from 18.52% to 68.45% and reduces EMR from 13.88% to 4.21% over the strongest baseline. On COCO2017, CREST still achieves 48.55--65.12% TRA when only 30--50% of the image area is retained, whereas all compared baselines fail to recover the message. These results suggest that severe cropping is better understood as an erasure problem rather than a conventional distortion problem, motivating the joint design of neural embedding and coding-based recovery.

</details>


### [163] [VQC-ZTI: Variational Quantum Control for Zero Trust Protection of the Tactile Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18572)
*Mubassir Serneabat Sudipto,Shakil Ahmed,Ashfaq Khokhar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VQC-ZTI框架，一种用于触觉互联网服务零信任保护的分裂平面变分量子分类器。该框架将离路径的VQC异常评分与在路径的策略执行分离，在不扰动控制路径的前提下实现安全决策，在CESNET数据集上达到0.99以上的AUC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉互联网服务将网络事件直接关联到物理执行，其安全决策必须在提升风险区分能力的同时不干扰控制路径。现有方案难以在保持控制行为可预测的前提下独立调优检测灵敏度和策略激进程度。

Method: 采用分裂平面架构：离路径的变分量子分类器分析加密流遥测数据，在路径的策略引擎应用缓存的确定性授权、限制、升级和拒绝动作。通过解耦异常评分与执行，实现检测灵敏度和策略激进程度的独立调节。使用混合PyTorch-PennyLane实现全混合量子神经网络，并在随机、实体组和时间三种划分方式下评估。

Result: 在CESNET聚合流量上，全混合量子神经网络在随机、实体组和时间划分下分别达到0.9981、0.9974和0.9941的平均AUC，相比ExtraTrees将假阳性率分别降低44.6%、49.6%和67.9%。组件时序分解显示批量VQC评分位于异步证据路径而非即时执行路径。

Conclusion: VQC-ZQC通过分裂平面设计有效实现触觉互联网服务的零信任保护，在保证控制路径可预测性的同时显著提升检测精度，验证了量子机器学习在安全决策中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Tactile Internet services couple cyber events directly to physical actuation, so security decisions must improve risk discrimination without perturbing the control path. This paper presents VQC-ZTI, a split-plane Variational Quantum Classifier framework for zero-trust protection of Tactile Internet services, in which an off-path VQC analyzes encrypted-flow telemetry while an on-path policy engine applies cached deterministic grant, restrict, step-up, and deny actions. By decoupling anomaly scoring from enforcement, VQC-ZTI preserves predictable control behavior and allows detector sensitivity and policy aggressiveness to be tuned independently. We evaluate the framework on CESNET-derived aggregated traffic using random, entity-group, and temporal holdouts with a hybrid PyTorch-PennyLane implementation. The full-hybrid Quantum Neural Network achieves mean areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.9981, 0.9974, and 0.9941 and reduces the false-positive rate relative to ExtraTrees by 44.6%, 49.6%, and 67.9%, respectively. A representative component-timing decomposition further illustrates that batched VQC scoring remains in the asynchronous evidence path rather than the immediate enforcement path.

</details>


### [164] [Autonomous Cyber Defense in Connected Vehicles: A Multi-Agent Approach to V2X Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19135)
*Krishna Teja Medam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三层次多智能体架构，用于在100毫秒的时间约束内检测联网车辆V2X消息中的伪造Basic Safety Message，将安全-安全冲突（丢弃可疑消息vs丢弃真实紧急警报）作为一等设计约束。车辆层10ms内完成消息分类（Accept/Drop/Quarantine/Escalate），边缘层50ms内跨车辆融合威胁评估，云端通过拜占庭容错联邦学习精化检测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有入侵检测系统按单车辆、单消息、静态规则运行，无法处理联网车辆在100毫秒内判定Basic Safety Message真伪的时间耦合问题。系统既无法识别跨车队或跨时间出现的攻击模式，也忽视了丢弃可疑消息与丢弃真实紧急警报之间的根本矛盾。

Method: 提出三层次多智能体架构：(1)车辆层：10ms预算内将每条V2X消息分类为接受/丢弃/隔离/升级四个动作，不确定时偏向升级而非冒险丢弃；(2)边缘层：50ms预算内跨多个车辆融合威胁评估，利用互补传感器观解决安全-安全冲突；(3)云端：通过拜占庭容错联邦学习精化检测模型并分发权重。所有时间约束直接源自SAE J2735和ETSI EN 302 637-2的100ms BSM周期。

Result: 该框架是首个同时为标准化的延迟预算分配到三个部署层级，并将安全-安全冲突解决作为一等设计约束的架构。边缘智能体的对抗投毒以及自主安全响应的监管框架缺失被识别为遗留的开放问题。

Conclusion: 该架构将时间约束作为硬性设计需求而非性能目标，通过多层协作和不确定性时的升级偏向策略，在安全性与安全性之间取得平衡。未来工作需解决边缘对抗投毒和监管框架缺失问题。

Abstract: A connected vehicle has roughly 100 milliseconds to decide whether an incoming Basic Safety Message is real or fabricated. If a false emergency braking alert reaches the planning pipeline in time, the car brakes - a safety failure triggered by a security failure. Existing intrusion detection systems are not designed to handle that coupling. They operate per vehicle, per message, with static rules - blind to attack patterns that only emerge across a fleet or over time, and blind to the fundamental tension between dropping a suspicious message and dropping a real emergency alert. We propose a three-tier multi-agent architecture that treats this timing constraint as a hard design requirement, not a performance target. At the vehicle level, an onboard agent classifies each incoming V2X message into one of four actions - Accept, Drop, Quarantine, or Escalate - within a 10-millisecond budget, deliberately biased toward Escalate when uncertain, passing ambiguous cases to the roadside edge agent rather than risking a dropped legitimate alert. The edge agent operates across a roadside unit zone with a 50-millisecond budget, fusing threat assessments from multiple vehicles and resolving safety-security conflicts using complementary sensor observations. The cloud tier refines detection models through Byzantine fault-tolerant federated learning and redistributes updated weights to the fleet. Every timing constraint derives directly from the 100-millisecond Basic Safety Message cycles mandated by SAE J2735 and ETSI EN 302 637-2. No existing framework simultaneously assigns standards-grounded latency budgets to all three deployment tiers while treating safety-security conflict resolution as a first-class design constraint. Remaining open problems - adversarial poisoning at the edge and the absence of regulatory frameworks for autonomous security response - are discussed as future work.

</details>


### [165] [Finality Before Disclosure for Ledger Authenticators in the Quantum Random Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18605)
*Maja Lie,Benjamin Marsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文引入"账本认证器"（ledger authenticators）概念及LAEUF不可伪造性实验，用于分析基于账本（如公共账本）的反应式授权协议的安全性。作者识别了两种条件性资源边界，并提出了一个联合账本与量子随机预言机的执行模型，证明了在封闭最终目标集大小为K时的安全性上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代公共账本中的状态转换授权越来越多地依赖于先前交易、最终状态、时序和排序信息，而非传统的公钥+消息+签名的简单模式。现有的安全性模型无法充分捕捉这类反应式授权协议的特性，如规范转换新鲜度、自适应腐化、暴露后包含、审查和对手排序等，因此需要新的安全模型。

Method: 本文提出账本认证器（ledger authenticators）框架，并定义LAEUF（Ledger Authenticator Existential Unforgeability）不可伪造性实验。该模型将认证安全性与账本活性分离，捕捉多种攻击场景。作者识别两类条件资源边界：(1)满足单事件条件的认证器可产生上下文一次性签名；(2)在可重绑定揭示类中，安全性需要计算性事后非可采纳性。此外，定义了联合账本与量子随机预言机（QROM）执行模型，其中量子状态在经典终结切割和预言机评估中持续存在。

Result: 对于封闭最终目标集大小为至多K的情况，作者证明了安全性上界为3β_cut² + 3c_co KQ²/2^λ + 6ℓ/2^λ，其中β_cut表示切割时已存在的新鲜开钥数量。一个提交-关闭-揭示（commit, close, reveal）认证器实例化了该框架，并获得多用户生命周期QROM安全界。

Conclusion: 本文为基于公共账本的反应式授权协议提供了一套系统的安全性分析框架，在量子随机预言机模型下给出了严格的安全性界限，并通过具体的认证器实例验证了框架的可用性。

Abstract: Public ledgers increasingly authorize state transitions using prior transactions, finalized state, timing, and ordering rather than only a public key, message, and portable signature. We introduce ledger authenticators and $\LAEUF$, an unforgeability experiment for reactive authorization protocols whose public judgment algorithm reads a finalized transcript. The model separates authentication safety from ledger liveness and captures canonical transition freshness, adaptive corruption, exposure before inclusion, censorship, and adversarial ordering. We identify two conditional resource boundaries. An authenticator satisfying our single event conditions yields a contextual one-time signature. Within our rebindable reveal class, safety requires computational post-disclosure non-admissibility. When precursor admission uses only public computation and ledger scheduling, this condition is enforced by closing the evidence eligible to use a disclosed credential. If newly constructed evidence remains admissible after disclosure, censoring the honest reveal gives a forgery. We then define a joint ledger and quantum random oracle execution model in which quantum state persists across classical finalization cuts and oracle evaluations made through the ledger are charged. For a closed finalized target set of size at most $K$, we prove the bound $3β_{\mathsf{cut}}^2+3c_{\mathsf{co}}KQ^2/2^λ+6\ell/2^λ$, where $β_{\mathsf{cut}}$ accounts for fresh openings already present at the cut. A commit, close, reveal authenticator instantiates the framework and obtains a multi-user lifetime QROM bound.

</details>


### [166] [IriSig-Spoof: A Real-World Benchmark for Time-Robust Satellite RF Fingerprinting and Spoofing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18642)
*Shichang Guo,Yuanyu Zhang,Shuangrui Zhao,Ji He,Pinchang Zhang,Yulong Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍IriSig-Spoof，一个包含517万条消息、覆盖66颗卫星、历时32天的真实Iridium卫星射频指纹数据集，并基于SDR生成室内外欺骗信号。论文建立了三个基准任务（时间鲁棒性、开放集识别、跨场景欺骗检测），并使用MACNN模型进行验证，最佳配置跨日平均准确率达97.75%，开放集AUROC为0.9715。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星互联网已成为关键通信基础设施，但开放无线链路容易受到卫星冒充和信号欺骗攻击。RF指纹识别是潜在防御手段，但现有卫星RFF方法缺乏统一数据集和基准框架来支持时间维度、开放集和跨场景评估，导致方法可靠性难以衡量。

Method: 构建了IriSig-Spoof真实数据集（66颗卫星、517万条消息、32天采集），使用SDR生成室内外欺骗信号；建立三个基准任务：时间鲁棒性评估、开放集未知信号拒绝的RFF识别、跨场景欺骗检测；采用多尺度注意力卷积神经网络（MACNN）作为基线模型进行实验。

Result: 时间鲁棒性方面，最佳配置平均跨日准确率达97.75%；开放集评估中MACNN的AUROC为0.9715，但发现有效未知信号拒绝并不保证可靠的ID分配；跨场景实验在低假阳性率下表现出差异。

Conclusion: IriSig-Spoof为时间变化和攻击条件变化下的鲁棒RFF方法评估提供了可复现的基础，揭示了现有RFF方法在时间鲁棒性、开放集识别和跨场景欺骗检测中的关键挑战与局限。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite Internet is becoming critical communications infrastructure, yet its open wireless links remain vulnerable to satellite impersonation and signal spoofing. Radio frequency fingerprinting (RFF) offers a potential defense by exploiting transmitter-specific hardware imperfections manifested in received signals. However, the reliability of existing satellite RFF methods remains difficult to assess because no unified dataset and benchmark support temporal, open-set, and cross-scenario evaluation. To address this gap, we introduce IriSig-Spoof, a real-world Iridium dataset comprising 5.17 million messages collected from 66 satellites over 32 days, together with software-defined radio (SDR)-generated spoofing signals from indoor and outdoor settings. We further establish three benchmark tasks: temporal robustness evaluation, open-set RFF identification with unknown-signal rejection, and cross-scenario spoofing detection. Experiments using a multi-scale attention convolutional neural network (MACNN) show that temporal robustness varies across configurations, with the best configuration achieving 97.75% average cross-day accuracy. In open-set evaluation, MACNN achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.9715, while showing that effective unknown-signal rejection does not necessarily ensure reliable identity assignment. Cross-scenario experiments reveal differences at low false-positive rates. IriSig-Spoof provides a reproducible basis for evaluating robust RFF methods under temporal variation and changing attack conditions.

</details>


### [167] [Improving LLM-Based SSH Honeypots Through Prompting and Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18686)
*Muris Sladić,Veronica Valeros,Eman Alibalić,Sebastian Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何通过提示设计和监督微调来提升基于本地LLM的SSH蜜罐的shell仿真准确性，测试了8个模型（包括原始GPT-3.5和七个开源权重本地模型），发现提示设计效果显著、微调效果依赖于数据集覆盖范围，但两者的效果并不能简单叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的SSH蜜罐常依赖封闭云模型（如GPT-3.5），虽仿真效果好但存在部署问题（版本不稳定、供应商变更、攻击者驱动的成本、模型退役）。本地开源权重模型可避免这些部署问题，但性能较差且易暴露蜜罐身份，因此需要研究如何改进本地模型的shell仿真能力。

Method: 使用提示设计和监督微调两种方法，对8个模型（原始微调GPT-3.5及七个开源权重本地模型，每个与其基础模型对比）进行调优，并测试提示结构在不同模型家族间的迁移性。用34个自动化单元测试在单会话和新会话两种场景下评估shell仿真准确率。

Result: 提示设计对shell仿真准确性有显著提升；微调效果取决于数据集覆盖范围，原始112对话数据集微调未提升总体通过率，而基于蜜罐日志扩展的数据集则产生了明显更强的本地模型；提示设计和微调各自都能改善本地LLM蜜罐，但两者结合的效果并非简单叠加，强规则提示与监督适应可能因解决重叠的shell行为约束而产生冲突。

Conclusion: 本地模型可通过提示设计和微调显著提升SSH蜜罐的shell仿真准确度，但两种优化方法之间的交互关系复杂，需要进一步研究如何有效结合它们以避免冲突并最大化整体性能。

Abstract: LLM-based SSH honeypots often use closed cloud LLMs because they give strong shell realism, but cloud models create deployment problems. These include no stable versioning, provider-side changes, attacker-driven cost, and model decommissioning. Local open-weight models avoid these problems, but they usually perform worse and make mistakes that reveal the honeypot. These mistakes include malformed outputs, command echoing, inconsistent filesystem state, and AI-style artifacts. This paper studies how to improve and evaluate the shell emulation accuracy of local LLM-based SSH honeypots using prompt design and supervised fine-tuning. We fine-tune and evaluate eight models in total: the original fine-tuned GPT-3.5 model used in shelLM and seven open-weight local models, each compared to its base model. We also test how prompt structure transfers across model families. Using 34 automated unit tests that measure shell emulation accuracy in single-session and fresh-session settings, we find that prompt design has a large effect and that fine-tuning depends on dataset coverage. Fine-tuning on the original 112-conversation dataset does not improve aggregate pass rate, while an expanded dataset built from honeypot logs produces clearly stronger local models. Taken together, the results suggest that prompting and fine-tuning can each improve local LLM honeypots on their own, but their effects do not combine straightforwardly, since strong rule-based prompting and supervised adaptation can also conflict by addressing overlapping shell-behavior constraints.

</details>


### [168] [CauSec: Unboxing the Causal Drivers of Static Vulnerability Analysis Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18876)
*Md Akram Khan,Daniel Rodriguez-Cardenas,Alejandro Velasco Dimate,Denys Poshyvanyk,Adwait Nadkarni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CAUSEC框架，用于测试静态应用安全测试（SAST）工具中隐含假设的有效性。通过对57个假设的系统性文献综述和57,038条告警的实验验证，揭示了假设对工具性能的因果影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAST工具常以牺牲检测能力换取性能（精度、速度、可扩展性），这些设计选择依赖于对目标代码或分析技术的假设。但这些假设是否真正有效、牺牲是否值得，缺乏系统的检验方法。

Method: 提出CAUSEC因果分析框架：将SAST工具假设形式化为安全假设抽象，结合假设驱动的因果建模、效应估计和验证；通过对crypto-API误用SAST的系统文献综述，识别并分析了57个假设；在四个主流工具上针对一个常见假设进行测试，使用57,038条人工标注告警数据集。

Result: 发现了关于假设和因果效应的多个关键发现，验证了CAUSEC框架的实用性和鲁棒性，提供了57个假设的定性分析结果。

Conclusion: SAST工具的牺牲性设计选择所依赖的假设并非总是有效，需要因果分析来验证。作者将研究发现凝练为3条对未来工作的启示。

Abstract: Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tools are widely used in both industry and academia. Such tools often make design choices that sacrifice detection to achieve higher performance, i.e., increased precision, decreased runtime, or increased scalability. These design choices rely on certain assumptions regarding the target code or the analysis technique itself. Hence, the assumptions directly impact the detection outcome through the design choices they influence. This motivates a key question: do the sacrifices in the detection capabilities actually help tools achieve the expected performance gains? That is, are the underlying assumptions valid?
  This paper seeks to address this question by relying on a key observation that the assumptions made by these tools are generally of a causal nature. We propose CAUSEC, a causal analysis framework that makes SAST assumptions testable and explains why the performance changes given certain assumptions, beyond simple correlations. CAUSEC formalizes the assumptions of the SAST tool into the abstraction of a security assumption and combines assumption-driven causal modeling with effect estimation and validation to test its validity and investigate the factors affecting it. To understand what security assumptions generally entail, we perform a systematic literature review of SASTs that detect crypto-API misuse, leading to the discovery and qualitative analysis of 57 assumptions. We then demonstrate the utility and robustness of CAUSEC by testing a popular assumption in four highly relevant tools, using a manually labeled ground truth dataset consisting of 57,038 alerts. Our analysis leads to several key findings that represent insights regarding assumptions and causal effects, which we distill into 3 takeaways for future work.

</details>


### [169] [Who Can Make the Action Happen? An Authority-Decomposition Framework for High-Risk Automated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18965)
*Mengting Wu,Lin Wang,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向动作（action-relative）的方法，用于推导哪些信任域联盟足以导致受保护执行（受保护状态转换的发生）。该方法建模组件、权限、资源、边界和备选实现结构，涵盖更新、恢复、覆盖、禁用和备选调用等机制，并区分对执行的控制与对操作权威记录的控制。通过跨域分析案例验证，在Havenlon协议模型中，普通见证需要五个信任域，而证书替换产生一个三域的最小充分需求集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统标签（如"授权"、"批准"、"特权"、"受保护"）无法回答一个基本因果问题：哪些行动者实际上能够使某个后果性动作发生？需要一种能揭示真实因果控制关系的方法。

Method: 提出动作相对（action-relative）的信任域联盟分析方法：建模组件、权力、资源、边界和备选实现结构；纳入更新、恢复、覆盖、禁用和备选调用机制；区分执行控制与操作权威记录控制；推导包含性最小充分联盟，并测试声明的执行边界是否独立于上游指定域。

Result: 在Havenlon协议模型（分裂控制、发布意图、开放状态、源受限）中：普通见证需要五个信任域；证书替换产生一个三域的包含性最小已知需求集；Linux域对完整转换不充分。部署层面的全局不可绕性和边界否决覆盖仍未解决。

Conclusion: 该框架是概念与分析工具，不认证实现、不确立部署安全、不保证完全发现隐藏权力，也不定义证据验证语义。其价值在于提供一种系统化方法回答"谁能真正导致受保护动作发生"这一因果问题。

Abstract: High-risk automated systems distribute control across services, credentials, protected components, and lifecycle mechanisms.
