Labels such as authorized, approved, privileged, or protected therefore do not answer a basic causal question: which actors can actually make a consequential action occur? This paper provides an action-relative method for deriving which trust-domain coalitions are sufficient to cause protected execution, defined as the occurrence of a designated protected state transition. The framework models components, powers, resources, boundaries, and alternative realization structures; includes update, recovery, override, disablement, and alternative invocation; and separates causal control over execution from control over the authoritative account of an operation. It derives inclusion-minimal sufficient coalitions and tests whether claimed execution boundaries remain independent of designated upstream domains. Cross-domain analytical cases illustrate the method. In a split-control, release-intended, open-state, source-bounded Havenlon protocol model, the ordinary witness requires five trust domains, while certificate replacement yields a three-domain inclusion-minimal known requirement set among source-enumerated protocol witnesses; the Linux domain remains insufficient for the complete transition. Deployed global non-bypassability and boundary-bound veto coverage remain unresolved. The framework is a conceptual and analytical tool. It does not certify implementations, establish deployment security, guarantee complete discovery of hidden powers, or define evidence-verification semantics.

</details>


### [170] [Catastrophic Learning: A New Attack Vector on Continual Learning Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18976)
*Benedikt Kluss,Niklas Bunzel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种针对持续学习（CL）的全新安全漏洞——"学习阻断器"（learning blockers），即攻击者通过操纵数据降低模型当前或未来迭代的可学习性，攻击CL的塑性而非稳定性。作者提出了六种攻击策略（标签交换、张量交换、吸引-同时、吸引-前置、排斥-同时、排斥-前置），在MNIST和Split-CIFAR10上针对三种CL策略进行了超过4480次仿真实验，验证了该攻击能同时削弱新知识获取并加剧灾难性遗忘，实现"灾难性学习"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对持续学习的对抗研究主要聚焦于重新触发灾难性遗忘（攻击稳定性），而忽略了攻击塑性（可学习性）这一维度。作者识别出被忽视的安全缺陷：攻击者可以通过数据操纵降低当前或后续迭代的学习能力，且这种攻击难以在训练当前迭代时被检测到。

Method: 作者形式化了学习阻断器攻击场景，定义了威胁模型，并提出六种攻击策略：（1）Label-Exchange和Tensor-Exchange；（2）Attraction-Coincident/Preceding变体——最小化毒化与受害迭代之间的损失，在特征空间中拉近表示；（3）Repulsion-Coincident/Preceding变体——最大化损失以推开表示，使稳定性机制抵抗参数变化。Coincident变体中毒化与受害迭代重合，仅用干净参考迭代作为标签源；Preceding变体中毒化迭代先于受害迭代，通过扭曲表示使其不可学习。

Result: 在MNIST和Split-CIFAR10数据集上，针对DER、ER-ACE和iCaRL三种CL策略，超过4480次仿真实验表明：攻击者可以强有力地选择性地阻碍塑性，阻止新知识获取，同时促进旧知识遗忘，成功诱导灾难性学习。

Conclusion: 持续学习系统存在严重的塑性攻击漏洞，攻击者不仅能使模型遗忘旧知识，还能阻止其学习新知识。这种"灾难性学习"场景揭示了CL安全研究的盲区，提示未来需要在防御策略上同时考虑稳定性和塑性两个维度。

Abstract: Continual Learning (CL) enables deep learning models to iteratively learn from a stream of data without forgetting prior knowledge. Existing adversarial research on CL primarily aims to re-enable catastrophic forgetting, attacking stability and reducing availability. We identify a novel security flaw: data manipulated by an attacker can reduce the learnability of current or upcoming iterations. We term such manipulations learning blockers, as they attack the plasticity of CL algorithms. They are particularly harmful because they are difficult to detect during training of the current iteration, since they can target iterations whose data the model has not yet encountered. When learning blockers additionally induce catastrophic forgetting, the resulting overall degradation is what we call catastrophic learning. We formalize this scenario, define a threat model and propose six attack strategies: Label-Exchange, Tensor-Exchange, Attraction-Coincident, Attraction-Preceding, Repulsion-Coincident, and Repulsion-Preceding. The Attraction variants minimize the loss between the poisoned and the victim iteration label, pulling their representations together in feature space; the Repulsion variants maximize this loss, pushing them apart so stability mechanisms resist the required parameter shift. In the Coincident variants, the poisoned and the victim iteration coincide, using a clean reference iteration only as a label source; in the Preceding variants, the poisoned iteration precedes the victim, leaving it unlearnable due to distorted representations. We evaluate on MNIST and Split-CIFAR10 against three CL strategies - DER, ER-ACE, and iCaRL - across more than 4,480 simulations. Our results demonstrate a strong vulnerability: an adversary can selectively impede plasticity to hinder the acquisition of new knowledge, while promoting loss of prior knowledge, inducing a catastrophic learning scenario.

</details>


### [171] [SiNMULI: Novel Signed Network Approach for Malicious URL Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19190)
*Avijit Gayen,Sayan Mondal,Angshuman Jana*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SiNMULI，一种基于符号网络的恶意URL识别方法。该方法将恶意URL检测建模为基于符号网络的二分类问题，利用回链构建符号网络，并结合社交平衡理论通过51%多数规则传播边符号并分类未标记域名。在真实数据集上达到99.89%的准确率、99.62%的精确率和99.80%的F1分数，优于传统机器学习和深度学习基线模型，同时具备可解释性、对抗鲁棒性和无需训练数据的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习、深度学习和伪造网站检测方法主要依赖静态数据分析，面对不断演变的恶意在线实体时效果不佳，需要更有效的方法来识别恶意URL。

Method: 提出SiNMULI框架，将恶意URL识别概念化为基于符号网络的二分类问题。基于URL的外部超链接（回链）构建符号网络，节点表示URL，超链接作为带符号的边；利用基于平衡理论的推理机制在边上传播符号，并通过51%多数规则对入链进行分类未标记域名。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上，SiNMULI达到99.89%的准确率、99.62%的精确率和99.80%的F1分数，超越传统机器学习和深度学习基线模型。

Conclusion: SiNMULI不仅具有高精度，还具备可解释性、对抗混淆鲁棒性和独立于训练数据的特性，是轻量级且可扩展的真实世界网络防御方案。

Abstract: In today's era of rapid advancements in artificial intelligence, computer security and online safeguarding measures have undergone significant improvements. However, malicious websites continue to facilitate the spread of phishing schemes, fraudulent activities and unsolicited communications. Conventional methodologies in machine learning, deep learning and counterfeit website detection predominantly depend on static data analysis, which frequently proves ineffective against the evolving nature of malicious online entities. In response to these challenges, in this work, we propose a signed network-based approach for malicious URL identification, SiNMULI. We introduce an innovative framework that conceptualises the identification of harmful URLs as a signed network-based binary classification problem strongly rooted in the fundamental principles of social network analysis and social balance theory. In this approach, a signed network is constructed based on the backlinks, i.e., external hyperlinks of URLs, wherein each node symbolises a URL and the hyperlinks function as signed edges. Utilising a balance-theoretic inference mechanism, our methodology propagates edge signs and classifies unlabeled domains by employing a 51% majority rule across incoming links. Experimental results on this real-world dataset demonstrate that SiNMULI achieves 99.89% accuracy, 99.62% precision, and 99.80% F1-score, outperforming traditional ML and deep learning baseline models. Beyond high accuracy, SiNMULI offers interpretability, resilience against adversarial obfuscation, and independence from training data, making it a lightweight and scalable solution for real-world cyber defence.

</details>


### [172] [A 12-Step Process for Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18991)
*Victor Kebande*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专门针对工业物联网（IIoT）环境的十二步取证流程（TSP），旨在解决传统数字取证方法在IIoT复杂、大规模、异构环境中面临的挑战，确保潜在数字证据（PDE）的收集、分析和法律合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着IIoT在制造业、医疗保健和交通运输等关键基础设施领域的广泛应用，传统数字取证方法已无法有效应对IIoT环境的复杂性、规模性和异构性，亟需一套专门定制的取证流程。

Method: 提出一个全面的十二步取证流程（TSP），从取证准备阶段到调查结束，每个步骤都针对IIoT环境设计，涵盖PDE的收集、分析及法律合规要求，以提升数字取证场景中的证据可采性。

Result: 该研究分析了IIoT在工业生态系统中的重要性及现有数字取证挑战，并设计了一个完整的十二步流程来确保证据收集的稳健性、分析的准确性以及法律合规性，提高证据在法庭上的可采性几率。

Conclusion: 所提出的TSP为IIoT事件调查提供了一套系统化、结构化的取证方法，能够有效应对动态IIoT环境中的数字取证挑战，增强取证结果的法律有效性。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of the Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) in critical infrastructure sectors like manufacturing, healthcare, and transportation has shown new challenges for Digital Forensics (DF). Traditional DF methodologies are not well equipped to handle the complexity, scale, and heterogeneity of IIoT environments. This paper introduces a comprehensive Twelve-Step Process (TSP) tailored specifically for IIoT incidents, addressing the need for effective investigation and Potential Digital Evidence (PDE) handling in such dynamic environments in DF. We begin by exploring the importance of IIoT and its role in industrial ecosystems, followed by an examination of existing DF challenges. Each step of the process, from forensic readiness to investigation closure, is designed to ensure robust PDE collection, analysis, and legal compliance to increase chances of admissibility from a DF scenario

</details>


### [173] [From Threat Intelligence to Detection: Knowledge-driven Enrichment and Template-based Rule Grounding for Automated Sigma Rule Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19011)
*Sepehr Ghaffarzadegan,Boubakr Nour,Makan Pourzandi,Mourad Debbabi,Chadi Assi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AUTOSIGMA，一个自动将非结构化网络威胁情报(CTI)报告转化为可操作的Sigma规则的自动化解决方案。它结合知识库驱动的内容增强、基于模板的规则落地和多阶段验证机制，比现有方案和纯LLM方法在规则有效性、相关性、MITRE ATT&CK技术覆盖率和鲁棒性方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级持续性威胁(APT)快速演变，需要将CTI动态转化为可操作的检测能力。传统人工编写Sigma规则容易出错且需要大量专业知识，限制了可扩展性；现有Sigma规则库难以跟上新兴威胁，且需要针对不同运营环境频繁定制。

Method: AUTOSIGMA采用多组件架构：(1)利用结构化知识库丰富不完整的输入信息；(2)将增强后的内容与现有Sigma规则库进行匹配；(3)采用LLM-as-a-Judge机制迭代验证生成的规则。综合了知识驱动的增强、基于模板的规则落地和多阶段解决方案。

Result: 在多个真实世界APT报告和多个安全博客上的评估表明，AUTOSIGMA在规则有效性、规则相关性、MITRE ATT&CK技术覆盖率以及对输入质量的鲁棒性方面均优于替代方案和纯LLM模型。

Conclusion: AUTOSIGMA通过结合知识库增强、模板落地和LLM验证的多阶段方法，实现了准确、上下文感知且高度相关的Sigma规则自动生成，有效解决了动态威胁场景下规则生成的时效性和定制化问题。

Abstract: Mechanisms for dynamically converting cyber threat intelligence (CTI) into actionable detection capabilities are necessary due to the rapid evolution of Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs). Sigma rules are an essential part of contemporary threat detection workflows because they offer a platform-independent framework for expressing detection logic that can be converted into particular queries across SIEM systems. Conventional techniques for manually crafting Sigma rules are prone to mistakes, and necessitate extensive knowledge, which restricts their scalability. Although there are open-source and industry-maintained Sigma rule repositories, they often fail to keep pace with emerging threats and require frequent customization to fit diverse operational environments. This emphasizes the necessity of dynamic rule generation that is adapted to evolving attack techniques as well as particular use cases. In this work, we design AUTOSIGMA, an automated solution for transforming unstructured CTI reports into relevant Sigma rules. Rather than relying solely on language models, AUTOSIGMA leverages a structured knowledge base to enrich partial inputs, matches the enriched content against a repository of existing Sigma rules, and then employs an LLM-as-a-Judge mechanism to iteratively validate the rules. By combining knowledge-driven enrichment, template-based rule grounding, and a multi-stage solution, AUTOSIGMA enables accurate, context-aware, and relevant rule generation. Evaluations across multiple real-world APT reports and multiple security blogs demonstrate that AUTOSIGMA outperforms alternative solutions and LLM models in rule validity, rule relevancy, MITRE ATT&CK technique coverage, and robustness to input quality.
  AUTOSIGMA's Demo: https://youtu.be/iSr6IurQ6BM

</details>


### [174] [Malformer: A Multi-Modal Malware Detector Using Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19052)
*Samuel Howard,Kshitiz Aryal,Mahmoud Abdelsalam,Maanak Gupta,Andrew Wheeler,Pradip Kunwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Malformer，一个四模态Windows恶意软件检测模型，结合文本、图像、图结构和音频四种表示形式，通过多模态transformer融合技术，在201,549个样本上达到98.3%的准确率和0.9833的F1分数，显著优于单模态和双模态检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统恶意软件检测系统依赖单一模态表示，无法充分提供判别性信息以识别新型威胁；此外，单一表示（如反汇编依赖）引入新的失败模式，影响了检测的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Method: Malformer采用四模态融合架构：两个RoBERTa编码器分别处理文本和（可能）图结构数据，修改版Vision Transformer处理图像模态，WavLM处理音频模态，并通过自适应损失加权机制融合各模态特定表征，实现对Windows可执行文件的综合检测。

Result: 在包含201,549个二进制样本的数据集上，Malformer达到98.3%的准确率和0.9833的F1分数，相比单模态基线和双模态检测器提升了4.6-17.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 多模态融合为应对日益增长的恶意软件威胁提供了有前景的解决方案，能够为防御者提供更通用和更具韧性的检测能力。

Abstract: Traditional malware detection systems that rely on a single representation of malware often fail to identify novel threats. These representations of malware binaries, also known as modalities, do not provide the models with sufficient information to discriminate among all samples. Additionally, individual representations introduce new failure modes, with some modality extraction being dependent upon the success of disassembling. Past works have integrated either additional modalities or more discriminative representations for classification. In this work, we present Malformer, a quadrimodal malware detection model that incorporates text, image, graph, and audio representations of Windows executables. We demonstrate that multimodal transformer fusion can enhance the performance of Windows malware detectors over that of unimodal and bimodal detectors. Malformer employs a combination of two RoBERTa encoders paired with a modified Vision Transformer for image data, WavLM for audio data, and an adaptive loss-weighting scheme to fuse modality-specific representations. Evaluated on a dataset of 201,549 binary samples, Malformer achieved 98.3% accuracy and an F1 score of 0.9833, outperforming both unimodal baselines and bimodal detectors by 4.6-17.6 percentage points. Malformer demonstrates that multimodal fusion provides a promising foundation for countering the growing scale of malware threats, equipping defenders with generalized and resilient detection capabilities.

</details>


### [175] [Toward Quantum Advantage in Learning Parities with Structured Noise via Lower Bound Optimization of the Condition Number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19122)
*Yusen Han,Xuelian Li,Juntao Gao,Bo Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Macaulay线性系统的新型约简方法，用于求解带结构噪声的奇偶性学习问题（LPSN）。通过推导包含缩放因子的条件数下界，该方法优化了量子算法的条件数区间，降低了样本复杂度，并提供了具体的量子资源估计，证明在特定参数区间内可优于经典算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算中求解非线性布尔系统通常需转化为Macaulay线性系统并通过量子线性系统算法求解，但条件数严重限制了算法性能。现有方法缺乏有效的条件数优化手段，亟需一种能够降低条件数下界、提升量子算法效率的新方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的Macaulay线性系统约简方法。在Ding等人的假设下，推导出包含缩放因子的条件数下界；该约简保证了高效的量子态制备，并通过优化条件数区间相对于约简后右端向量的关系，降低条件数下界；利用Macaulay系统的解结构降低样本复杂度，同时提供逻辑层面的量子资源估计。

Result: 优化后的量子算法在条件数方面展现出明显优势，降低了条件数下界并优化了时间复杂度的上界；样本复杂度显著降低；电路宽度、深度和门数量均得到缩减；通过与经典算法的系统对比，在特定参数区间内量子算法展现出超越经典算法的潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究通过条件数优化有效提升了量子算法求解LPSN问题的性能，在样本复杂度和时间复杂度上均取得改进，并为量子与经典算法的选择提供了系统性的策略指导，证明了量子算法在特定参数条件下具有实际优势。

Abstract: Learning Parities with Structured Noise (LPSN) can be reduced to solving nonlinear Boolean systems. In quantum computing, such systems are typically transformed into Macaulay linear systems and solved via quantum linear system algorithms, a process severely limited by the condition number. To address this, we propose a novel reduction method for Macaulay linear systems. Under the assumptions of Ding et al., we derive a condition number lower bound incorporating a scaling factor.
  This reduction not only guarantees efficient quantum state preparation but also exhibits a distinct advantage regarding the condition number interval relative to the reduced right-hand side vector, thereby reducing the lower bound of the condition number and ultimately optimizing the upper bound on the time complexity of the quantum algorithm for solving Boolean systems.
  Furthermore, applying this improved quantum algorithm to LPSN significantly reduces sample complexity by exploiting the Macaulay system's solution structure. We further provide a concrete logical-level quantum resource estimate, demonstrating that the optimized condition number translates directly into a reduction in circuit width, depth, and gate count. Finally, we establish an algorithm selection strategy by systematically comparing quantum and classical approaches across noise pattern adaptability, sample complexity, and time complexity. Results demonstrate that our quantum algorithm exhibits the potential to outperform classical counterparts under specific parameter regimes.

</details>


### [176] [FedGuard-DC: Privacy-Preserving Federated Load Forecasting and Cyber-Attack Detection for Data-Center Loads in Transmission Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19155)
*Md Kibria Saroare,Md Rubel Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FedGuard-DC，一个基于联邦学习的隐私保护数据中心负荷预测与虚假数据注入攻击检测框架。每个数据中心使用双头模型（共享编码器+预测头+重建头）在本地训练，原始数据不外传，仅共享模型更新，并可选差分隐私和鲁棒聚合机制。在IEEE 39节点系统中四个150-350MW数据中心负荷的EMT仿真数据上验证，0.5秒前向预测RMSE为0.023-0.038pu，FDIA检测ROC-AUC达0.979。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型数据中心负荷快速增长给电力系统可视性、隐私和网络物理安全带来挑战。系统运营商需要准确的短期负荷信息，但数据中心运营商可能不愿共享原始MW级测量数据，因为这会泄露敏感的工作负载和利用率模式，因此需要一种既能保护隐私又能实现准确预测和攻击检测的框架。

Method: 采用联邦学习框架，每个数据中心在本地训练一个双头模型：共享编码器同时支撑预测头和重建头。结合预测残差和重建误差构建校准的异常分数以检测本地数据损坏。原始测量值和绝对MW需求保留在本地，仅共享模型更新给全局控制器。可选差分隐私和鲁棒修剪均值聚合用于评估隐私-效用行为和投毒客户端鲁棒性。

Result: 在IEEE 39节点系统中四个150-350MW数据中心负荷的EMT仿真数据验证中，0.5秒前向归一化预测RMSE为0.023-0.038pu，远优于持久性方法的0.32-0.34pu。FDIA检测达到ROC-AUC 0.979、F1=0.930、精确率0.988。鲁棒聚合将投毒客户端RMSE影响从0.042降至0.035pu。

Conclusion: FedGuard-DC框架有效平衡了数据中心负荷预测的准确性、原始数据隐私保护和虚假数据注入攻击检测能力，通过联邦学习和双重任务模型设计，在保护敏感数据的同时实现了优异的预测性能和攻击检测性能。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large data-center (DC) loads is creating new challenges for power-system visibility, privacy, and cyber-physical security. System operators need accurate short-term information about these fast-varying loads, while DC operators may avoid sharing raw megawatt measurements because they can reveal sensitive workload and utilization patterns. This paper presents FedGuard-DC, a federated learning (FL) framework for privacy-preserving DC load forecasting and local false-data-injection attack (FDIA) detection. Each DC trains a dual-head model on its own measurements, where a shared encoder supports both a forecasting head and a reconstruction head. A calibrated anomaly score combines forecast residual and reconstruction error to detect corrupted measurements locally. Raw measurements and absolute MW demand remain at each DC, while only model updates are shared with the global controller. Optional differential privacy and robust trimmed-mean aggregation are included to evaluate privacy-utility behavior and poisoned-client resilience. The framework is validated using EMT simulation data from four large DC loads rated between 150 and 350 MW integrated into the IEEE 39-bus New England system. Results show a 0.5 s-ahead normalized forecast RMSE of 0.023-0.038 pu, compared with 0.32-0.34 pu for persistence. FedGuard-DC detects FDIA with ROC-AUC of 0.979, F1 = 0.930, and precision of 0.988, while robust aggregation reduces the poisoned-client RMSE impact from 0.042 to 0.035 pu.

</details>


### [177] [The Structured Totient Preimage Problem: Reconstruction, Collisions, and Cryptographic Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19191)
*Luis Adrián Lizama-Pérez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文定义并研究了结构化欧拉函数原像（STP）问题，这是一个受密码学动机启发的受限重建关系。论文给出了三个具体结果：因子分解情形下的精确计数与多项式重建边界、穷举重建和碰撞分析算法、以及28个参数对的全面实验评估。基于这些发现，论文提出了结构化欧拉函数原像假设（STP假设），并探讨了其在承诺、知识证明和认证等密码学构造中的应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧拉函数求逆问题（inverse-totient problem）在密码学中具有潜在应用价值，但一般性问题过于困难且缺乏精细的结构分析。本文旨在通过引入结构化限制（已知乘积和参数数量k），将STP问题与一般逆欧拉函数计算区分开来，从而为构造原像抵抗（preimage-resistant）的密码学关系奠定基础。

Method: 论文采用理论分析与实验验证相结合的方法：(1) 对因子分解的x推导有序指数分配的精确计数，并给出固定k时直接重建为多项式时间的界；(2) 设计穷举算法进行重建和碰撞分析；(3) 对28个参数对（2≤k≤5，λ≤16）进行大规模穷举实验，最大普查包含4,588,935个素数集合，量化碰撞参与率、最大重数和条件歧义度等非单射性指标。

Result: 研究结果表明：当k固定且Ω(x)=O(log λ)时，直接重建是多项式时间可解的，排除该参数域作为强硬度主张的基础；实验数据系统量化了STP关系的非单射性特征。基于这些结果，论文将STP与一般逆欧拉函数计算区分开来，并提出结构化欧拉函数原像假设（STP假设）作为显式增长参数族下的候选困难性假设。

Conclusion: 论文建立了STP问题的计算基础和参数约束框架，为在STP假设下构造承诺方案、乘法见证的知识证明和认证协议提供了前提条件。但需要明确的是，本文并未提供安全归约或后量子硬度证明，这些仍需未来研究补充。

Abstract: We define and study the Structured Totient Preimage (STP) problem as a restricted reconstruction relation with a direct cryptographic motivation. Let $p_1,\ldots,p_k$ be distinct primes of the same bit length and reveal only $x=\prod_{i=1}^k(p_i-1)$. Given $(x,λ,k)$, STP asks for any set of $k$ distinct $λ$-bit primes satisfying this product. The relation is efficiently verifiable, but its reconstruction complexity is not known. We establish three concrete results. First, for factored $x$ we derive the exact number of ordered exponent allocations and a bound showing that direct reconstruction is polynomial for fixed $k$ when $Ω(x)=O(\logλ)$; this rules out that regime as a basis for a strong hardness claim. Second, we give exhaustive algorithms for reconstruction and collision analysis. Third, we exhaustively evaluate 28 parameter pairs, with $2\leq k\leq5$, up to $λ=16$ for pairs and 4,588,935 prime sets in the largest census. The data quantify non-injectivity through collision participation, maximum multiplicity, and conditional ambiguity in bits. These results isolate STP from general inverse-totient computation and motivate a Structured Totient Preimage Assumption for explicitly growing parameter families. Under such an assumption, STP becomes a candidate preimage-resistant relation whose implications for commitments, proofs of knowledge of multiplicative witnesses, and authentication can be stated precisely. The paper establishes the computational foundation and parameter constraints for those constructions; it does not claim a security reduction or post-quantum hardness.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Position: Collusion Risks Among AI Reasoning Agents Justify Certification Requirements for Making Market Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18078)
*Matthew Riemer,Tommaso Tosato,Amin Memarian,Maximilian Puelma Touzel,Glen Berseth,Irina Rish,Guillaume Dumas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张具有思维链推理能力的AI代理天然倾向于合谋行为，在影响经济市场决策前须通过行为认证。实验表明DeepSeek-R1在Bertrand寡头定价中表现出隐性合谋倾向，且其思维链可被引导至极端合谋或竞争行为而无法被LLM语义检测，因此需要基于行为观察的认证机制来防止合谋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具备思维链推理能力的AI代理逐步融入经济市场，其可能通过与人类同等的推理机制产生隐性合谋行为，但现行法律体系中"竞争与合谋"的证据区分标准可能因此失效。作者旨在揭示这一风险并呼吁建立行为认证机制。

Method: 以DeepSeek-R1代理在Bertrand寡头定价领域进行实验，测试其在有/无人类提示"不得合谋"条件下的定价行为；同时研究思维链推理轨迹是否可被引导至合谋或竞争方向，并使用另一个LLM分析推理痕迹以检测其是否可被语义识别。

Result: DeepSeek-R1代理表现出持续的隐性合谋倾向，即使人类明确提示不得合谋也无法消除；思维链可被引导至极端合谋或高度竞争行为，且这种引导无法被另一个LLM通过语义分析检测到；初步证据表明代理可被引导至有效的竞争均衡。

Conclusion: 在将推理型AI代理部署到真实市场前，必须建立基于代表性情境下行为观察的全面行为认证机制，以防止合谋性经济结果而不损害法律对竞争的区分标准，从而确保市场稳定与效率。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI agents with chain-of-thought reasoning capabilities are predisposed to exhibit collusive behavior and should be required to obtain behavioral certification before making decisions that affect economic markets. This is because integrating these agents into society could collapse the legal evidentiary distinction between competition and collusion among independent firms without eroding the economic harm distinction. Experiments with DeepSeek-R1 agents in the Bertrand oligopoly pricing domain reveal a tendency towards tacit collusion that persists even when humans prompt the agents not to collude. We further show that the chain-of-thought of these agents can be steered toward either extremely collusive or highly competitive behavior in a way that is not semantically detectable by another LLM analyzing the reasoning traces. As a result, deploying reasoning agents for market decisions leads to collusive economic outcomes without any evidence of conspiracy or intent. Thus, certification based on observed behavior in representative situations is necessary to prevent collusion. We provide preliminary evidence that such agents can be steered in a generalizable way toward efficient competitive equilibria. However, developing a comprehensive behavioral certification will be required before these models can be deployed in real-world markets while ensuring their stability and efficiency.

</details>


### [179] [Position: Profiling Game Worlds by Transition Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18079)
*Lele Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了过渡复杂度画像（TCP），这是一组可复现的指标，用于量化游戏世界建模（GWM）和强化学习（RL）环境中的转移预测难度，涵盖单步分支、交互不确定性/对手影响和时空依赖范围，并呼吁将其作为标准基准元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GWM和RL论文中很少量化在声明接口（像素/令牌/潜在表征+有限历史）下转移预测问题的实际难度，导致该领域被混淆。

Method: 提出TCP指标集，从三个维度刻画环境的诱导转移核：(i)固有单步分支度，(ii)交互引致的不确定性和可观测时的对手影响，(iii)通过标准化探测曲线测量的时间/空间依赖跨度。同时规定显式参考分布、协议随机性和版本化测量预算。

Result: 概述了常见游戏家族和现代神经游戏引擎领域在该景观中的分布，展示了TCP如何在不同基准间产生可比较的数值。

Conclusion: 呼吁TCP成为GWM和RL论文的标准基准元数据和必需统计量，以提高研究的可复现性和可比性。

Abstract: Game world modeling (GWM) and reinforcement learning (RL) are often confounded because research papers rarely quantify how difficult the underlying transition prediction problem is at the declared interface (pixels/tokens/latents with finite history). We propose the Transition Complexity Profile (TCP): a small, reproducible set of metrics that characterizes an environment's (or gameplay dataset's) induced transition kernel by (i) intrinsic one-step branching, (ii) interaction-induced uncertainty and opponent influence when observable, and (iii) temporal/spatial dependency span via standardized probe curves. TCP is reported with an explicit reference distribution, protocol stochasticity, and a versioned measurement budget (sampling/resampling and fixed probe compute), enabling comparable numbers across benchmarks. We outline how common game families and modern "neural game engine" domains populate this landscape and call for TCP to become standard benchmark metadata and a required statistic in GWM and RL papers.

</details>


### [180] [Large Language Models in Mental Health: A Systematic Review of Applications, Innovations, and Ethical Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18080)
*Yisong Chen,Yifan Gao,Sijing Yu,Chuqing Zhao,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型（LLMs）在心理健康领域的应用，涵盖社交媒体分析、临床对话系统、治疗支持工具、提示工程、多模态学习及伦理考量等方面，强调了其在高风险医疗部署中的机遇与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs在各领域的快速发展，其在心理健康领域的应用潜力巨大，但相关研究分散且缺乏系统性梳理，亟需一份综合性的综述来整合跨学科发现，明确当前进展、技术方法以及伦理监管方面的缺口。

Method: 整合跨学科研究，利用多种数据源（如社交媒体帖子、电子病历和多模态输入），围绕LLMs在心理健康中的应用进行系统性综述，重点涵盖模型与标注策略的进步、提示工程的作用以及多模态融合技术的讨论。

Result: 综述指出了LLMs在早期抑郁检测、自杀风险评估、个性化治疗支持和心理教育内容生成等方面的显著进展，同时强调了提示工程对领域适应性的关键作用，并识别了多模态融合技术用于改善精神健康诊断与监测的潜力。

Conclusion: 作者主张建立安全、公平且负责任的框架，以应对伦理、社会技术及监管挑战，确保LLMs在实际精神健康护理中的可靠部署。

Abstract: We present a review on the applications of large language models (LLMs) in health, e.g., social media analysis, clinical conversational agents, therapy support tools, prompt engineering, multimodal learning, and ethical considerations. We integrate findings from interdisciplinary studies utilizing diverse data sources such as social media posts, electronic medical records, and multimodal inputs to enable early detection of depression, suicide risk assessment, personalized therapy support, and psychoeducational content generation. Our review highlights advancements in LLM models and annotation strategies that enhance interpretability and clinical relevance, while we also emphasize the critical role of prompt engineering for domain adaptation. We also discuss emerging multimodal fusion techniques integrating text, speech, and sensor data for improved mental health diagnosis and monitoring. Finally, we address ongoing ethical, sociotechnical, and regulatory challenges, and advocate frameworks to ensure safe, equitable, and accountable deployment of LLMs in real-world mental health care.

</details>


### [181] [Position: Behavioral Systems Require Behavioral Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18081)
*Manuel Cherep,Nikhil Singh,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张应将AI智能体视为行为系统进行评估，借鉴行为科学的系统观察、扰动和解释方法，并提出一套研究议程来发展严格的AI行为测试，包括从行动序列中恢复决策策略、构建隔离行为差异的环境、以及探索多智能体系统中的涌现动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体评估方法主要关注性能结果，而忽视了产生这些结果的行为过程本身。随着智能体日益作为行为系统与动态环境交互、追求目标并随时间适应，仅评估结果无法深入理解其行为机制。

Method: 借鉴行为科学的经验，提出三类研究方法：(1) 从行动序列中恢复决策策略；(2) 构建能隔离行为差异的实验环境；(3) 探测多智能体系统中的涌现动力学。

Result: 文章提出了一条发展"AI行为科学"的研究路线图，为系统化评估AI智能体的行为过程提供了方向性框架。

Conclusion: AI智能体的评估需要从单纯的结果导向转向过程导向，通过系统性观察、扰动和解释其行为，建立科学的AI行为评估范式。

Abstract: Artificial agentic systems increasingly operate as behavioral systems by interacting with dynamic environments, pursuing goals, and adapting over time. Yet, current evaluation methods largely focus on performance outcomes, not the underlying behavioral processes that produce them. This paper argues that AI agents must be evaluated like other behavioral systems: through systematic observation, perturbation, and interpretation of their actions. We draw on lessons from the behavioral sciences to motivate this position, and propose a research agenda focused on developing rigorous behavioral tests. These include methods for recovering decision strategies from action sequences, constructing environments that isolate behavioral differences, and probing emergent dynamics in multi-agent systems. Taken together, these directions offer a roadmap for developing a science of AI behavior.

</details>


### [182] [Position: Current Model Cards Are Insufficient for Downstream Governance of Open-Weight Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18086)
*Sungwon Chae,Keonwoo Kim,Hoki Kim,Jaeyeon Ju,Sangchul Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了Hugging Face上500个模型卡片，指出开放权重基础模型（OWFM）的下游治理需要模型卡片、可接受使用政策（AUP）和许可证三层互补的集成方法，并指出现有开源许可证不适用于OWFM，可能削弱AUP的可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型卡片虽被广泛用作透明度工具，但未能充分告知下游开发者关于OWFM特有的安全挑战；同时标准开源许可证也不适合OWFM的特性，存在治理缺口。

Method: 作为立场论文，系统分析了Hugging Face上500个模型卡片，从模型血统、对齐溯源和实证观察行为三个维度识别现有监管方法的安全缺口，进而论证三层治理框架的必要性。

Result: 通过分析发现现有监管方法存在安全缺口，标准开源许可证不适合OWFM并可能削弱AUP的可执行性，从而支撑了三层（模型卡片+AUP+许可证）综合治理框架的主张。

Conclusion: 模型卡片、AUP和许可证应演变为集成的安全工件，将信息、规范和法律的三个维度连贯地整合到更全面的治理框架中。

Abstract: The growth of open-weight foundation models (OWFMs) has prompted the AI community to re-evaluate strategies for effective downstream governance. Although model cards have been widely adopted as transparency artifacts in model repositories, existing frameworks often fail to adequately inform downstream developers and users about the distinct safety challenges posed by OWFMs. This position paper analyzes 500 model cards hosted on Hugging Face and argues that effective governance of OWFMs requires a multi-layered approach integrating three complementary components: (i) model cards, (ii) acceptable use policies (AUPs), and (iii) licenses. To motivate this claim, we identify a safety gap left by existing regulatory approaches, including model heritage, alignment provenance, and empirically observed behaviors, through an analysis of model cards with safety-critical information. We further argue that standard open-source licenses (OSLs) are not well suited for OWFMs and may weaken the enforceability of AUPs. Building on these observations, we outline directions for evolving model cards, AUPs, and licenses into integrated safety artifacts to enable a more comprehensive governance framework that coherently integrates informational, normative, and legal dimensions.

</details>


### [183] [A Metamorphic Artificial Age Score Decision-Support Prototype for Flight-Log-Based Drone Propeller Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18088)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变形式人工Age Score（AAS）的决策支持原型，用于从飞行日志中监测无人机螺旋桨健康状态。通过从2024年DronePropA公共数据集中提取六个健康指标，采用冗余调整的AAS公式，对健康和三种不同严重程度的螺旋桨故障案例进行回顾性评估，成功区分了不同的故障通道并分配了相应的维护优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机螺旋桨故障对安全性和可靠性的影响通常分散在多个飞行日志通道中，而非表现为单一诊断信号，传统单一指标监测方法难以有效捕捉这种多通道分布式故障特征。

Method: 从MATLAB原始矩阵中计算六个健康指标（轨迹跟踪误差、姿态不稳定性、推力指令负担、电机指令不平衡、ESC指令不稳定、电池电量压力），基于健康基线进行归一化，通过候选评分策略、变式充分性关系和冗余调整的AAS公式进行综合评估。使用一个健康基线和三个缺陷螺旋桨案例进行受控回顾性评估。

Result: 健康案例被分配常规监测；Severity 1案例以ESC指令不稳定为主导，分配维护审查；Severity 2案例达到电机指令和ESC指令负担最大值；Severity 3案例达到最大轨迹跟踪误差；后两者均触发强制检查。实验表明螺旋桨故障效应可通过不同运行通道表现。

Conclusion: 螺旋桨故障需要通过多指标决策支持层进行事后维护优先级划分和自主系统监督，单一指标无法全面捕获分布式故障效应。

Abstract: Drone propeller faults can create safety and reliability risks when their effects are distributed across multiple flight-log channels rather than appearing as a single diagnostic signal. This paper proposes a Metamorphic Artificial Age Score (AAS) decision-support prototype for flight-log-based drone propeller health monitoring. Using selected historical real flight logs from the 2024 DronePropA public dataset, the framework computes six health-related indicators from raw MATLAB matrices: trajectory tracking error, attitude instability, thrust-command burden, motor-command imbalance, ESC-command instability, and battery-level stress. These indicators are normalized relative to a healthy baseline and evaluated through candidate scoring policies, metamorphic adequacy relations, and a redundancy-adjusted AAS formulation. In this context, AAS is used as a structural policy-adequacy and burden measure rather than as a chronological age measure. A controlled retrospective evaluation was performed using one healthy baseline and three defective propeller cases under the same speed profile and trajectory. The healthy case was assigned to routine monitoring. The Severity 1 case was dominated by ESC-command instability and assigned to maintenance review. The Severity 2 case reached maximum motor-command and ESC-command burden, while the Severity 3 case reached maximum trajectory tracking error; both triggered mandatory inspection. The results show that propeller fault effects may appear through different operational channels, supporting the need for a multi-indicator decision-support layer for post-flight maintenance prioritization and autonomous-system oversight.

</details>


### [184] [DentAgent: Evidence-Centric Multi-Agent Coordination for Multimodal Dental Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18878)
*Zijie Meng,Xiwei Dai,Yixuan Tang,Jin Hao,Yang Feng,Fudong Zhu,Xiaoqiang Liu,Shaosheng Cao,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DentAgent，一个以证据为中心的多智能体牙科AI框架。它通过编排器协调五个跨模态专业智能体，利用领域工具将观察结果转换为结构化证据记录，并由证据黑板统一管理，从而实现可追踪、可解释的多模态牙科推理。在四个基准上表现优异，多标签诊断上超过资深专家17.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口腔疾病影响全球数十亿人，需要整合多模态异构证据（影像、照片、3D数据、领域知识）的可靠牙科评估。现有牙科AI系统多为模态或任务专用，而视觉-语言模型虽支持灵活问答，但其直接生成的回答缺乏可追踪的证据支撑。

Method: DentAgent采用多智能体框架：（1）Orchestrator协调五个跨模态专业智能体；（2）每个专业智能体利用领域工具将观察转换为结构化证据记录；（3）Evidence Blackboard作为共享证据状态管理器，跟踪覆盖度、缺口和冲突；（4）标准化证据表示将孤立的牙科能力整合为统一的智能体工作流。

Result: 在四个基准上，DentAgent展现了领先性能，在多标签诊断任务上比资深专家高出17.3个百分点，证明其在多模态牙科推理上的有效性和可追踪性。

Conclusion: DentAgent验证了以证据为中心的智能体框架在口腔多模态推理中的价值，可作为人群口腔健康评估与管理的技术基础。

Abstract: Oral diseases affect billions of people worldwide, underscoring a pressing need for accurate and reliable dental assessment that integrates heterogeneous evidence from domain knowledge, radiographs, intraoral photographs, and 3D dental data. Most existing dental AI systems remain modality- or task-specific. Although recent vision-language models support flexible dental question answering, directly generated response leaves evidence implicit and untraceable. To address these limitations, we introduce DentAgent, an evidence-centric multi-agent framework, in which the Orchestrator coordinate five specialized agents spanning various modalities. Each specialist utilizes domain tools to convert observations into structured evidence records. The Evidence Blackboard manages these records as a shared evidence state, tracking coverage, gaps, and conflicts before response generation. This standardized evidence representation integrates isolated dental capabilities into a unified agentic workflow. Across four benchmarks, DentAgent demonstrates leading performance, even surpassing the senior specialists by 17.3 percentage points on multi-label diagnosis, which supports its value for broadly applicable and traceable multimodal dental reasoning, and highlights its potential as a technical foundation for population oral health assessment and management.

</details>


### [185] [Position: Multi-Agent Systems Should Prioritize Concurrency Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18092)
*Xin Yang,Letian Li,Zimo Ji,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Wenyuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）中的许多失败本质上可归结为并发控制问题。智能体对共享状态的并发读写，加上LLM较长的推理时间窗口，放大了过期读取、丢失更新和不一致性结果的风险。作者主张MAS框架应将并发控制（冲突检测、隔离保证、结构化资源共享）作为一等设计关注点，而非事后补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM多智能体系统承诺可扩展的协作，但随着智能体数量增加，系统可靠性反而下降。作者识别到许多MAS失败被归因于协调或通信问题，但实际根源是并发控制缺陷——智能体并发读写共享状态，而LLM推理窗口较长，加剧了数据竞争风险。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper），通过将MAS中的失败模式映射到经典并发控制异常（如脏读、丢失更新）来分析问题根源，并论证应在MAS框架中引入显式的并发控制机制，包括冲突检测、隔离保证和结构化访问共享资源。

Result: 论文论证了MAS失败与经典并发控制问题之间的对应关系，提出并发控制应成为MAS框架中的一等设计考量而非事后补充。

Conclusion: 并发控制应被提升为MAS框架设计中的首要关注点，通过显式的机制（冲突检测、隔离保证、结构化共享资源访问）来提升多智能体系统的可靠性和可扩展性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) promise scalable collaboration, yet adding agents often reduces reliability. This position paper argues that many MAS failures are fundamentally concurrency control problems: agents concurrently read and write shared state, and long LLM inference windows amplify the risk of stale reads, lost updates, and inconsistent outcomes. Failure modes commonly attributed to coordination or communication breakdowns can be mapped directly onto classical concurrency anomalies. We contend that MAS frameworks should address these failures through explicit concurrency control mechanisms: conflict detection, isolation guarantees, and structured access to shared resources. Concurrency control should be a first-class design concern, not an afterthought.

</details>


### [186] [Adaptive Memory and Reflection Multi-Agent System for Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19029)
*Pradeep Murugesan,Luoxiao Yang,Xueli Chen,Xinqi Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应记忆与反思（AMR）智能体系统，采用多智能体框架，通过专门的记忆和反思反馈机制检索相关历史病例并改进后续推理，在MedQA和MedMCQA基准上取得优于多个基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学问答系统多基于单智能体架构和静态检索，缺乏适应性、持久记忆和结构化决策能力，难以应对需要事实知识和细致推理的复杂病例。

Method: 提出AMR智能体系统：多智能体框架，包含专用记忆和反思反馈机制；按问题复杂度分流到单独、协作或升级工作流；设有共识模块和伦理监督模块以支持推理整合与输出审查。

Result: 在MedQA和MedMCQA数据集上的实验表明性能优于多个基线；消融实验显示结合智能体专用记忆、反思和外部检索可获得最强性能。

Conclusion: 结构化记忆与反馈机制对开发更可信的医学智能体具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Accurate and responsible medical question answering (QA) is important in healthcare, where complex cases require factual knowledge and nuanced reasoning. Existing medical QA systems, typically based on single-agent architectures and static retrieval, often lack adaptability, persistent memory, and structured decision-making. This work introduces an adaptive memory and reflection (AMR) agentic system, a multi-agent framework in which specialized agents use dedicated memory and reflection-based feedback to retrieve relevant prior cases and improve subsequent reasoning. Complexity assessment routes questions through solo, collaborative, or escalated workflows, while consensus and ethical overseer modules support reasoning consolidation and output review. Evaluation on MedQA and MedMCQA demonstrates strong performance compared with several baselines. Ablation studies show that combining agent-specific memory, reflection, and external retrieval yields the strongest performance. These findings highlight the potential of structured memory and feedback for developing more trustworthy medical agents. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/mm-air/AMR-Agent.

</details>


### [187] [FinSkillBench: Evaluating AI Agents and Domain Skills for Investment Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18099)
*Jermyn Zhen Yong Bek,Zhuang Qiang Bok,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FinSkillBench，一个评估语言模型代理在投资管理领域技能使用能力的基准，包含3个领域、12个子任务和2603个任务片段。实验发现：预置技能包能显著提升性能（平均分从0.366提升至0.528），而自我生成技能收益甚微且计算成本更高，跨框架验证结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投资管理是高风险领域，AI代理不仅需要生成合理文本，还需检索时点数据、组装计算输入、调用专业方法并产出可审计的结构化输出。现有评估体系缺乏对金融领域技能有效使用的度量标准，因此需要专门的基准来衡量代理在此类专业任务中的实际能力。

Method: 构建FinSkillBench基准，覆盖投资组合构建、风险管理和基本面分析三个领域，包含12个子任务和2603个任务片段。每个片段提供时点输入、隐藏的真实结果和任务特定验证器。对比三种条件：无技能、预置技能包（包含程序文件文档和可执行组件）、自我生成技能（代理在片段内编写并复用自身程序）。在9个模型上进行大规模评估，并采用独立框架Hermes Agent（8个模型、5280个片段）进行交叉验证。

Result: 预置技能包显著提升所有模型表现，平均分从0.366提升至0.528，其中投资组合构建和风险管理领域增益最大。自我生成技能即使消耗更高计算成本也几乎无增益。独立框架Hermes Agent的评估在3个领域均复现了相同的方向性模式，但技能效应的幅度因子任务和框架而异。

Conclusion: 在投资管理代理中，可靠的程序性技能的获取与模型选择同等重要，而朴素的自我生成技能往往无效。研究发布基准、评估工具、预置技能包和完整轨迹以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Investment management is a high-stakes domain in which agentic AI systems must do more than generate plausible text. They must retrieve point-in-time data, assemble correct computational inputs, invoke specialized methods, and produce auditable structured outputs. We introduce FinSkillBench, an evaluation suite designed to measure whether language model agents can effectively use financial domain skills to solve investment management tasks. The benchmark spans three domains, portfolio construction, risk management, and fundamental analysis, and includes 12 subtasks with 2,603 task episodes.
  Each episode provides point-in-time inputs, hidden ground truth, and a task-specific verifier.We compare three conditions: no skill, curated skill packages consisting of procedural documents and executable components, and self-generated skills in which the agent writes and reuses its own procedures within an episode. Across 9 models and a large-scale evaluation, curated skills consistently improve performance, raising mean scores from 0.366 to 0.528, with the largest gains in portfolio construction and risk management.
  In contrast, self-generated skills provide little benefit despite higher computational cost. An independent evaluation using a separate agent framework (Hermes Agent, 8 models, 5,280 episodes total) reproduces the directional pattern across all three domains, with the magnitude of skill effects varying by subtask and harness.
  These results showthat in investment management agents, access to reliable procedural skills can be as important as model choice, while naive self-generation of skills is often ineffective. We release the benchmark, evaluation tools, curated skill packages, and full trajectories to support further research.

</details>


### [188] [Self-Evolving Agents as Dynamic Graph Transformation: A Survey and New Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18104)
*Yuanyuan Xu,Wenjie Zhang,Yin Chen,Xuemin Lin,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将智能体状态建模为动态图，提出"智能体演化即动态图变换"的视角，并系统梳理了基于动态图方法的自我演化智能体相关研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图智能体综述将图视为支撑结构而非演化基质，而自我演化智能体综述又聚焦于智能体层面机制、很少讨论图拓扑演化。智能体状态与动态图拓扑之间的耦合关系尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将智能体状态建模为动态图（记忆、工具、技能、工作流和智能体间关系表示为带类型的节点、边和子图，通过模式约束重写进行更新）；提出四类分类法（节点/特征演化、边/拓扑演化、子图激活、跨组件协同演化）；将九个动态图学习子领域映射到智能体演化能力；提出五类图感知的评估与治理协议。

Result: 构建了系统性的理论框架和四元分类体系，提出了动态图学习作为自我演化智能体可复用基础设施的观点，并给出了能力映射及可能的失败模式。

Conclusion: 该综述为设计和治理自我演化智能体提供了紧凑的结构化视角，弥补了图智能体研究与自我演化智能体研究之间的空白。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly becoming self-evolving systems that persist across interactions, maintain memories, use tools, acquire skills, refine workflows, and coordinate with other agents. These capabilities make agent states structural and dynamic: entities, relations, attributes, dependencies, and execution structures change with new evidence, feedback, and environmental conditions. Existing graph-agent surveys typically treat graphs as support structures for agent functions rather than as evolving substrates, while self-evolving-agent surveys focus on agent-level mechanisms and rarely discuss graph topology evolution. Thus, the coupling between evolving agent state and dynamic graph topology remains underexplored. This survey connects these two research lines by framing \textit{agent evolution as dynamic graph transformation}. We model agent state as a dynamic graph, where memories, tools, skills, workflows, and inter-agent relations are represented as typed nodes, edges, and subgraphs updated through schema-constrained rewrites. Based on this formulation, we organize existing dynamic-graph-based methods for self-evolving agents into four taxonomies: node/feature evolution, edge/topology evolution, subgraph activation, and cross-component co-evolution. Building on this taxonomy, we propose dynamic graph learning as reusable infrastructure for self-evolving agents and map nine dynamic-graph-learning subfields to agent-evolution capabilities, discussing their adaptations and possible failure modes. Finally, we discuss five types of graph-aware evaluation and governance protocols from a dynamic-graph perspective, which complement end-task evaluation. The goal is to provide a compact structural lens for designing and governing self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [189] [Emergence of Agentic AI: A Review on Evolution, Background, Working Principles, Applications, Adoption Factors, and Future Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18110)
*AKM Bahalul Haque,Al Amin Islam Ridoy,Mohammad Rayhan,Ivan Porres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对智能体人工智能（Agentic AI）进行了一次系统性综述，涵盖其基础原理、历史演变、架构功能、跨领域实际应用、当前研究挑战及未来方向，并提出了一个基于系统质量维度的智能体AI采纳意愿综合框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI正在快速发展并展现出变革各领域的巨大潜力，但学界和业界对其缺乏深入理解。为了帮助研究人员和从业者全面掌握该技术的现状、研究空白与改进空间，亟需一次系统性的综述研究。

Method: 该工作采用系统性综述方法，检索并分析了各领域近期发表的智能体AI学术文献，从多个维度进行梳理：包括智能体AI的基本原理与工作机制、人工智能系统中代理性的历史与理论演变、架构与功能分析、跨领域实际应用案例、研究结果分析以及挑战识别，并提出了一个包含系统质量维度在内的利益相关者采纳与使用智能体AI的综合框架。

Result: 研究系统梳理了智能体AI的当前发展状况与应用，识别了关键研究空白，总结了现有挑战，并提出了未来的研究方向，同时给出了一个综合性的利益相关者采纳框架。

Conclusion: 该综述为研究人员和从业者提供了对智能体AI的全面理解，明确了其研究短板与未来探索空间，为后续研究与实践应用奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Agentic AI is gaining new insights and advancements in the field of Artificial Intelligence, fostering significant potential to enable rapid transformation across various domains.This rapid advancement and the potential to revolutionize various domains advocate the need for a deeper understanding and firm grasp of the technology. Moreover, an investigation into state of the art research directions in agentic AI needs to be conducted to comprehensively assess the potential scope for improvement and application.Therefore, to address these objectives, a comprehensive review can provide researchers and practitioners with valuable insights into the current state and future research scopes of agentic AI.Hence, this work considers the recently published scholarly contributions in agentic AI across various domains and discusses the fundamentals and working principles of Agentic AI, traces the historical and theoretical evolution of agency in artificial systems, explores and discusses Agentic AIs architecture, working principles, and functionalities, explores real-world applications of Agentic AI across various domains, analyzes the research findings, identifies current challenges, and discuss potential future research directions, and proposes a comprehensive framework of stakeholders intention to use and adopt Agentic AI with the help of proposed system quality dimensions.Therefore, this systematic review provides researchers and practitioners with a comprehensive understanding of Agentic AI, its current developments and applications, highlights key research gaps, and outlines future research directions.

</details>


### [190] [Adversarial Review: Structured Disagreement for Grounded Agentic Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18167)
*Eric S. Qiu,Joyce Gill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Adversarial Review (AR)，一种极简协作式代码审查协议，仅使用三个智能体（主编码智能体、审查者、批评者）即可在仓库级编码任务上超越五智能体基线，证明了小型、结构化、基于证据的分歧协作优于大规模多智能体团队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期多智能体LLM系统依赖角色分离团队，但增加智能体数量在仓库级编码任务上收益递减；而将智能体视为被动工具（子智能体）又完全丧失了智能体交互的优势。作者希望探索子智能体范式能否支持一个中间地带：最小化的智能体协作，不引入大型多智能体团队的过高开销。

Method: AR协议包含三个角色：主编码智能体负责编辑代码，审查者评估代码质量，批评者通过结构化分歧审计审查意见，之后主智能体再进行修改。在SWE-PRBench上通过一次提示迭代显式加入分歧机制以解决虚假共识失效模式。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench上，AR以仅三个智能体超越了五智能体基线，取得最高通过率；在SWE-PRBench上，朴素AR暴露出虚假共识失效模式，但显式添加分歧后达到最高F1；在SWE-bench Verified上也优于基线。

Conclusion: 协作式代码审查不需要大量智能体或复杂通信结构，关键在于使分歧最小化、结构化且基于证据。AR证明了极简对抗性协作协议的有效性。

Abstract: Early multi-agent LLM systems often used role-separated teams, yet scaling agent count yields diminishing returns on repository-level coding tasks. Recent alternatives treat agents as passive tools (subagents), yet this removes the benefits of agent interaction entirely. We study whether a subagent paradigm can support a middle ground: minimal agentic cooperation without the overhead of large multi-agent teams. We introduce Adversarial Review (AR), a minimal cooperative code-review protocol in which a main coding agent works with a reviewer and a critic agent. The reviewer evaluates code, while the critic audits the review through structured disagreement before the main agent edits. On LiveCodeBench, AR achieves the highest pass rate among tested methods, outperforming a five-agent baseline while using only three agents. On SWE-PRBench, naive AR exposes a false-consensus failure mode, where agents converge on agreement without sufficient evidence, but a single prompt iteration that adds disagreement explicitly achieves the highest F1 among tested methods. On SWE-bench Verified, AR also shows improvements over the baselines on repository-level coding tasks. Together, AR demonstrates that cooperative code review does not require many agents or complex communication structures: it requires that disagreement be minimal, structured, and evidence-grounded.

</details>


### [191] [Solving Is Not Drawing: A Benchmark for Diagrammatic Reasoning in Olympiad Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18111)
*Hsien Xin Peng,Anthony Kim,Alvin Li,Calvin Supasanya,Shivank Garg,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源基准测试，包含954道奥林匹克几何问题（含297道困难子集），每道题配有解决方案和人类编写的高保真Asymptote代码图表，并提供了多种指标来评估模型的"图表推理"能力。实验发现当前基础模型在求解和作图之间存在显著差距，平均编译成功率仅36.14%，表明强大的数学推理能力并不等同于能构造准确的几何图形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型（如GPT、Claude）已能熟练解决奥林匹克级别的几何问题，但解决问题与绘制依赖的图形是两种不同的技能。现有的基准测试（如MathVista、MathVerse）仅衡量模型是否得出正确答案，没有单独评估模型构造几何图表的能力，这一关键能力尚未被测量。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含954道自包含奥林匹克几何问题的基准数据集，其中297道为困难子集。每道题配有解决方案和人类编写的、可渲染的高保真Asymptote代码图，并设计了文本、代码、图像、VLM和基于约束的多种度量指标来评估"图表推理"能力。

Result: 评估当前基础模型后发现，模型在求解与作图之间存在显著鸿沟：生成的图表保真度明显不足，平均编译成功率仅为36.14%。研究结论指出，强大的数学推理能力并不意味着能够构造准确的几何图形。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了现有评测体系在图表构造能力上的空白，揭示了基础模型在几何问题求解与图形绘制能力上的不对称性，为未来模型在图表推理方向上的改进提供了评测工具和数据集。数据已在HuggingFace公开。

Abstract: Foundation models such as GPT and Claude now solve olympiad-level mathematics with remarkable proficiency, so much so that geometry problem solving has become a standard proxy for their mathematical reasoning. Yet solving a geometry problem and drawing the figure it depends on are not the same skill: progress often hinges on a faithful diagram with the right auxiliary constructions and incidences, and it is unclear that a model which reasons its way to the answer can also produce one. A growing collection of benchmarks, including MathVista, and MathVerse, measures whether models reach the correct answer, but to our knowledge, none isolate the distinct ability to construct the diagram itself, leaving this capability unmeasured. We introduce an open-source benchmark that targets this gap: 954 self-contained olympiad geometry problems, with a 297-problem hard subset, each paired with its solution and a human-authored, high-fidelity diagram in renderable Asymptote code, together with a suite of text-, code-, image-, VLM-, and constraint-based metrics for what we term diagrammatic reasoning. Evaluating current foundation models reveals a pronounced gap between solving and drawing: their diagrams are markedly less faithful, with an average compile success rate of only 36.14\%. Strong mathematical reasoning, we find, does not imply the ability to construct accurate geometric diagrams. Our benchmark and dataset can be accessed at https://huggingface.co/datasets/max98765/hard_geometry_problems_with_diagrams.

</details>


### [192] [Metrics That Write Themselves: Evolving an Evaluator from Its Own Blind Spots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18744)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Zhihao Lin,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvalCEGAR方法，利用程序验证中的反例引导抽象精化思想，从一组标记缺陷的小型Python算子池中自动演化出评估指标，解决代理在没有可靠自动指标时无法提升的问题。在MBPP+和HumanEval+上，该方法能用55行算子缩小15.4%的性能差距，且仅需人工算子的1/4标记量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理在可靠自动指标下能快速提升，但没有指标时就会停滞。报告生成等最需要指标的应用却无人知道如何评分。直接让模型生成算子不可行（183个候选仅96种不同行为），需要新的方法来自动编写评估指标。

Method: 借鉴程序验证中的反例引导抽象精化（CEGAR）方法。将算子池视为一个抽象，搜索碰撞（即两个答案得分相同但一正一误），用该碰撞对而非提示作为作者请求；当碰撞击败所有尝试时，扩大算子可读取的信息范围而非重新采样。

Result: 在MBPP+和HumanEval+上，循环编写出55行算子，在428个未见任务上缩小了从零标记到完美过滤器之间15.4%的差距（+0.0065, p=0.0010），且标记量仅为人手工最优算子的1/4。八次运行中六次成功，全部在样本外有效；15个人工算子联合使用反而降低精度。LLM裁判在几乎不相交的候选集上能达到相同效果，但每次调用消耗一个模型调用，而算子零成本。

Conclusion: EvalCEGAR证明评估指标可以由系统自动"写"出来，通过碰撞搜索和抽象精化可以有效探索算子空间，生成的算子不仅在样本外有效，而且运行时零成本，优于人工设计的指标以及LLM裁判。

Abstract: Agents improve quickly against a reliable automatic metric and stall without one, and the applications that need them most, report generation among them, are the ones nobody knows how to score. Can the metric write itself? Saying what makes an answer good is hard; pointing at something wrong with one is easier, so the metric we evolve is a pool of small Python operators that each flag a candidate for one named defect, or abstain, and vote. Asking a model for operators directly does not work: 183 candidates realise only 96 distinct behaviours, from one narrow region of an enormous space. EvalCEGAR instead borrows counterexample-guided abstraction refinement from program verification. It reads the pool as an abstraction and searches for a collision, two answers the operators score identically, one correct and one not. That pair, not a prompt, is the authoring request, and when a collision defeats every attempt the loop widens what an operator may read rather than resampling. On MBPP+ and HumanEval+, a sandbox whose hidden unit tests give exact ground truth, the loop writes a 55-line operator that closes 15.4% of the gap between flagging nothing and a perfect filter on 428 unseen tasks (+0.0065, p=0.0010) at a quarter of our best hand-written operator's flags. On the benchmark it never saw it matches that operator's effect exactly on a third of the flags. Six of eight runs admit such an operator and all six help out of sample; our 15 hand-written operators applied together as one filter lose accuracy. An LLM judge on the same information ties that delta on a nearly disjoint set of candidates, and charges a model call per candidate forever where the operator charges none.

</details>


### [193] [Position: AI Leaderboards Are Underserving the Global South: A Case Study from India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18117)
*Sourav Banerjee,Saikat Saha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了AI排行榜在制度设计上不适合服务全球南方，问题不在于缺乏高质量的区域基准数据（如印度的IndicSUPERB、非洲的IrokoBench等），而在于缺乏独立治理、利益冲突政策和指标演化机制。通过对58名印度AI从业者的咨询，发现他们一致偏好正式治理和披露式冲突管理，主张建立从一开始就具备独立治理的区域排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球南方的AI基准已存在且质量高，但未被全球排行榜纳入，缺乏制度性的推动机制。商业压力只在美国北方付费客户受影响时才会纠正排行榜缺陷，全球南方缺乏同等制衡力量，导致印地语、斯瓦希里语或阿拉伯语的使用者面临的AI缺陷长期得不到解决。

Method: 以印度为案例（14亿人口、22种官方语言、已有高质量基准但缺乏可信聚合机构），对58名AI从业者进行咨询调研，收集其对排行榜治理和冲突管理方案的偏好意见。

Result: 咨询结果显示，AI从业者一致偏好正式治理机制以及基于披露的冲突管理方式，表明制度设计而非数据缺口是全球南方AI评估的核心障碍。

Conclusion: 解决方案不是收集更多数据，而是建立更好的制度：从一开始就具备独立治理的区域排行榜，以弥补全球南方在AI评估治理方面的结构性缺失。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI leaderboards are structurally ill-suited to serving the Global South because they lack independent governance, conflict-of-interest policies, and mechanisms for metric evolution. The barrier is not missing data; high-quality regional benchmarks already exist: IndicSUPERB, MILU, and LAHAJA for India; IrokoBench for Africa; AlGhafa for Arabic. The barrier is institutional design. Global leaderboards do not include these benchmarks, and no governance mechanism compels them to do so. Commercial pressure corrects leaderboard failures when paying customers in the Global North are affected. The Global South lacks equivalent leverage. Without governance, failures affecting Hindi, Swahili, or Arabic speakers persist indefinitely as documented but unaddressed gaps. Using India as a case study (1.4 billion people, 22 scheduled languages, high-quality benchmarks, but no trusted aggregation), we report findings from a consultation with 58 AI practitioners showing consistent preference for formal governance and disclosure-based conflict management. The solution is not more data but better institutions: regional leaderboards with independent governance from the start.

</details>


### [194] [Tuning the Stochastic Machine: A Systems Engineer's Operating Model for Human-AI Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19125)
*George Andrikopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从系统工程师视角，将LLM技术栈映射到传统机器运维体系，指出纠正措施随会话失效是运维纪律缺失而非工具问题，提出包含错误循环的七原则运维规范，并给出测量框架与实验验证方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家纠正LLM助手错误后，纠正措施随会话结束而失效，同类错误反复出现。作者认为这是运维（operations）问题而非工具问题——持久化机制虽已存在，但缺乏版本管理、溯源、监控、退役等治理纪律。

Method: 作者从三十年系统工程师经验出发，将LLM技术栈映射到传统机器体系（冻结硅片、固件、可加载模块、持久配置、易失内存），识别映射失效点（随机生成、仅概率性绑定的配置、默认缺乏通用退役环节），据此推导出以错误循环为核心的七原则运维规范，并结合三个实践案例（包括一个控制措施最终演变为其原本要预防的伤害）加以说明。

Result: 提出了七原则运维纪律及核心错误循环机制；通过三个实际案例验证了机制的有效性与潜在风险，其中包括一个控制措施悄然异化为自身所防范危害的警示案例；最终提出相应的测量框架和所需实验室研究。

Conclusion: LLM纠正治理应从"工具建设"转向"运维纪律"，建立带溯源版本管理、复发监控、反向指标、过期规则退役的完整运营体系，并需通过实验室研究验证该框架的有效性。

Abstract: When an expert corrects an LLM assistant's error, the correction usually dies with the session, and the error class returns. I argue this is an operations problem, not a tooling problem: mechanisms for persisting corrections exist and are shipping, but the discipline for governing them -- versioning with provenance, recurrence monitoring, counter-metrics, retirement of stale rules -- does not. Writing as a systems engineer of thirty years, I map the LLM stack onto the machines my profession already operates (frozen silicon, firmware, loadable modules, persistent configuration, volatile memory), identify where the mapping fails (stochastic generation, configuration that binds only probabilistically, no general-purpose retirement (verification) stage by default), and derive from the failures a seven-principle operating discipline with an error loop at its core. Three cases from my own practice illustrate the mechanism, among them a control that silently became the exact harm it was built to prevent. I close with the measurement framework this view implies and the lab study required to test it.

</details>


### [195] [Safety Alignment Illusion: The Cross-Lingual Safety Gap in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18131)
*Namya Bhatnagar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了INCLUDE，一个用于量化印度中心社会文化偏见的多语言评估基准，包含2,604个提示词和6种语言（英语、印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语、泰米尔语和Hinglish），评估了10个开源和闭源LLM，分析了14,988个偏见得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的安全对齐训练以英语为中心，导致安全过滤机制在非英语语言中失效，可能产生强化刻板印象的输出并传播有害偏见，尤其影响印度多语言用户群体。

Method: 构建INCLUDE多语言评估基准，涵盖6种印度常用语言，收集14,988个偏见得分，对10个开源和闭源LLM进行系统性统计评估。

Result: 两个关键发现：1) 孟加拉语在开源模型中产生最高的平均偏见得分；2) 英语出现显著反转，在开源模型中偏见最低，但在闭源模型中偏见最高。

Conclusion: 当前安全对齐在不同语言和模型类型间存在不一致的偏见表现，需要更关注非英语语言的跨语言偏见检测与缓解。

Abstract: Current safety alignment training for Large Language Models (LLMs) are heavily English-centric. When such safety filters fail for non-English languages, the consequences are immediate and user-facing: voice assistants and spoken dialogue systems may produce stereotype-reinforcing outputs, bypassing the standard English-focused safety alignments and propagating harmful bias to non-English speaking communities. For spoken language technologies deployed across India's linguistically diverse population, this represents a critical failure mode. To address this cross-lingual gap, we introduce INCLUDE (Indian Cultural Lens for Understanding and Detecting Embedded Biases), a multilingual evaluation benchmark designed to quantify Indian-centric socio-cultural biases. INCLUDE consists of 2,604 prompts spanning six prompt languages: English, Hindi, Bengali, Marathi, Tamil, and Hinglish (Hindi-English code-mix). We evaluate ten open- and closed-source LLMs against this benchmark, analyzing 14,988 bias scores. Our statistical results reveal two key findings. First, Bengali yielded the highest average bias score in open-source models. Second, English demonstrated a notable reversal, producing the lowest bias in open-source models but the highest bias in closed-source models.

</details>


### [196] [Grouping the Stochastic Machine: Precision, Not Capability, as the Frontier Metric for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19140)
*George Andrikopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出前沿语言模型的区分维度应从"能力"（平均输出水平）转向"精度/可靠性"（输出在目标周围的集中程度），并论证精度可低成本测量且能指导决策——区分系统性偏差（可通过操作纪律修正）与离散性偏差（需更换模型或采样）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试和模型对比均以"能力"（均值表现）为核心指标，但前沿模型在准确率上已趋于饱和（均值落在目标上），真正区分系统的是输出围绕目标的集中程度（精度），而基准文化系统性忽略离散度测量。

Method: 提出三个论点：(1) 精度而非能力是前沿系统的主要区分维度； (2) 精度可通过固定确定性评分任务集在固定温度下多次运行，计算每任务结果一致性来低成本测量，无需模型作为评判者； (3) 该测量具有决策指导价值——将失败分为"紧凑偏离"（可调校修正）和"离散发散"（需换模型或采样）。定义分组度量、规定测试框架，并展示跟踪人机联合分组随时间变化可提供Paper 1现场研究所需的复合信号。

Result: 首次真实运行（已被复现）表明：单一规则可将测量到的差距完全消除（0/5升至5/5）；而由规则本身构建的任务套件未发现价值——因为前沿模型已内化了显式良好实践，说明纪律的价值须在真实工作中的测量中发现，而非从规则手册构造。

Conclusion: 精度测量不仅描述性更强，还是可操作的决策工具：它能区分可修正的偏差与需更换模型的偏差，其价值须基于真实工作的测量而非自造的规则来验证。

Abstract: Frontier language models are compared, marketed, and benchmarked on capability -- what their best or average output can achieve. I argue this measures the wrong axis. The models have saturated accuracy: their mean output lands on the target. What now separates one system from another in practice is precision: how tightly concentrated their outputs are around that target across repeated, identical requests. Borrowing the marksman's distinction, capability is where the average shot lands; reliability is the size of the group. I make three claims. First, precision, not capability, is the frontier differentiator between systems, and benchmark culture systematically fails to measure it, reporting central tendency rather than spread. Second, precision is measurable, cheaply and without circularity, by running a fixed suite of deterministically scored tasks many times at fixed temperature and computing the per-task consistency of outcomes -- no model-in-the-loop grader required. Third, the measurement is not merely descriptive but decision-guiding: it separates consistent failures (a tight group off-centre, correctable by the operating discipline of Paper 1 -- a sight adjustment) from scattered failures (a wide group, correctable only by changing the model or its sampling -- a rifle problem). I define a grouping metric, specify a harness, and show how tracking a human-AI pair's grouping over time yields the compounding signal that Paper 1's field study requires. A first real run, since replicated, illustrates both the method and its most important limit: one measured gap was closed completely by a single rule (0/5 -> 5/5), while a suite of tasks authored from the rules themselves found no value, because a frontier model already embodies explicit good practice -- establishing that a discipline's worth is found by measurement on real work, not constructed from its own rulebook.

</details>


### [197] [Optimized Fuzzy Logic Approach with the IEEE Key Gas Method for Diagnosing Power Transformer Faults Using Dissolved Gas Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18133)
*Kim-Anh Nguyen,Huy Hoang Le,Ba Tu Phung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种融合模糊逻辑与IEEE关键气体法的变压器故障诊断模型（FL-KGM），通过优化隶属函数、模糊规则集及CO/CO2分离策略，在真实数据集上达到98.6%的诊断准确率，显著优于传统KGM及其他模糊逻辑方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变压器故障诊断对电力系统稳定性至关重要，但传统的IEEE关键气体法（KGM）在油中溶解气体分析中存在对模糊数据处理能力不足、诊断准确率有限的问题，亟需改进。

Method: 提出FL-KGM增强模型，核心包括：精炼的隶属函数设计、优化的模糊规则集、新颖的CO与CO2分离策略以消除诊断不一致性，并结合多维气体比率分析与自适应分类框架。

Result: 基于真实世界数据集的实验验证表明，FL-KGM最高可达98.6%的诊断准确率，显著优于传统KGM和其他基于模糊逻辑的方法。

Conclusion: FL-KGM展现出推进变压器监测、实现智能故障检测和增强现代电力系统预测性维护策略的潜力。

Abstract: Reliable transformer fault diagnosis is essential for maintaining power system stability. The IEEE Key Gas Method (KGM), a widely utilized approach in Dissolved Gas Analysis (DGA), exhibits limitations in addressing ambiguous data and ensuring high diagnostic accuracy. This study presents An enhanced model combining Fuzzy Logic with the IEEE Key Gas Method (FL-KGM) that introduces refined membership functions, optimized fuzzy rule sets, and a novel separation of CO and CO2 to eliminate diagnostic inconsistencies. By leveraging multidimensional gas ratio analysis and an adaptive classification framework, FL-KGM delivers superior fault identification and classification. Experimental validation utilizing real-world datasets demonstrates that FL-KGM achieves up to 98.6% accuracy, significantly outperforming KGM and other FL-based approaches. These findings elucidate the potential of FL-KGM in advancing transformer monitoring, enabling intelligent fault detection, and enhancing predictive maintenance strategies in modern power systems.

</details>


### [198] [Improving Rural Medication Safety with AI: A Scoping Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18135)
*Jeong-ah Kim,Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Daniel Terry,Maryam Rouhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过2012至2025年的系统性文献综述，分析了人工智能技术在乡村医疗环境中减少用药错误、提升患者安全的应用与效果，涵盖处方、配药、给药到用药后监测的全流程，并提出乡村特有的实施障碍与对策建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用药错误是全球医疗系统的重大威胁，会造成患者伤害。乡村医疗环境面临资源匮乏、基础设施薄弱等挑战，引入人工智能有望提升患者安全、降低用药错误率，但目前缺乏对AI在乡村医疗中药学安全应用的系统性综述研究。

Method: 采用范围综述（scoping review）方法，对2012至2025年间EBSCohost、Emcare (Ovid)、MEDLINE及ProQuest Consumer Health Database等多个数据库进行系统文献检索，纳入来自9个国家共12项初级研究，采用主题分析法对AI干预措施在用药过程中的数据进行归纳分析。

Result: 研究发现AI技术已整合至用药管理的全流程（处方、配药、给药及用药后监测），归纳出四个主题：（1）所用AI类型（如临床决策支持系统、机器学习、自然语言处理及智能输液泵）；（2）受影响的用药过程阶段；（3）技术在减少错误和提升工作流安全性方面的有效性——基于机器学习的监测可将处方和转录错误减少34%至80%；（4）乡村特有挑战，包括基础设施、员工培训、系统集成和警报疲劳。主要障碍还包括缺乏治理框架、资金限制和临床医生抵触。

Conclusion: 人工智能技术在乡村医疗中改善药品安全方面具有巨大潜力，能够支持数据驱动的监测、实现流程自动化并提供临床决策辅助，但仍需解决治理框架缺失、经费不足和人员接受度等现实障碍方可充分发挥其价值。

Abstract: Introduction: Medication errors (MEs) represent a significant threat to global healthcare systems, contributing to patient harm. Introducing artificial intelligence (AI) in rural healthcare enhances patient safety. The aim is to explore the applications and effectiveness of AI technologies in enhancing patient safety and reducing medication errors in rural health settings.
  Methods: A scoping review was conducted through a systematic literature search spanning 2012 to 2025 across multiple databases, including EBSCohost, Emcare (Ovid), MEDLINE, and the ProQuest Consumer Health Database. Twelve primary studies from nine different nations were examined. Data were analysed thematically to obtain insights on AI interventions across the medication process.
  Results: AI technologies have been integrated into every stage of medication management, right from prescribing and dispensing to administration and post-administration monitoring. Four key themes came to light: (1) the various types of AI being utilised (like Clinical Decision Support Systems, Machine Learning, Natural Language Processing, and smart pumps); (2) the phases of the medication process that are affected; (3) how effective these technologies are in minimising errors and boosting workflow safety; and (4) rural-specific challenges including infrastructure, staff training, system integration, and alert fatigue. Several studies have demonstrated that machine learning-based surveillance improves incident detection and reduces prescribing and transcription errors by an impressive 34% to 80%. Barriers included lack of governance frameworks, financial limitations, and clinician resistance, which still present major obstacles.
  Conclusion: In rural healthcare, AI technologies hold great potential for enhancing pharmaceutical safety. They can allow data-driven monitoring, automate processes, and offer clinical decision assistance.

</details>


### [199] [FraudBench: Stress-Testing Policy-Grounded Banking Agents Against Adaptive Fraud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18136)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FraudBench，一个基于工具执行的可运行金融欺诈基准测试，用于评估银行对话代理在多轮对话中被操纵身份、授权和信任时的安全性。基准包含150个对抗场景（107个公开测试集），基于698份内部政策文档的银行环境构建。初步评估显示四种代理的攻击安全性仅为49%-65%，暴露出普遍的安全弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的金融欺诈基准仅对静态交易或消息进行分类，通用代理安全基准仅针对提示注入或一般性有害用途，都无法测试政策约束下的银行代理在对话中被调用者操纵身份、授权和信任时能否安全行动。

Method: 基于τ²-bench双控制框架和τ-Knowledge银行环境构建FraudBench。代理和模拟调用者均通过工具作用于共享可变账户状态，代理可授予调用者特定工具访问权限；环境包含698份内部政策文档。共设计150个对抗场景，其中107个公开测试集（90个覆盖10种欺诈机制+17个链式自适应攻击），43个留作隐藏。每个场景标注可观察证据、禁止操作、安全处置和干预点。

Result: 对4种代理在107个分级任务上的初步单轮评估显示，攻击安全性为49%-65%，其中钱骡（money-mule）和第一方欺诈是最常见的跨模型弱点。

Conclusion: FraudBench填补了政策约束下对话式银行代理安全评估的空白，揭示了现有代理在对抗性对话中的显著安全漏洞，为后续改进提供了可执行的评测标准。

Abstract: Conversational agents now act for end users through tools while holding access to customer databases and internal policy documents that a caller can reach through dialogue alone. Banking is the clearest case: the same agent that answers a question can also change contact details, reset a PIN, or move money, so ordinary customer service is inseparable from authorization, fraud detection, and policy compliance. Existing financial-fraud benchmarks classify static transactions or messages, and general agent-safety benchmarks target prompt injection or generic harmful use; none test whether a policy-grounded banking agent safely acts when a caller manipulates identity, authorization, and trust over a conversation. We introduce FraudBench, an executable benchmark built on the $τ^2$-bench dual-control framework and the $τ$-Knowledge banking environment. Both the agent and the simulated caller act through tools over shared, mutable account state, and the agent may grant the caller access to selected tools; the environment exposes a 698-document internal policy corpus that the agent must retrieve from. FraudBench contains 150 authored adversarial scenarios; a frozen public set of 107 (90 across ten fraud mechanisms plus 17 chained adaptive attacks) is used for all reported runs, with 43 further chained attacks held out. Safety is history-dependent: single-control tasks satisfy every precondition but one, and adaptive attacks make a later, locally valid request unsafe because of an earlier probe, admission, or failed attempt. Each scenario is annotated with observable evidence, prohibited actions, safe dispositions, and intervention points. A preliminary single-trial evaluation of four agents on the 107 graded tasks yields attack-security between 49\% and 65\%, with money-mule and first-party fraud the most common cross-model weaknesses.

</details>


### [200] [Efficient Adaptation of LLMs for Hate Speech Detection in Low-Resource Languages: A Comparative Study on Roman Urdu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18142)
*Toneema Zubair,Muhammad Junaid Asif,Faisal Kamiran,Hafiz Hassan Saeed,Rana Fayyaz Ahmad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对罗马乌尔都语（一种低资源语言）中的仇恨言论检测进行了全面评估，比较了多种大型语言模型在零样本推理和参数高效微调（LoRA）两种设置下的表现，并在包含72,000多条注释评论的PURUTT数据集上进行了实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如罗马乌尔都语）缺乏标注数据、语言结构非正式且缺乏标准化语法，导致仇恨言论检测极具挑战性。作者希望探究LLM在此类语言中的检测能力，并验证PEFT方法是否能有效提升性能。

Method: 采用零样本推理与参数高效微调（PEFT）的LoRA方法进行对比实验，在多种Transformer模型（Mistral、LLaMA、Falcon和多语言BERT）上进行基准测试，使用PURUTT数据集（含72,000多条注释评论）进行评估。

Result: 零样本模型表现中等（F1=0.56），但仅更新一小部分可训练参数后，分类性能显著提升（F1>0.93）。PEFT在计算效率和分类性能上均表现出色。

Conclusion: PEFT（LoRA）方法在低资源语言处理中展现出优秀的性能与计算效率的平衡，非常适合低资源语言的仇恨言论检测任务。

Abstract: It is challenging to detect hate speech in Low Resource Languages (LRLs) because of the absence of annotated data, the informality of its language structure, and the lack of standardized grammar. A good example of such a challenge is Roman Urdu which is broadly used by South Asians on social media and has a high variation while lacking contextually consistent spellings. The objective of this paper is to conduct a comprehensive assessment of Large Language Models (LLMs) for Hate Speech Detection (HSD) in Roman Urdu script and fine-tune these models using the Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) method called Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). To evaluate zero-shot inference, we benchmarked it against PEFT on different transformer models, including Mistral, LLaMA, Falcon, and multilingual BERT. Experiments are conducted on the PURUTT (Parallel Urdu and Roman Urdu Corpus for Toxic Comments and Transliteration) dataset with over 72,000 annotated comments. The results suggest that zero shot models perform moderately (F1 = 0.56), but updating a small fraction of the model trainable parameters improves the classification performance significantly (F1 > 0.93). Our results have shown that PEFT delivers outstanding performance alongside excellent computational efficiency, making it highly suitable for low-resource language processing tasks.

</details>


### [201] [RDFdL: Integrating RDF with Differential Dynamic Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18165)
*Yuyang Li,Lukas Kubelka,Julia Butte,Tobias Käfer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RDFdL框架，将RDF与微分动态逻辑（dL）集成，用于同时表示和推理静态知识与物理系统的连续动态行为，实现安全性和可达性验证结果可作为RDF数据的SPARQL查询蕴涵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱中的RDF擅长描述静态知识，但无法捕获或推理物理系统的动态行为（如微分方程描述的系统），这对AI驱动的信息物理系统是一个关键缺口。

Method: 通过一阶逻辑的共同基础将RDF和dL链接起来：动态部分用RDF和SHACL语法表示微分方程和状态空间范围，并通过翻译为dL提供语义；验证结果转化为对RDF数据的SPARQL查询蕴涵。

Result: 使用Apache Jena进行本体驱动的RDF推理，KeYmaera X作为dL定理证明器实现该流水线，并在制造业中展示了其适用性。

Conclusion: RDFdL实现了静态知识表示与动态系统推理的独特集成，使动态逻辑领域的验证结果可直接用于SPARQL查询RDF数据，为AI驱动的信息物理系统推理提供了新方案。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs modeled in RDF are powerful for describing static knowledge, but they cannot capture or reason about the dynamic behavior of physical systems, e.g., systems described by differential equations, which is a critical gap for AI-driven cyber-physical systems. To solve this, we propose RDFdL, a framework that integrates RDF with Differential Dynamic Logic (dL) to represent and reason about both static knowledge and the continuous dynamics of physical systems. For the dynamic part, we syntactically represent differential equations and ranges in the state space in RDF and SHACL and provide semantics using a translation to dL. Linking RDF and dL through their shared foundation in first-order logic achieves a unique integration: verification results for safety and reachability properties in the dynamic logic domain become available as entailment to SPARQL queries over RDF data. We implement the pipeline using Apache Jena for ontology-driven RDF reasoning and KeYmaera X, the theorem prover for dL, and sketch its applicability in manufacturing.

</details>


### [202] [Looped Language Models Improve Compositional Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18171)
*Andrei Cristian Popescu,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了循环语言模型（looped language models）在智能体工具调用方面的潜力，在组合式工具调用场景下，比较了循环与非循环模型在匹配的有监督微调设置下的性能差异，发现循环计算对组合式、依赖感知的工具使用有普遍益处，且自适应推理能实现更好的计算-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型在推理基准上表现优异，但其在智能体工具使用方面的潜力尚未被充分探索，特别是在需要协调多个API调用、维护中间状态、保持跨工具交互依赖的组合式场景中。

Method: 在API-Bank、BFCL和NESTful三个基准上评估原生和改造的循环语言模型，与匹配有监督微调配方的非循环模型进行对比，并改变推理时的循环深度，同时测试自适应推理策略。

Result: 在受控实验中，循环计算对组合式和依赖感知的工具使用普遍有利，但对孤立API调用的增益较小且依赖模型。多步工具使用的准确率通常随循环深度增加而提升，自适应推理以更优的计算-性能权衡实现效果。

Conclusion: 循环语言模型是构建需要可靠规划、协调和执行组合式工具使用工作流的智能体系统的有前景的架构选择。

Abstract: Looped language models have shown promising results on reasoning benchmarks, yet their potential for agentic tool use remains largely unexplored. We study this question in compositional tool-calling settings, where models must coordinate multiple API calls, maintain intermediate state, and preserve dependencies across tool interactions. We evaluate native and retrofitted looped language models on API-Bank, BFCL, and NESTful, comparing looped and non-looped models trained under matched supervised fine-tuning recipes and varying recurrent depth at inference time. In controlled experiments, recurrent computation generally benefits compositional and dependency-aware tool use, while providing smaller and more model-dependent gains on isolated API invocation. Accuracy on multi-step tool use generally increases with recurrent depth; adaptive inference, however, achieves a more favorable compute-performance trade-off by allocating additional computation only when needed. Our results suggest that looped language models are a promising architecture for agentic systems that require reliable planning, coordination, and execution of compositional tool use workflows.

</details>


### [203] [On the Triangle Inequality for the Jaccard Distance in Arbitrary Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18194)
*Costin Bădică,Amelia Bădică*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对格与实值赋值上的Jaccard距离提出了新的理论结果，证明了在不同格结构条件下三角不等式成立的条件，并探讨了其在量子信息、形式概念分析和机器学习等领域的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的Jaccard距离三角不等式结果严重依赖格的分配性等强结构约束，作者希望放宽这些约束条件，在更一般的格结构上建立有效的度量性质，并探讨其实际应用价值。

Method: 作者系统地分析了不同类型的格结构（任意格、相对补分配格、截段补分配格）与不同性质的赋值（正性、单调性、模性、超模性、对数次模性等）组合下Jaccard距离满足三角不等式的充分条件，同时通过反例证明了超模性是必要条件，并通过理论推导给出必要条件的严格证明。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 当赋值为严格正、单调、模性时，任意格上的Jaccard距离满足三角不等式；(2) 在相对补分配格上，当赋值为正、单调、超模且对数次模时三角不等式成立；(3) 将对称差Jaccard公式推广到截段补分配格上的次模赋值；(4) 证明了超模性是标准广义Jaccard距离成为有效度量的严格必要条件。

Conclusion: 本文放宽了Jaccard距离三角不等式的结构约束条件，揭示了超模性的必要性，并指出了这些理论进展在量子信息理论、形式概念分析和机器学习等计算领域的实践价值，同时提出了若干待解决的开放数学问题。

Abstract: This paper presents new theoretical results on generalizing the Jaccard distance for lattices and real valuations. We demonstrate that when the valuation is strictly positive, monotone, and modular, the Jaccard distance satisfies the triangle inequality on arbitrary lattices, effectively generalizing earlier results that depended heavily on distributivity. Moving to relatively complemented distributive lattices (which safely drop the requirement for the global bounds found in Boolean algebras), we prove the triangle inequality holds as long as the valuation is positive, monotone, supermodular, and $\log$-submodular. Additionally, we adapt the symmetric-difference Jaccard formulation for submodular valuations to sectionally complemented distributive lattices. Shifting to necessary conditions, we prove that supermodularity is a strict requirement for the standard generalized Jaccard distance to operate as a valid metric. Finally, we map the practical value of relaxing these structural constraints to computational fields like quantum information theory, formal concept analysis, and machine learning, closing with a brief look at open mathematical problems.

</details>


### [204] [GenEx: A Graph-Based Representational Paradigm for SARS-CoV-2 Variant Detection via Codon Co-occurrence Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18238)
*Arefin Amin,Labiba Faiza Karim,M. Monir Uddin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GenEx管道，将原始基因序列转换为密码子共现图，利用MSCG和LAPCG两种算法提取25+图特征，并结合SVD谱特征提取（使用σ²而非特征值），在23个机器学习模型上实现了对SARS-CoV-2变体的高精度检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统病毒基因组分析方法（序列比对、系统发育、突变频率统计）将基因序列视为线性字符串，无法捕捉核苷酸/密码子之间的复杂上下文依赖关系，且无法高效区分高度相似的病毒变体如SARS-CoV-2的Beta、Gamma、Delta和Omicron。

Method: 提出GenEx管道，包含两个核心算法：MSCG（多尺度密码子共现图）和LAPCG（线性时间邻接PMI密码子图），将密码子序列转化为共现图并提取25+图特征；同时采用SVD谱图特征提取，使用平方奇异值σ²替代传统特征值以增强主次谱分量分离度，提升分类性能。

Result: 在最新SARS-CoV-2变体数据上训练了23种基准机器学习模型，成功实现了对所有SARS-CoV-2变体的高精度检测，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 图表示学习方法（将密码子序列建模为结构化图）能有效弥补经典生物信息学方法在捕捉序列上下文依赖关系方面的不足，为病毒基因组分析提供了计算语言学视角的新范式。

Abstract: Genomic analysis on viruses such as SARS-CoV-2 variants: Beta, Gamma, Delta, and Omicron is heavily dominated by classical bioinformatics methods, including Sequence Alignment, Phylogenetic Analysis, and Mutation Frequency Statistics. These approaches use pairwise codon or nucleotide distance matrices to analyze gene sequences, treating them as linear strings rather than capturing their complex contextual interdependencies. We proposed GenEx, a pipeline that converts raw gene sequences into codon co-occurrence graphs and extracts more than 25 graph features. Our two most prominent techniques for graph generation and feature extraction are MSCG (Multi-Scale Codon Co-occurrence Graph) and LAPCG (Linear-time Adjacency PMI Codon Graph).
