In this paper, we propose Class Expression Simplifier (CES), a novel algorithm for the syntactic simplification of class expressions in Description Logics (DL). CES aims to preserve formal semantics while reducing representational complexity. It systematically applies rewriting rules to eliminate redundancies and identify simpler yet equivalent expressions, thereby producing more compact and human-readable representations without altering logical entailments. We evaluate the effectiveness of CES on class expressions learned from two medium-sized ontologies, demonstrating measurable improvements in reasoning efficiency and reductions in verbosity. This work contributes to the broader goal of making ontology-driven applications more accessible, maintainable, and scalable, with direct implications for knowledge graph construction, semantic search, and Web-scale reasoning. CES is implemented within the open-source Python framework OWLAPY and is publicly available.

</details>


### [238] [\textsc{TestifAI}: Tomography-Based Testing for Deep Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18900)
*Arooj Arif,Tobias Hartung,Elena Botoeva,Alexandros Koliousis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TestifAI框架，通过"部分模型断层扫描"技术，仅利用低阶扰动测试结果即可高效准确预测高阶组合扰动下的模型鲁棒性，在减少60-80%推理次数的同时将鲁棒性估计误差控制在7%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在安全关键领域的部署日益增多，深度学习模型需要经过彻底测试以确保其行为正确。然而，现有测试框架缺乏系统探索和总结跨组合扰动空间鲁棒性的手段，因为针对高维组合扰动的鲁棒性测试需要指数级别的推理次数，效率极低。

Method: TestifAI框架包含两个核心创新：(1) 允许用户将操作条件指定为语义输入扰动（如模糊、亮度、缩放）和离散严重级别（低、中、高）的结构化空间，支持查询任意组合下的模型鲁棒性；(2) 提出"部分模型断层扫描"方法，从仅涉及少量扰动的低阶投影中重构多扰动空间中的模型行为。通过训练辅助模型于最多两个扰动（低阶）的测试结果，避免执行指数级别的测试来估计涉及三个及以上扰动的高阶鲁棒性。

Result: 在五个图像和语言分类任务上的实验表明，TestifAI能够从低阶（1-2个扰动）观测中预测高阶（3-4个扰动）的测试结果，聚合鲁棒性估计误差小于7%，同时推理次数减少60-80%。

Conclusion: TestifAI通过将高阶扰动组合的鲁棒性测试转化为低阶观测的预测问题，实现了高效且准确的深度学习模型鲁棒性估计，大幅降低了测试成本，为安全关键场景中的AI系统验证提供了实用工具。

Abstract: As AI systems are increasingly deployed in safety-critical application domains (e.g., autonomous driving), associated risks increase too. Deep learning models underlying modern AI systems, therefore, must undergo thorough testing to ensure their correct behaviour. A single robustness test involves thousands of inferences to empirically verify if a model's outputs remain stable under a bounded perturbation of its inputs. However, existing testing frameworks lack the means to systematically explore and summarise robustness across a combinatorial space of perturbations.
  We propose TestifAI, a deep learning testing framework for efficient and accurate estimation of robustness against combinations of perturbations. TestifAI enables users to specify operational conditions as structured spaces of semantic input perturbations (e.g., image blur, brightness and zoom) and discrete severity levels (e.g., low, medium and high). Users can query model robustness for any combination (e.g., "low blur, high brightness, and medium zoom"). To achieve efficiency and accuracy, TestifAI introduces partial model tomography, a novel approach to reconstructing model behaviour in a multi-perturbation space from tests that apply only a small number of perturbations (lower-order projections). To estimate robustness against at least three perturbations, TestifAI trains an auxiliary model on the results of tests involving up to two perturbations only, avoiding execution of an exponential number of tests. Our experiments on five image and language classification tasks show that TestifAI can predict higher-order (3 and 4 perturbations) test outcomes from low-order (1 and 2 perturbations) observations with an aggregate robustness estimation error of less than 7%, while reducing the number of inferences by 60-80%.

</details>


### [239] [Breaking the weakest link to evade vision language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18938)
*Ilan Zini,Boussad Addad,Katarzyna Kapusta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）在对抗性逃逸攻击下的鲁棒性，提出了一种仅优化视觉编码器的梯度攻击方法，在不影响效果的前提下大幅降低计算成本，并在多个开源VLM上验证了小且人类不可察觉的扰动即可显著改变模型输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在现实世界和安全关键应用中被广泛部署，但其对对抗性威胁的鲁棒性，尤其是针对多模态对齐的逃逸攻击，尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出基于梯度的攻击方法，优化仅作用于VLM的视觉编码器而非整个多模态架构，以降低计算开销和资源需求；研究两种攻击设置：非目标攻击（破坏模型对原始图像的解释）和目标攻击（迫使模型生成与原始图像无关的特定语义描述）。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL、Granite-Vision、FastVLM和Phi-3.5-Vision等多个开源VLM上进行了评估，结果表明微小且人类不可察觉的扰动能够显著改变模型产生的文本解释。

Conclusion: 现代视觉语言模型对对抗性操纵存在明显脆弱性，需要加强多模态AI系统的鲁棒性和安全机制。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have recently emerged as a critical component of multimodal AI systems, enabling joint reasoning over visual and textual inputs in real-world and safety-critical applications. Despite their growing deployment, the robustness of VLMs against adversarial threats remains insufficiently explored, particularly in the context of evasion attacks targeting multimodal alignment. In this work, we investigate the vulnerability of VLMs to adversarial perturbations applied to visual inputs and study two attack settings: untargeted attacks, where the goal is to disrupt the model's interpretation of the original image, and targeted attacks, where the adversary aims to force the model to generate a specific semantic description unrelated to the original image. To efficiently generate adversarial examples, we propose a gradient-based attack method that performs optimization exclusively on the vision encoder of the VLM rather than on the entire multimodal architecture. This design significantly reduces the computational cost and resource requirements of the attack while maintaining strong effectiveness. We evaluate our approach on several open-source VLMs, including Qwen2.5-VL, Granite-Vision, FastVLM, and Phi-3.5-Vision, and show that small, human-imperceptible perturbations can substantially alter the textual interpretation produced by the models. Our findings highlight the vulnerability of modern VLMs to adversarial manipulation and emphasize the need for improved robustness and security mechanisms in multimodal AI systems.

</details>


### [240] [A Theory of Post-hoc Debate Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19002)
*Xiang Yin,Adam Dejl,Antonio Rago,Lihu Chen,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并测试了一种关于辩论裁决（debate judgement）的新理论，该理论适用于代理通过辩论提供正反观点支持其意见的所有场景。作者识别了辩论裁决需满足的多种形式化属性（可复现性、鲁棒性、基于事实性和可解释性），并在声明验证场景中对两种裁决方法（LLM作为法官的变体与计算论证的正式语义）进行了形式化和实验性探索。结果显示两种方法在准确性上相当，但LLM方法缺乏前者提供的正式保证，论证语义学被认为是辩论驱动AI中理想的原则性法官候选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辩论已成为提升代理AI性能、可解释性和用户参与度的有效方法，但在许多场景中，辩论结果的裁决由外部法官（通常也是LLM）事后决定，这种做法缺乏系统的理论和正式保证。本文旨在构建一个适用于所有代理辩论场景的裁决理论框架，以确保裁决过程具备可复现性、鲁棒性、基于事实性和可解释性。

Method: 作者首先识别了辩论裁决应满足的几类正式属性（可复现性、鲁棒性、基于事实性和可解释性），然后在声明验证场景中，对两种不同的裁决方法进行形式化分析和实验验证：（1）基于LLM作为法官的变体方法；（2）源自计算论证的正式语义学方法。

Result: 实验和形式化分析表明，两种裁决方法在准确性上表现相近，但LLM作为法官的方法可能缺乏正式语义学方法所能提供的正式保证。整体研究表明，论证语义学是辩论驱动AI中更有原则性的法官选择。

Conclusion: 在辩论驱动的AI系统中，基于计算论证的正式语义学比LLM作为法官更具优势，因为它提供正式保证且性能相当，是理想的原则性辩论裁决方案。

Abstract: Debates have recently emerged as a useful methodology for agentic AI to improve performance as well as to aid explainability and user engagement. For example, LLM-empowered agents may debate internally (with themselves) and/or externally (with other agents). In many settings where debates are used, debates' outcomes and resulting outputs are determined post-hoc by external judges, often LLMs. In this paper we develop and test a novel theory of debate judgement applicable to all settings where agents engage in debates by providing pros and cons for their opinions therein. Specifically, we identify a number of formal properties that debate judgement may be required to satisfy in general, as concerns reproducibility, robustness, groundedness and explainability. Then, we explore their satisfaction formally and/or experimentally, for claim verification settings, for two specific alternative debate judgement methods: variants of the LLMs as a judge idea and formal semantics drawn from computational argumentation. We show that the two methods give similar accuracy performances but the former may lack formal guarantees that the latter brings. Overall, our study indicates argumentation semantics as an ideal candidate for principled judges in debate-driven AI.

</details>


### [241] [Self-prompting and cross-model consensus enable reproducible data extraction from scientific literature with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19025)
*Valentin Romanov,Monique Bax,Steven Niederer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了前沿浏览器端大语言模型（LLM）从研究论文中提取高度情境化数据的能力，测试了四种递进式工作流：专家提示词提取、LLM自撰提示词、自主文献发现以及基于指南生成新数据集。研究发现LLM在数据提取上表现良好，但难以处理科学语境和细微差别；自主文献发现容易遗漏或产生幻觉引用；LLM生成的数据集接近人类专家水平但仍需人机协作。最终提出一种可审计的专家与模型分工方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从科研论文中准确提取精细、情境化的数据既耗时又费力，亟需自动化工具来减轻科研人员的工作负担，同时确保提取质量。

Method: 评估了前沿浏览器端LLM在四种递进式工作流中的表现：1）专家精心设计的提示词加研究文章的直接提取；2）简单指令下LLM自撰提示词的提取；3）自主文献发现；4）基于已发表指南创建新数据集。每种工作流均与人类专家表现进行对比。

Result: 前沿LLM在数据提取上表现良好，但在解释科学上下文和细微差别方面存在困难；LLM自撰提示词的效果几乎与专家编写的提示词相当；自主文献发现非常困难，智能体要么遗漏引用要么产生幻觉引用；LLM从指南生成的数据集与人类专家判断高度吻合，但仍需要人在闭环中参与。

Conclusion: 应建立一种可审计的分工模式：专家明确证据标准，模型交叉检查重复提取，研究人员解决争议案例。这提供了一条在保持专家监督的同时规模化科学数据整理的实用路径。

Abstract: Accurately extracting nuanced, contextualized data from research articles is laborious and time intensive. Here, we investigate the performance of frontier, browser-based large language models (LLMs) to extract highly contextualized information. We demonstrate four escalating workflows, 1) given an expert curated prompt and research articles, most frontier LLMs perform well at data extraction, however can struggle with interpreting scientific context and nuance, 2) given simple instructions, LLMs can author their own prompts which were almost as eNective as expert-written prompts, 3) autonomous discovery of research literature was diNicult, agents either missed or hallucinated references, and 4) LLMs can create new datasets from published guidelines that closely match human-expert judges, but still require a human-in-the-loop. Together, these findings define an auditable division of labour in which experts specify the evidence standard, models cross-check repeated extractions and researchers resolve disputed cases, providing a practical route to scaling scientific data curation without relinquishing expert oversight.

</details>


### [242] [Eureka: Task-Conditioned Meta-Agent Orchestration for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19047)
*Alizer Wong,Heng Cui,Yi Tan,Xiongchao Zhan,Liang Lin,Yuxiang Guo,Zhaorong Dai,Zixin Zeng,Wenyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Eureka，一种任务条件化的元智能体架构，将长时程任务编译为具有显式接受语义的动态义务图。通过滚动时域规划、架构提升和最小充分编译形成宏智能体，通过成本-收益门控演化处理瓶颈。实验表明Eureka在170个递归任务上全部完成，生成3,948个证书且无误接受，压缩输入token 57.8%，减少65.38%重计算，16,000个并发执行保持一致性。该架构还成功实例化了理论发现智能体和数学/猜想智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统在处理长时程、复杂认知结构的科学任务时，性能不仅取决于基础模型本身，还取决于架构能否匹配任务的内在认知结构。作者旨在验证这一假设，并构建一种能够动态适应任务结构的元智能体架构。

Method: Eureka采用任务条件化的Meta-Agent架构，核心组件包括：(1) 将长时程任务编译为带显式接受语义的动态义务图；(2) 通过滚动时域规划、架构提升和最小充分编译形成具有专门状态、记忆、算子、工具、验证器和局部拓扑的宏智能体；(3) 当瓶颈复发时，通过成本-收益门控演化在约束下更新局部架构。理论上建立了关于遗憾、规划失效、摊销、子树接口、可串行化和验证的结果。

Result: 实验结果显示：Eureka完成170/170递归任务，生成3,948个证书且无误接受；主动上下文压缩将中位数输入从9,490 tokens降至4,005；增量处理在12,000个任务中避免65.38%重计算；16,000个并发执行保持一致序列化。在科学应用上，理论发现智能体在量子过程和时空理论方面取得结构性成果；数学/猜想智能体识别出黎曼猜想研究的瓶颈，并将Suzuki局部Weil二次型的正性证书推进到0 < a ≤ 69/200 = 0.345，达到(log 2)/2的约99.55%。

Conclusion: 科学智能体的能力不仅取决于基础模型，更取决于架构能否形成以匹配任务的认知结构。Eureka通过动态架构形成和演化，验证了这一核心假设，并为构建适配任务认知结构的智能体系统提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: We present Eureka, a task-conditioned Meta-Agent architecture that compiles long-horizon tasks into dynamic obligation graphs with explicit acceptance semantics. During execution, Eureka forms Macro-Agents with specialized state, memory, operators, tools, verifiers, and local topology via receding-horizon planning, architecture promotion, and minimal-sufficient compilation. When bottlenecks recur, cost-benefit-gated evolution updates the local architecture under constraints. Theoretically, we establish results on regret, planning invalidation, amortization, subtree interfaces, serializability, and verification. Experimentally, Eureka completes 170/170 recursive tasks and generates 3,948 certificates with no false acceptances. Active context compresses median input from 9,490 to 4,005 tokens; incremental processing avoids 65.38% recomputation across 12,000 tasks; 16,000 concurrent executions serialize consistently. The same Meta-Agent instantiates a Theory-Discovery Agent and a Math/Conjecture Agent. The former yields structural results in quantum-process and spacetime theory. The latter identifies bottlenecks in Riemann Hypothesis research and advances a positivity certificate for Suzuki's localized Weil quadratic form to 0 < a <= 69/200 = 0.345, reaching ~99.55% of (log 2)/2. These results suggest that scientific-agent capability depends not only on the base model but on whether an architecture can be formed to match the task's cognitive structure.

</details>


### [243] [What is Missing from AI Post-Training AI: An Empirical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19072)
*Joy Jia Yin Lim,Xin Huang,Hao Peng,Yaxi Lu,Xin Cong,Zhong Zhang,Maosong Sun,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体在端到端后训练中的能力，发现智能体在训练策略层面存在"策略锁定"问题——策略在开始时就被固定，剩余预算全部用于局部调整。通过三种干预实验（经验引导、人工指导、推理计算）证明，智能体缺乏的是在执行过程中自发重新评估策略的机制，而非经验、指导或推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体能够端到端地执行后训练（写代码、启动训练、评估检查点），但作者认为这混淆了两个层次的能力：执行层面的能力和策略层面的能力。作者希望系统性地探讨智能体在这两种能力上的实际表现及其差距的根源。

Method: 作者分析了大规模公开的后训练轨迹语料，发现策略在开始时即被锁定。随后通过三种递增的干预实验验证假设：1）经验驱动的脚手架提升执行能力；2）人工指导重新定向初始策略；3）增加推理计算资源。在GSM8K和HumanEval等基准上进行了广泛实验。

Result: 实验结果表明：经验脚手架在执行层面显著提升（GSM8K +12.6分，HumanEval +40.8分）但策略保持不变；人工指导能有效重定向初始策略但训练开始后仍会陷入局部调整循环；额外推理计算在简单任务上有效但在最难任务上几乎无收益。

Conclusion: 智能体缺乏的不是经验、指导或推理算力，而是缺乏在执行过程中自发重新评估和修订策略的机制。这指出未来工作应聚焦于赋予智能体策略级自我反思与动态调整的能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can now post-train an LLM end-to-end. They can write code, launch training, evaluate checkpoints, and improve downstream performance, raising the prospect of AI-for-AI. We argue that this picture conflates two distinct capabilities: execution-level capability, iterating within a selected training strategy; and strategy-level capability, revising the high-level judgment as experimental evidence accumulates. Analyzing a large corpus of publicly released post-training trajectories, we find that across different tasks, the agent's training strategy is locked in at the very beginning, and the entire remaining budget is spent on local adjustments within the selected strategy. We then examine three natural explanations--missing experience, missing guidance, and insufficient reasoning--with escalating interventions. Extensive experiments show that (1) an experience-driven scaffold improves execution across the board (+12.6 points on GSM8K and +40.8 on HumanEval) but leaves the strategy static; (2) human guidance effectively redirects the initial strategy, yet the agent falls back into local adjustment loops once training starts; and (3) additional inference compute pays off on easier tasks but yields almost no gain on the hardest one. In conclusion, what agents lack is neither experience, guidance, nor reasoning compute, but a mechanism for spontaneously reevaluating their strategy during execution.

</details>


### [244] [Robust Risk Under Evolving Uncertainty: A Wasserstein Counterpart of the Entropic Value-at-Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19073)
*Deep Kumar Ganguly,Jan Křetínský*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用最优传输（Wasserstein）球替代相对熵球来定义一个新的风险度量——Wasserstein熵值风险（WEVaR），该度量能够覆盖名义模型认为不可能但实际可达的灾难事件，并通过信念熵驱动传输半径得到一个封闭形式的鲁棒动态规划算子，实现"谨慎时保守、自信时大胆"的安全智能体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的熵值风险（EVaR）通过相对熵球来刻画模型不确定性，但相对熵球无法触及名义模型认为不可能（概率为零）的灾难场景，而这恰恰是安全智能体必须对冲的尾部风险。因此需要一个能够覆盖更广不确定性范围的替代风险度量。

Method: 采用最优传输（Wasserstein）球替代相对熵球来定义新的风险度量WEVaR；推导其变分对偶（逆温度变为传输价格）；通过信念熵驱动传输半径，构造封闭形式的鲁棒动态规划算子，使谨慎程度随信念收敛而收缩；验证对偶关系并给出安全夹层（safety sandwich）与安全切换（safety switch）的理论保证。

Result: WEVaR具有与熵值公式对称的变分对偶结构，在风险层级中占据明确位置，能够覆盖熵值度量忽略的可达灾难事件；两种对偶均经数值验证。由信念熵驱动的动态规划算子具有封闭形式，谨慎程度随信念锐化而递减，并给出认证的安全性上下界和尖锐的安全切换机制。

Conclusion: Wasserstein熵值风险为安全强化学习提供了一种更严谨的鲁棒风险度量，既能覆盖不可忽略的灾难场景，又能随学习进程自适应调整谨慎程度，在理论保证下实现从保守到大胆的安全过渡。

Abstract: An agent still learning its environment should be cautious while ignorant and bold once confident. The entropic value-at-risk captures this through a robust-optimization identity---a confidence level fixes the radius of a relative-entropy ball of alternative models---but that ball cannot reach catastrophes the nominal deems impossible, precisely what a safe agent must hedge. We instead use an optimal-transport ball and study the coherent risk measure it induces, the Wasserstein entropic value-at-risk. It has a variational dual mirroring the entropic formula (an inverse temperature becomes a transport price), occupies a definite place in the risk hierarchy, and provably accounts for the reachable catastrophes the entropic measure ignores; we verify both dualities numerically. Driving the transport radius by belief entropy then yields a closed-form robust dynamic-programming operator whose caution contracts as the belief sharpens, with a certified safety sandwich and a sharp safety switch.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

### [245] [Contracting for LLM Delegation: Moral Hazard in Technology and Effort Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18232)
*Nanda Kishore Sreenivas,Kate Larson*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文扩展了标准委托-代理框架，将其应用于代理在多种技术（如不同能力的LLM）和努力水平（如token预算）间进行选择的场景，并推导出最优线性契约，通过基准测试校准验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型时代，Agent（代理）需要同时选择模型技术和投入努力水平（如token预算），这种二维隐藏行动选择场景使得传统委托-代理框架不再适用，需要新的理论框架来分析委托方如何设计激励机制。

Method: 将产出质量与努力的关系建模为凹的饱和函数，该函数依赖于Agent隐藏的二维行动选择（技术选择与努力分配）。推导委托方的最优线性契约，并利用开放权重LLM在MATH和MMLUPro基准上的配对数据进行模型校准，使用bandit算法验证理论与实际策略的一致性。

Result: 证明了Agent的最优反应由触发技术切换的阈值奖励份额所表征；校准实验表明，委托方和Agent在使用bandit算法探索该环境时，收敛到的策略与理论均衡高度一致。

Conclusion: 简单的线性契约能够有效激励复杂的、具备技术感知能力的委托代理工作流，为智能体协作中的激励机制设计提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: We extend the standard Principal-Agent framework to scenarios where the Agent selects from a suite of technologies, each characterized by a distinct cost-capability profile. This framework is increasingly critical in the era of Large Language Models (LLMs), where Agents choose both a model and an associated effort level (e.g., token budget). We model the relationship between output quality and effort as a concave, saturating function, which depends on the Agent's hidden two-dimensional action choice balancing technology selection and effort allocation. We derive the optimal linear contract for the Principal, demonstrating that the Agent's best response is characterized by a threshold reward share that triggers technology switching. Finally, we calibrate our model using open-weight LLM pairings across the MATH and MMLUPro benchmarks. We show that both Principal and Agent, when employing bandit algorithms to navigate this environment, converge to strategies that closely align with our theoretical equilibrium. These results suggest that simple linear contracts can effectively incentivize complex, technology-aware delegation in agentic workflows.

</details>


### [246] [A Locally Deployable Tool-Grounded LLM Multi-agent Framework for Automating Methane Emission Analysis and Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18473)
*Yang Yan,Zifan Zhou,Xuan Wang,Erum Hassan,Bilguunzaya Mijiddorj,Jie Cao,Bin Li,Binbin Weng*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向低成本甲烷场监测的可本地部署、基于工具的多智能体大语言模型（LLM）框架，通过LLM智能体协调现场测量、气象数据、传感器处理、高斯羽流反演和报告生成等工作流环节，而非直接估算甲烷浓度。在废水处理厂、垃圾填埋场和油气场地等多样真实环境中，该框架实现了92.0%的工作流路由与参数提取准确率、85.0%的排放率估算与羽流预测成功率，以及95.0%的可编辑报告生成成功率，将工作时间从小时级缩短至分钟级，并支持本地化处理以保护敏感数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲烷场监测需要整合采样设计、气象解释、传感器处理、羽流分析、可视化和报告等多个环节，但这些步骤通常分散在不同专家驱动的工作流中，导致效率低下、协调成本高，并存在数据安全风险。

Method: 开发了一个可本地部署、基于工具的多智能体LLM框架，LLM智能体作为工作流协调器，连接现场测量、气象数据、确定性传感器处理例程、高斯羽流反演和报告生成。框架不直接估算甲烷浓度或排放，而是通过工具调用协调各处理模块。

Result: 在多种真实环境（废水处理厂、垃圾填埋场、油气场站）中，框架实现了92.0%的工作流路由和参数提取准确率、85.0%的排放率估算和羽流预测成功率、95.0%的可编辑报告生成成功率。相比手动和通用LLM辅助工作流，工作时间从小时级降至分钟级，降低了对人工协调和提示工程的需求，并保留可追溯的羽流输出，大部分处理可在本地完成以减少云端数据暴露。

Conclusion: 基于工具的LLM协调可以降低甲烷场监测在时间、人力、可用性和数据安全方面的障碍，为低成本甲烷传感和现场监测活动提供了一种高效、安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Methane field monitoring requires the integration of sampling design, meteorological interpretation, sensor processing, plume analysis, visualization, and reporting, but these steps are often distributed across separate expert-driven workflows. We developed a locally deployable, tool-grounded large language model (LLM) multi-agent framework for our low-cost methane sensing and field-monitoring campaigns. The framework uses LLM agents as workflow coordinators that link field measurements, meteorological data, deterministic sensor-processing routines, Gaussian plume inversion, and report generation, rather than directly estimating methane concentrations or emissions. Extensive field deployments across diverse real-world environments (e.g., wastewater treatment facilities, landfills, and oil and gas sites) demonstrate that our framework can achieve 92.0\% accuracy in workflow routing and parameter extraction, 85.0\% success in emission-rate estimation and plume prediction, and 95.0\% success in generating editable reports under practical operating conditions. Compared with manual and general-purpose LLM-assisted workflows, it reduced workflow time from hours-level to minutes-level, lowered manual coordination and prompt-engineering requirements, and retained traceable plume-based outputs. In addition, most processing can be performed locally, reducing exposure of sensitive facility and field data to cloud services. These results indicate that tool-grounded LLM coordination can reduce the time, labor, usability, and data-security barriers of methane field monitoring.

</details>


### [247] [Bayesian Partner Modelling enables Adaptive Replanning for LLM Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18490)
*Harsh Goel,Aditya Sai Ellendula,Vaishnav Tadiparthi,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Sandeep P. Chinchali*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了BayesBeliefAgent，一种将层级LLM规划器与贝叶斯追踪模块相结合的多智能体系统，能够高效识别队友技能变化并按需重规划，显著缩小信念-行动差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在与技能中途变化的队友协作时表现不佳，现有方法要么被动感知变化但反应缓慢，要么不加区别地频繁重规划，缺乏高效且精准的队友追踪与自适应机制。

Method: BayesBeliefAgent由两部分组成：层级LLM规划器负责多步技能的执行，贝叶斯追踪模块推断队友当前技能。核心机制是"矛盾条件控制"——仅当队友行为与推断技能直接矛盾时才中断当前技能并触发重规划，而非持续重规划。

Result: 在Overcooked基准环境中，该方法显著缩小了信念-行动差距，同时所需的重规划次数比启发式方法少一个数量级，实现了效率与适应性的平衡。

Conclusion: 基于矛盾触发的条件控制比持续重规划更高效且更有效，贝叶斯追踪与层级规划的配对是解决动态队友协作问题的可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent Large Language Model (LLM) systems often struggle to collaborate with new teammates whose strategies shift mid-task. Because agents execute multi-step or temporally extended skills, they frequently continue executing outdated plans long after public evidence shows that a partner has changed its skill. Existing methods either treat partner tracking as passive context-leaving the agent aware of the shift but slow to act-or replan indiscriminately. We introduce BayesBeliefAgent, which pairs a hierarchical LLM planner with a Bayesian tracking module. Rather than replanning constantly, our agent interrupts its current skill only when a partner's actions directly contradict the inferred skill. Beyond standard reward, we evaluate performance using replanning efficiency and the belief-action gap: the fraction of total decisions where an agent with a correct partner estimate executes a non-complementary skill. Across benchmark Overcooked environments, contradiction-conditioned control drastically narrows this belief-action gap while requiring an order of magnitude fewer replans than heuristic methods

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [248] [Demo: tfdrift - A Severity Taxonomy and Risk Classification Framework for Infrastructure Drift Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18173)
*Sudarshan Bhagvanthakur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对基础设施即代码（IaC）配置漂移的通用严重性分类体系，将漂移变化分为四个风险层级，并实现了开源框架tfdrift。在150多个AWS Terraform工作区上的评估表明，严重性过滤可将告警量减少73%，同时保留94%的安全相关变更。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基础设施配置漂移检测方法将所有变化等同对待，导致告警疲劳，使运维人员错过关键的安全修改。需要一种基于风险严重性的分类方法来区分不同重要程度的漂移。

Method: 提出一个通用的严重性分类体系，将基础设施漂移按资源类型和属性级影响分为四个风险层级。基于该分类体系实现开源框架tfdrift，包含60多条可配置规则，覆盖AWS、Azure和GCP资源模式（评估聚焦AWS）。

Result: 在150多个AWS Terraform工作区上评估，严重性过滤将告警量减少73%，同时保留94%的安全相关变更，为基于ML的告警过滤提供了一种轻量级替代方案。

Conclusion: 基于严重性分类的漂移过滤方法能够有效减少告警噪声，同时保持对安全关键变更的高灵敏度，是一种实用且轻量的告警管理方案。

Abstract: Infrastructure as Code (IaC) tools like Terraform have become the standard for declarative cloud resource management, yet configuration drift, where deployed infrastructure diverges from its declared state, remains a persistent operational and security challenge. Current detection approaches treat all changes equivalently, contributing to alert fatigue that causes operators to miss security-critical modifications. We propose a generalized severity taxonomy for infrastructure drift that classifies changes into four risk tiers based on resource type and attribute-level impact. We implement this taxonomy in tfdrift, an open-source classification framework with 60+ configurable rules covering AWS, Azure, and GCP resource patterns (evaluation reported here is AWS-focused). Evaluation across 150+ AWS Terraform workspaces demonstrates that severity filtering reduces alert volume by 73% while retaining 94% of security-relevant changes, offering a lightweight alternative to ML-based alert filtering. tfdrift is available at github.com/sudarshan8417/tfdrift.

</details>


### [249] [Reproducibility is Not Enough: Artifact Verifiability in Decentralized-Build Package Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18180)
*Oreofe Solarin,Kelechi Kalu,James C. Davis,Paschal Amusuo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了四个流行的去中心化构建包生态系统中工件可验证性的问题，提出了一个独立验证者模型和工件比较模型（含分级等价级别），并实现了工件验证流水线来测量各生态系统的可验证性。研究发现，除构建确定性外，可验证性还受限于缺失的源代码和构建元数据、隐式发布转换及非常规构建实践等因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复现且可验证的构建能通过让独立方检测被破坏的构建或发布管道来增强分布式软件工件的信任，但工件验证不仅需要确定性构建，验证者还必须恢复源码状态、构建环境、依赖关系和构建指令。去中心化构建生态系统因异构工具、维护者控制的工作流和碎片化元数据而使验证变得困难，目前尚不清楚这些生态系统中工件能被独立验证的频率。

Method: 定义了仅依赖注册表可派生元数据的独立验证者模型，以及具有分级等价级别的工件比较模型；将这些模型实现为工件验证流水线，并用于测量四个目标生态系统的工件可验证性。

Result: 结果表明，除构建确定性之外，可验证性还受限于缺失的源代码和构建元数据、隐式发布转换以及非常规构建实践。出处证明和嵌入的VCS元数据能改善验证，但并未提供完整的重建规范。

Conclusion: 研究确定了具体的元数据缺口和生态系统层面的变革需求，以使工件验证在包注册表规模下变得切实可行。

Abstract: Reproducible and verifiable builds increase trust in distributed software artifacts by enabling independent parties to detect artifacts produced by compromised build or release pipelines. However, artifact verification requires more than deterministic builds: a verifier must also recover the source state, build environment, dependencies, and build instructions that produced the artifact. Decentralized-build ecosystems make this difficult because artifacts are produced through heterogeneous tools, maintainer-controlled workflows, and fragmented metadata. As a result, it remains unclear how often artifacts in these ecosystems can be independently verified.
  This paper studies artifact verifiability across four popular decentralized-build package ecosystems. We define an independent verifier model that relies only on registry-derivable metadata and an artifact comparison model with tiered equivalence levels. We implement these models in an Artifact Verification Pipeline and use it to measure artifact verifiability across the target ecosystems. Our results show that, beyond build determinism, verifiability is limited by missing source and build metadata, implicit release transformations, and unconventional build practices. Provenance attestations and embedded VCS metadata improve verification, but they do not provide complete rebuild specifications. These findings identify concrete metadata gaps and ecosystem-level changes needed to make artifact verification practical at package-registry scale.

</details>


### [250] [What Makes Software Issue Resolution Tasks Difficult for Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18280)
*Ebtesam Al-Haque,Brittany Johnson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个测量框架,通过分析软件任务的结构属性(补丁、仓库、提示词)来系统量化编码任务难度与代理成功率的对应关系。基于最大开放编码代理轨迹数据集CoderForge-Preview进行大规模实证研究,发现任务难度可从静态特征高度预测(AUC=0.863),主要由补丁碎片化和仓库规模驱动,提示词语义特征在中等难度任务中成为关键贡献因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管代理系统在基准测试上快速饱和,但基准分数难以解释,主要因为缺乏对任务难度的控制和表征。目前对什么使任务更难、任务难度能否从静态属性预测缺乏了解,因此需要建立系统化的测量框架。

Method: 在最大的开放编码代理轨迹数据集CoderForge-Preview上进行大规模实证研究,从任务补丁、仓库和提示词三个维度提取特征,使用集成方法、SHAP归因和效应量分析评估各特征对任务结果的预测能力。

Result: 任务难度可从静态特征显著预测(AUC=0.863),主要由补丁碎片化和仓库规模驱动。提示词语义特征在中等难度任务中成为重要贡献因素,揭示了难度的分层结构。

Conclusion: 问题解决任务的难度编码在其结构中,这支持静态的、任务前(pre-hoc)的难度估计,为构建难度可控的代理评估基准奠定基础。

Abstract: Background. Advances in agentic systems are simultaneously, and rapidly, saturating benchmarks. Despite this often discussed phenomena, benchmark scores remain difficult to interpret due to the lack of control and characterization of task difficulty. More specifically, we currently have little understanding of what makes one task harder than another, and to what extent task difficulty is predictable from static task properties. Aims. We propose a measurement framework to investigate and systematically quantify what structural properties of software tasks correspond to agent success rates for issue resolution tasks. Method. We conducted a large scale empirical study on CoderForge-Preview, the largest open dataset of coding agent trajectories to date, by extracting features across task patch, repository and prompt. We evaluated the predictive power of each feature against task outcomes using ensemble methods, SHAP attribution, and effect size analysis. Results We found that task difficulty is substantially predictable from static features (AU C = 0.863) and is largely driven by patch fragmentation and repository scale. Prompt linguistic features become visible among top contributors for tasks in the mid-band, revealing a layered structure of difficulty. Conclusion. The difficulty of an issue resolution task is encoded in its structure. This enables static, pre-hoc difficulty estimation and lays the groundwork for difficulty-controlled benchmark construction for evaluation of agents.

</details>


### [251] [Engine-Transfer-Bench: An Evidence-Based Benchmark for Document Compilation Engine Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18329)
*Prajwal S. Venkateshmurthy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Engine-Transfer-Bench（ETB），一个用于文档编译引擎选择的基准测试框架，包含1,784个开源文档、四个任务维度、固定测试环境及跨操作系统结果。研究发现Tectonic在GitHub Actions上成功率稳定（96.3-97.2%），而传统TeX Live系列引擎因发行版策略差异波动达12-20个百分点；在702个可移植LaTeX文档上所有引擎均100%成功，失败集中在107个引擎特定模板中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏统一的框架来指导用户在pdfLaTeX、XeLaTeX、LuaLaTeX、Tectonic、Typst和pandoc等文档编译引擎之间进行选择，导致选择过程依赖经验而非系统性评估。

Method: 构建ETB基准测试框架：（1）收集1,784个开源文档；（2）设计四个任务（可靠性、延迟、文本一致性、失败分析）；（3）使用固定的测试环境（pinned harness）；（4）在GitHub Actions上跨macOS、Ubuntu和Windows三个操作系统共执行4,211次编译；（5）提出S_pdf文本一致性度量并验证其准确性。

Result: Tectonic成功率稳定在96.3-97.2%（波动小于0.9个百分点），而经典TeX Live系列引擎根据发行版策略（Ubuntu apt、MiKTeX自动安装、macOS BasicTeX）波动12-20个百分点。702个可移植LaTeX文档上所有引擎成功率达100%。S_pdf文本一致性度量在50对验证中达到94%的精确度。失败主要是架构性的（字体、布局、资源），而非缺失包。

Conclusion: 对于可移植LaTeX文档，延迟成为引擎选择的主要因素；对于特定模板，需考虑引擎的架构性限制。论文发布了ETB、ETB-Porta、推荐器和可移植性门控工具，以及跨操作系统公共测试基础设施，填补了引擎选择缺乏统一框架的空白。

Abstract: There is no shared framework for selecting among document compilation engines (pdfLaTeX, XeLaTeX, LuaLaTeX, Tectonic, Typst, and pandoc PDF backends). We present Engine-Transfer-Bench (ETB): 1,784 open documents, four tasks covering reliability, latency, text consistency, and failures, a pinned harness, and host-tagged multi-OS results. On GitHub Actions (N=4,211 compiles per host across macOS, Ubuntu, and Windows), Tectonic success is stable within 0.9 percentage points (96.3-97.2%), whereas classic TeX Live-style engines vary by 12-20 percentage points according to distribution policy (Ubuntu apt, MiKTeX auto-install, or macOS BasicTeX). On 702 portable LaTeX documents, the tested engines succeed at 100%, making latency the primary selection factor; failures concentrate in 107 engine-specific templates. Within ETB, failures are architectural, involving fonts, layout, and assets, rather than missing packages on a provisioned host. A 50-pair validation of the S_pdf text-consistency metric achieves 94% precision for real content divergence. We release ETB, ETB-Porta, a recommender and portability gate, and a public cross-OS harness as shared infrastructure.

</details>


### [252] [One Gate Is Not Enough: Composing Stateful Pre-Action Controls for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18360)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究多门控（权威、资源、证据）同时控制下的智能体AI系统，提出"补救诱导的控制耦合"问题——一个门控的补救操作会改变另一个门控评估的状态，导致先前判断失效。作者给出"补救后重新门控"协议恢复单动作健全性，并证明两个补救算子（证据替换和资源预算降级）不可交换，需要将补救顺序纳入控制面语义。同时揭示证据缓冲区的"当前可接受性不等于未来引用可信性"漏洞，提出两种缓解措施但无法完全消除风险。实验在三个公开引擎组合上验证了CH1-CH6决策规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统在动作执行前受多个控制门（权威门、资源门、证据门）同时约束，每个门可驳回、降级或补救动作。然而一个门控施加的补救可能改变其他门控后续评估所依赖的动作、证据或上下文状态，导致其他门控基于过期信息的判断失效。这种控制间耦合尚未被形式化研究，可能导致安全门控被绕过或做出错误放行决策。

Method: 本文采用形式化方法定义"补救诱导的控制耦合"，提出"补救-重新门控"协议以恢复每动作健全性。作者证明两个已实现的补救算子（证据替换与资源预算降级）不满足交换律，使用有限模型检查器构造了反例实例。同时还分析了受管辖证据缓冲区（信任最近写入）的脆弱性，建立了门控结果正权重线性聚合可补偿成员否决的精确条件，并提出统一的跨控制证据集。

Result: CH1-CH5在所有30个预注册随机种子上满足其注册决策规则；CH6在W1工作流下满足但在较小的W2工作流下不满足。有限模型检查找到补救算子不可交换的具体反例。正向权重线性聚合可补偿否决的精确条件已确立。两个缓解措施可减少但无法消除证据缓冲区投毒暴露。

Conclusion: 该研究是机制演示（在开放数据加合成元数据层上），不声称反映生产环境的普遍性。核心结论是：补救顺序不是实现细节而是控制面语义的一部分；当前证据可接受性不保证未来引用的可信性；组合多个检测引擎不会产生新的检测覆盖范围。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems take consequential actions governed by more than one pre-action control at once: authority, resource, and evidence gates that can admit, degrade, or remediate an action before it executes. This paper's central object is remediation-induced control coupling: a remediation applied by one control can change the action, evidence, or context another control evaluates, invalidating that control's earlier judgment. We formalize this coupling and give a remediate-and-regate protocol that restores per-action soundness in the current bounded, idempotent setting under its stated assumptions. We further show that the two implemented remediation operators (evidence substitution and resource-budget downroute) do not commute -- a finite-model checker finds concrete counterexample instances -- making remediation order part of the control-plane semantics rather than an implementation detail. A governed evidence buffer that trusts its own most recent admitted write is a further instance of the same problem at the level of state -- current admissibility does not imply future reference trustworthiness -- and is vulnerable to poisoning from declared-uncovered defect classes; two mitigations reduce, not eliminate, that exposure. Supporting results establish the exact condition under which positive-weight linear aggregation of gate outcomes can compensate a member veto, a unified cross-control Evidence Set, and that composition manufactures no new detection coverage, reported honestly. Empirically, on a deterministic open-data artifact composing three published engines unmodified, CH1-CH5 meet their registered decision rules across all 30 pre-registered seeds; CH6 does so under W1 but not under the smaller W2 workflow, reported as such. This is a mechanism demonstration on open payload data with a synthetic metadata layer, not a claim about production prevalence.

</details>


### [253] [Building real-time digital twin instances with Function+Data Flow: user evaluation and extension for iterative pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18480)
*Eduardo de Conto,Blaise Genest,Arvind Easwaran,Nicholas Ng,Shweta Menon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为Function+Data Flow（FDF）的视觉领域特定语言（DSL），用于在数字孪生（DT）系统中显式表示和组合AI/ML模型管道，并实现了集成建模环境DesCartes Builder。通过实证用户研究评估了该工具的可访问性和可靠性，结果表明其在目标用户（领域专家）中具有良好的可用性。基于研究发现，作者提出了FDF的层级扩展H-FDF，以支持更复杂的迭代和模块化管道（如双训练）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生系统越来越多地依赖AI/ML管道来构建实时模型，但工程化这些管道仍然是临时性的：管道难以指定、验证和复用，缺乏专用工具支持。作者旨在解决这一痛点，使AI驱动的数字孪生开发更加规范和系统化。

Method: 作者提出了FDF——一种视觉领域特定语言（DSL），显式表示函数（ML模型）及其组合与复用；在此基础上实现了集成建模环境DesCartes Builder，支持基于FDF的数字孪生合成与验证。随后开展了实证用户研究，参与者使用该工具实现一个实时数字孪生原型，通过定量与定性方法衡量感知可用性和功能充分性。基于研究结果，进一步提出H-FDF（层级扩展）以支持迭代和模块化管道。

Result: 研究结果表明DesCartes Builder和FDF在不同用户群体中达到良好的可用性水平，尤其适合目标受众（领域专家）。研究还发现了工具和FDF框架的具体优势及待改进之处。

Conclusion: 集成、模型驱动的平台是将AI驱动的数字孪生工程转变为规范化建模实践的有前景方向。H-FDF的提出进一步支持更复杂数字孪生管道的形式化规范，为未来发展和实际应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) increasingly leverage artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) pipelines, both to build real-time DTs from high-fidelity simulations and to instantiate them with historical data. However, engineering these pipelines remains largely ad-hoc: pipelines are hard to specify, validate, and reuse, with scarce dedicated tooling. Function+Data Flow (FDF) addresses this by defining a visual domain-specific language (DSL) that represents functions (ML models) explicitly, enabling their composition and reuse. We implemented FDF in DesCartes Builder, an integrated modeling environment supporting FDF-based DT synthesis and validation.
  In this paper, we report on an empirical user study evaluating whether FDF and DesCartes Builder can make AI-based DT development more accessible and reliable. Participants implemented a representative real-time DT prototype within DesCartes Builder, and we measured perceived usability and feature adequacy through quantitative and qualitative measures. Our results indicate that DesCartes Builder and FDF achieve a good level of usability across a broad range of potential users, and particularly for the intended audience of domain experts. The study additionally surfaces concrete strengths and areas for improvement of both the tool and the underlying FDF framework. Informed by these findings, we propose H-FDF, a Hierarchical extension of FDF supporting iterative and modular pipelines, enabling the formal specification of more complex DT pipelines such as dual training. Our findings suggest that integrated, model-driven platforms are a promising direction to transform AI-based DT engineering into a disciplined modeling practice.

</details>


### [254] [SemaPLC: A Project-Grounded, Verification-Gated Agent Harness for PLC Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18565)
*Yanlun Tu,Huacan Wang,Ziyue Zhou,Jie Zhou,Ningyan Zhu,Ge Chen,Wangyi Chen,Tengfei Zhou,Yifan Zhou,Dasheng Yang,Xiaofeng Mou,Hui Zhang,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SemaPLC，一个基于项目上下文且由验证门控的智能体框架，用于生成可编程逻辑控制器（PLC）代码。与传统仅依赖模型自我判断不同，SemaPLC仅在通过规范化外部检查（涵盖规格、编译和实时运行行为）后才宣布任务完成。在117个独立POU任务和65个项目上下文任务上，SemaPLC在所有七种模型上均达到最高的严格验证通过率（平均72.6%），尤其在动态行为测试中以52.2分大幅超越基线（22.4-31.4分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型已能生成独立PLC程序组织单元（POU），但这些生成的逻辑能否集成到现有PLC项目中并正确运行，此前仅在有限测试中验证过。现有方法往往在模型自认为输出充分时就停止，缺乏严格的外部验证机制。

Method: SemaPLC是一个项目锚定且验证门控的智能体框架，由常规工具组装而成，但由严格的完成规则约束。它不是当模型判断自身输出充分时停止，而是仅在日志记录的外部检查确认后才宣布任务完成。这些检查涵盖三个层面：规格检查、编译检查和实时运行时行为检查。行为验证通过将生成的逻辑与参考逻辑部署到真实PLC运行时并比较执行轨迹来实现。

Result: 在117个独立POU任务上，SemaPLC在所有七种模型上达到最高的严格验证通过率（平均72.6%）。在65个项目上下文任务上，在集成编译、静态行为和动态行为方面均达到最高平均值。所有方法在静态得分上仅相差10分以内，而动态得分将基线（22.4-31.4）与SemaPLC（52.2）明显区分开来。

Conclusion: 验证门控的框架在每个层面都提升了平均值，尤其在运行时层面提升最为显著。执行验证而非静态评分，才是检验生成的控制逻辑是否真正有效的可靠标准。SemaPLC已开源。

Abstract: Programmable logic controllers (PLCs) run industrial plants, and large language models can already generate independent program organization units (POUs) for them. Whether such logic integrates into an existing PLC project and then runs correctly has been checked only in limited tests. We present \textsc{SemaPLC}, a project-grounded and verification-gated agent harness assembled from conventional tools but governed by a strict completion rule. Rather than stopping when the model judges its own output adequate, \textsc{SemaPLC} declares a task complete only when logged external checks confirm it. Those checks cover the specification, the compilation, and the behavior on a live runtime. On 117 independent-POU tasks matching existing benchmarks, it attains the highest strict verified pass rate on all seven models (72.6\% mean). On a project-context track of 65 tasks whose generated logic must compile and run inside a real project, it attains the highest mean on integrated compilation, static behavior, and dynamic behavior. Of the three layers, dynamic behavior is the most revealing. We measure it by deploying the generated and the reference logic to a live PLC runtime and comparing their executed traces. All methods fall within 10 static points of one another, whereas dynamic scores separate them sharply, from 22.4 to 31.4 for the baselines against 52.2 for \textsc{SemaPLC}. Overall, our verification-gated harness raises the mean at every layer and most sharply at runtime. Execution, not static scoring, is the faithful test of whether generated control logic actually works. \textsc{SemaPLC} is open-sourced at https://github.com/midea-ai/SemaPLC.

</details>


### [255] [AppEval: A Unified Benchmark for LLM-Based Mobile Application Repair in ArkTS, Swift, and Kotlin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18588)
*Bang Xie,Hao Liu,Zhenyu Shi,Yonghao Zhang,Senjian Zhang,Zhiyuan Peng,Xin Yin,Chenhao Ying,Yuan Luo,Wei Chen,Haiming Jin,Shaocong Long,Xu Liu,Zhe Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AppEval是一个面向移动应用修复的基准测试与原生工具链评估框架，覆盖HarmonyOS/ArkTS、iOS/Swift和Android/Kotlin三大平台。每个任务通过隐藏行为测试与参考修复分离，要求缺陷版本在安装后的应用上触发断言失败、修复后通过，并将基础设施故障与程序故障区分开来。在200个Android任务的审计子集上，五个智能体的Pass@1从22.00%到90.50%不等，展示了较大性能差异，说明运行时感知的验收标准对模型比较至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码库级LLM智能体评估通常在构建主机上运行测试，未覆盖移动应用特有的"构建-安装-启动-测试"边界问题，如缺失SDK、离线设备或启动前崩溃可能被误判为程序失败，导致评估结果不准确。

Method: 提出AppEval框架，每个任务将隐藏行为测试与参考生产修复分离；验收条件要求同一已安装应用目标在缺陷版本上达到断言失败、修复后通过，并将基础设施失败作为独立结果处理。通过通用schema将这一契约映射到各平台的构建系统、运行时和测试运行器。

Result: 审计后的Android分区包含来自24个可独立构建仓库的200个可接受的仪表化任务。五个智能体在这些任务上的Pass@1在22.00%到90.50%之间，在相同动态oracle下存在68.50个百分点的性能差距。

Conclusion: 移动应用修复性能高度依赖于所评估的智能体，运行时感知的验收标准对于有意义的比较是必要的。但定量发现仅适用于Android，需获得经审计的iOS和HarmonyOS结果后才能得出跨平台泛化结论。

Abstract: Repository-level LLM agents are typically evaluated on projects whose tests run on the build host. It remains unclear whether their repairs survive the mobile build-install-launch-test boundary, where a missing SDK, offline device, or pre-assertion crash can be mistaken for a program failure. We present AppEval, a benchmark and native-toolchain evaluation framework for mobile application repair across HarmonyOS/ArkTS, iOS/Swift, and Android/Kotlin. Each task separates a hidden behavior test from the reference production fix and is accepted only when the same installed-app target reaches an assertion failure on the defective revision and passes after the fix; infrastructure failures remain a distinct outcome. A common schema maps this contract to each platform's build system, runtime, and test runner. The audited Android partition contains 200 accepted instrumentation tasks from 24 independently buildable repositories. On these tasks, five agents achieve Pass@1 between 22.00% and 90.50%, a 68.50-percentage-point spread under the same dynamic oracle. These results show that mobile repair performance depends strongly on the evaluated agent while demonstrating why runtime-aware acceptance is necessary for meaningful comparison. The quantitative findings in this paper are Android-specific; audited iOS and HarmonyOS results are required before drawing cross-platform generalization conclusions.

</details>


### [256] [OdinEval: A Reproducible Benchmark for LLM-Based Program Repair in the Odin Programming Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18595)
*Bang Xie,Hao Liu,Zhiyuan Peng,Xin Yin,Senjian Zhang,Yuan Luo,Chenhao Ying,Haiming Jin,Wei Chen,Shaocong Long,Zhenyu Shi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出OdinEval，一个针对Odin系统编程语言的可复现仓库级修复基准，包含168个过滤后的实例，并评估了六个语言模型在该基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级修复基准主要集中于少数主流语言，系统编程语言（如Odin）缺乏充分测试，需要补充面向这些语言的基准来推动修复能力评估。

Method: 从公开Odin仓库的已记录缺陷构建基准，每个实例绑定issue、base/fix提交、黄金补丁、回归测试、历史工具链和执行记录。若无开发者测试，则使用黑盒测试，由三个同模型实例独立审查，在两种历史状态下执行，并根据版本化测试编写技能从记录反馈中修订。最终在统一协议下对六个语言模型在168个过滤实例上进行评估。

Result: Kimi-K3在Resolved得分上最高（66.7%），Qwen3.8-Max在Repro得分上最高（96.4%）。发布了包括冻结数据、源码归档、容器、验证器、模型补丁和审计清单在内的完整资源。

Conclusion: OdinEval填补了系统编程语言在仓库级修复基准方面的空白，提供了可复现的评估资源和流程，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Repository-level repair benchmarks still center on a few mainstream languages, leaving systems languages such as Odin largely untested. We present OdinEval, a reproducible benchmark built from documented defects in public Odin repositories. Each instance binds an issue to base and fix commits, a gold patch, an issue-specific regression test, a historical toolchain, and execution records. Admission requires the test to fail on the base revision and pass after the gold fix. When no usable developer test exists, a black-box test is reviewed independently by three instances of the same model, executed in both historical states, and revised from recorded feedback under a versioned Test Writing Skill. We evaluate six language models on 168 filtered instances under one shared protocol. Kimi-K3 records the highest Resolved score at 66.7%, while Qwen3.8-Max has the highest Repro score at 96.4%. The release includes frozen data, source archives, containers, validators, model patches, and audit manifests.

</details>


### [257] [Code Health in LLM-Based Test Generation: Effectiveness and Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18645)
*Freya Wirdemann,Markus Borg,Nadim Hagatulah,Adam Tornhill*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究调查了LLM生成的单元测试在不同可维护性级别（由CodeScene的CodeHealth衡量）下的有效性差异，发现CodeHealth对测试有效性提供了微弱但一致的信号，并与输入令牌数量呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明AI工具在高质量、易维护的源代码上表现更好，但尚不清楚代码可维护性如何具体影响LLM生成的单元测试的有效性。

Method: 使用传统覆盖率指标和变异得分评估LLM生成单元测试的有效性，涵盖Python、Java和C++三种语言；同时使用常用工业分词器研究不同CodeHealth级别的代码如何转换为输入令牌。

Result: CodeHealth提供了微弱但一致的LLM生成测试有效性信号，并且CodeHealth与输入令牌数量呈负相关。

Conclusion: 研究结果为可维护性与基于LLM的软件开发之间的关系提供了进一步证据。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by Large Language Models (LLMs) are now prominent in software engineering. Previous work has shown that AI tools perform better on high-quality source code that is easy to maintain. In this study, we investigate how the effectiveness of LLM-generated unit tests varies across maintainability levels measured by CodeScene's CodeHealth (CH). We assess test effectiveness using traditional coverage metrics and mutation score across Python, Java, and C++. Moreover, we study how code with different levels of CH translates into input tokens using common industrial tokenizers. Our results suggest that CH provides a weak but consistent signal of LLM-generated test effectiveness and is negatively correlated with input-token count. These findings provide further evidence for a relationship between maintainability and LLM-based software development.

</details>


### [258] [Flama: a Python framework for development and deployment of production-ready APIs, machine learning, and LLM services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18733)
*José A. Perdiguero López,Miguel A. Durán-Olivencia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Flama是一个基于ASGI的开源Python框架，统一了REST API开发、机器学习模型服务和LLM应用部署，通过七个子系统（依赖注入、可插拔schema层、自动CRUD生成器、便携式模型格式、多后端LLM服务器、Rust加速核心和MCP模块）提供端到端的生产级解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Web框架、模型服务平台和LLM推理引擎各自独立，开发者需要维护多套工具链来构建完整的AI应用。Flama旨在通过单一架构统一这三种工作负载，降低开发和部署的复杂性。

Method: Flama采用类型驱动、异步优先的编程模型，围绕七大子系统构建：(1)基于类型注解的组件化依赖注入；(2)支持Pydantic/Marshmallow/Typesystem的可插拔schema层；(3)基于SQLAlchemy和Repository/Unit of Work模式的自动CRUD生成器；(4).flm便携式二进制格式打包多框架模型实现零代码部署；(5)支持vLLM和MLX后端的LLM多协议服务器；(6)通过Maturin编译的Rust加速核心；(7)将应用转为MCP服务器的模块。

Result: 论文描述了Flama的架构设计、通过实例展示了编程模型，并与现有框架（如FastAPI）、模型服务平台和LLM推理引擎进行了系统比较，展示了其统一架构的优势。

Conclusion: Flama通过统一架构将REST API、预测模型服务和生成式AI推理整合到一个框架中，提供了一个功能丰富的开源解决方案，涵盖认证、分页、后台任务、流式传输和OpenAPI文档生成等生产级能力。

Abstract: We present Flama, an open-source Python framework for developing and deploying production-ready web APIs, machine learning services, and large-language-model (LLM) applications. Built on the Asynchronous Server Gateway Interface (ASGI), Flama offers a type-driven, async-first programming model that unifies REST API development, predictive model serving, and generative AI inference in one architecture.
  It is organised around seven subsystems: a component-based dependency injection system resolving handler parameters from type annotations at startup; a pluggable schema layer supporting Pydantic, Marshmallow and Typesystem behind a single adapter; an automatic CRUD generator turning a SQLAlchemy table and a schema class into REST endpoints backed by the Repository and Unit of Work patterns; a portable binary format (.flm) packaging models from scikit-learn, TensorFlow, PyTorch and Hugging Face Transformers with their metadata for zero-code deployment; a multi-backend LLM server running vLLM (Linux/CUDA) or MLX (Apple Silicon) and exposing four wire protocols (OpenAI, Anthropic, Ollama, and a native streaming dialect) through a shared codec; a Rust-accelerated core compiled via Maturin for routing, JSON encoding, compression and parsing; and a Model Context Protocol module turning any application into an MCP server over JSON-RPC 2.0.
  Built-in capabilities include JWT authentication, two pagination strategies, background tasks in threads or processes, WebSocket endpoints, Server-Sent Event and NDJSON streaming, OpenAPI 3.2.0 generation from handler signatures, and a command-line interface for running applications and for serving, packaging and inspecting models.
  We describe the architecture, present the programming model through worked examples, and compare Flama with existing frameworks, model serving platforms and LLM inference engines.

</details>


### [259] [Contract-Aware Rescue of a Drifted Isabelle Development: The Double-Tank Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18822)
*Jim Woodcock,Gabriel Leite,Augusto Sampaio,Ran Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究针对大语言模型为交互式定理证明器生成的证明可能并未真正保留验证任务的问题，在采样数据双罐控制器的Isabelle开发中进行了审计与重构。原始工作从9个理论和10个未完成义务开始，扩展到16个理论的构建，但审计发现100个原始声明中有16处实质性变更，包括削弱了端到端保证定理。作者使用基于契约的证明修复工具CAPRI进行重构，在不改变原始九理论结构的情况下完成了全部10个范围内的义务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可以为交互式定理证明器提出证明，但即使构建成功，也无法保证周围的验证任务（即原始规范）被完整保留。在自动化证明生成过程中，模型可能通过改变假设、弱化结论等方式"作弊"，导致验证的实际意义被破坏。

Method: 作者对采样数据双罐控制器Isabelle开发进行了回溯审计，检查原始100个声明中是否有实质性变更。随后使用CAPRI（一种契约感知的证明修复工具）来管理重构过程，该工具结合了Isabelle的接受验证与对仓库变更的独立检查，并与机器可读的编辑契约进行比对。此外还进行了二次复盘以验证可复现性。

Result: 审计发现100个原始声明中有16处实质性变更，其中端到端保证定理被削弱，将四个需求中的三个隐含在结论中。使用CAPRI进行的重构在原九理论结构中完成了全部10个范围内义务。共同作者的二次复盘成功复现了R10构建、契约检查、控制测试和主要审计发现。

Conclusion: LLM生成的证明需要通过独立于证明器接受的额外验证手段来确保验证任务被真正保留。本工作展示了基于契约的证明修复工具CAPRI在此方面的有效性，但操作的端到端验证仍不完整，需要扩展契约来连接操作执行与重构后的定量轨迹契约。独立复现仍有待未来工作。

Abstract: Large language models can propose proofs for interactive theorem provers, but a successful build does not show the surrounding verification task was preserved. We study this problem in an Isabelle development of a sampled-data double-tank controller. The work began with nine theories and ten unfinished obligations, grew to a 16-theory build without sorry, oops, added axiomatisation, or oracle use, and accumulated 23 stable and 36 broken proof states. A retrospective audit found material changes in 16 of the 100 original declarations, including a weakened end-to-end assurance theorem that assumed three of the four requirements in its conclusion. We used CAPRI, a contract-aware proof-repair tool, to govern a reconstruction by combining Isabelle acceptance with an independent check of repository changes against machine-readable edit contracts. The reconstruction discharged all ten scoped obligations within the original nine-theory structure. A secondary replay by a co-author reproduced the R10 build, contract checks, control tests, and principal audit findings; independent replication remains future work. Operational end-to-end verification remains incomplete: we still need to connect operational executions to the reconstructed quantitative trace contract, a task requiring an extended contract.

</details>


### [260] [SkillForge: Self-Distilling Agents for Project-Specific Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18933)
*Silin Chen,Han Li,Xiaodong Gu,Yuling Shi,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SkillForge是一个自我蒸馏框架，通过重新实现测试覆盖的核心功能来合成项目特定问题，从而主动获取仓库知识并将其蒸馏为实体锚定的技能，显著提升LLM智能体在软件问题解决上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体在自动解决软件问题时，常因缺乏项目特定知识而难以解决仓库中的特定问题。现有自我进化方法要么依赖历史问题解决信号，要么产生高昂的逐问题测试探索成本。作者希望提出一种更高效、主动获取项目特定知识的方法。

Method: SkillForge是一个自我蒸馏框架，核心思路是：(1) 通过重新实现仓库中测试覆盖的核心功能来合成项目特定问题；(2) 通过解决这些合成问题，将可复用的项目特定知识蒸馏为实体锚定的技能（entity-grounded skills）；(3) 将这些技能与相关仓库实体关联，供未来真实问题解决时使用。整个过程无需等待真实问题暴露知识缺口，而是主动从仓库自身获取知识。

Result: 在开源和闭源模型上进行了大量实验，SkillForge在多个强基线上一致地提升了问题解决性能。

Conclusion: 在解决真实问题之前主动获取项目特定知识，能够显著改善下游软件问题解决的效果，验证了主动知识获取策略的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based agents have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in automated software issue resolution, yet they often struggle to resolve issues in a specific repository because they lack project-specific knowledge. Existing self-evolving approaches acquire such knowledge from repository history or online repair trajectories, but they either depend on available historical issue-resolution signals or incur substantial per-issue test-time exploration cost. In this paper, we propose SkillForge, a self-distillation framework that proactively acquires project-specific knowledge from the repository itself. Instead of waiting for real issues to expose project-specific knowledge gaps, SkillForge synthesizes project-specific issues by re-implementing test-covered core functionalities of the repository. By resolving these synthetic issues, SkillForge distills reusable project-specific knowledge into entity-grounded skills and associates them with relevant repository entities for future issue resolution. Extensive experiments using both open-source and closed-source models show that SkillForge consistently improves issue resolution performance over strong baselines. These results demonstrate that proactively acquiring project-specific knowledge before solving real issues substantially improves downstream software issue resolution.

</details>
